Juristic Platform · Documentation

UI Design, Mockups & User Flows

Owner: Senior Product DesignerDate: 2026-09-06Status: Draft v1.0Version: 1.0
Contents

About this document

This is the user-interface specification for Juristic Platform — the web application that manages apartment water, electricity and common-area fees and notifies tenants through a LINE Official Account. It fixes what every screen looks like, what every control does, what every state shows and what every visible string says, in enough detail that an engineer can build a screen from it without inventing anything and a tester can write an assertion from it without asking a question.

It is a specification, not a style deck. Every mockup below is live HTML rendered by the product's own design tokens, so the pictures cannot drift from the stylesheet that ships. Every screen is annotated control by control, and every screen states its empty, loading, error, success and permission-denied appearance. Nothing here is decorative.

What binds this document, and in what order

Three inputs are binding and are never re-argued here. The PRD fixes scope: the feature list, the arithmetic, the demo scenario of §28 and the absolute out-of-scope list of §24. The product decision log (D-01…D-47) fixes product behaviour: statuses, dialogs, permissions, message text, formats. The Architecture Decision Record (ADR-001) fixes names, routes, types and conventions. Where this document and one of those disagree, the source wins and this document is wrong. Where two screen chapters disagree with each other, Design Foundations wins, then the ADR.

Purpose

  • Fix the interface before it is built. Every page identifier the PRD names — Login, Dashboard, Rooms, Tenants, Meter Reading, Monthly Billing, Bill Detail, Payment, Receipt, Announcements, Reports, Settings — is drawn at its real width, populated with the seed data, and annotated.
  • Fix the Thai copy. Users are Thai, so every label, button, badge, validation message, empty state and LINE message is written here in Thai, verbatim, in a per-screen copy table. Engineering types those strings; it does not compose them.
  • Fix the states. A screen is not specified until its empty, loading, error, success and permission-denied appearances are specified. Each screen chapter carries that table.
  • Make the design testable. Every element that renders a bill status carries data-status="Draft|Unpaid|Paid|Overdue", so the test plan asserts on the English identifier while the human reads Thai (D-02).

Audience

ReaderRead forStart at
Front-end engineerMarkup structure, component classes, breakpoints, the exact Thai strings, which control is disabled whenDesign Foundations §7–§10, then the screen chapter you are building
Back-end engineerWhich route renders which screen, which payload each screen needs, which error code produces which inline message, which action needs a dialogThe .screen-meta line and states table of each screen
TesterExpected values, status hooks, per-state expectations, the exact Thai strings to assert onEvery States and exact Thai copy table, plus Appendix D
Project advisor / examinerThat the design covers the PRD, respects the out-of-scope list, and demonstrates the §28 scenario end to endDesign Foundations §3–§5, then LINE Official Account Messages
Product ownerThat every decision D-01…D-47 landed on a screen, and what was deliberately not builtAppendix C and each chapter's assumptions block

How to read this document

Device frames

A mockup sits inside a frame that declares its surface. .device.desktop is the office desktop at 1200 px; .device.phone is the staff or tenant phone at 390 px; .device.print is an A5 sheet. The frame's address bar carries the route the screen is served from.

Numbered annotations

A blue numbered marker sits on the control it describes; the numbered list under the frame explains, for each marker, what the element is, how it behaves, what it validates and what state results. Numbering restarts per screen.

States tables

Every screen carries a table with at least empty · loading · error · success · permission denied. Several screens add sub-states — a 409 race, a partial delivery, a blocked action — where the screen genuinely has one.

Exact Thai copy tables

Strings are given verbatim, in Thai, with the moment they appear. Where a decision fixes a string byte for byte — the LINE templates of D-31, the six dialogs of D-39, the 403 page of D-40 — the table reproduces it rather than paraphrasing it.

Decision citations

A parenthesised D-nn is the decision-log entry that forced the design. It is a citation, not a footnote: if you disagree with the screen, the argument is with the decision, and the decision is where it must be reopened.

Notation

Money is 1,790.00 in tables and on the receipt, 1,790 บาท in sentences and LINE messages, and ฿1,790 only in a tile where space is tight (D-03). Dates are Gregorian: 3 ก.ย. 2026 in lists, 3 กันยายน 2026 in LINE and on the receipt, 2026-09-03 in payloads. Times are 14:30 น., Asia/Bangkok. Periods are 2026-09, displayed กันยายน 2026.

English identifiers, Thai interface. Page names, routes, statuses, class names, column names and test hooks stay English throughout this document because that is what the code, the API payloads and the test selectors use. Everything a user can see is Thai. The two never mix in one string: the Monthly Billing page is called บิลประจำเดือน on screen and /bills in every sentence that names a route.

Which numbers in this document are expected values, and which are scenery

Only three groups of figures may ever be asserted on. (1) The PRD §28 demo arithmetic for room 205 — 30 ตร.ม., water 1,250 → 1,260 = 10 units × 18 = 180, electricity 5,280 → 5,350 = 70 units × 8 = 560, common area 30 × 35 = 1,050, total 1,790, due 5 ก.ย. 2026, paid 3 ก.ย. 2026, receipt REC-202609-000852. (2) The seed room counts 80 / 67 / 13 and the common-area total 70,350.00 (67 × 30 × 35). (3) The 54 LINE-linked tenants of D-44. Everything else — every other room, tenant, phone number, chart series, arrears row and month total — is illustrative scenery invented to make a screen legible. PRD §16's water total 18,500 and electricity total 62,500 are not reproducible at the stated rates and are never used as expected values (D-44).

One illustrative period, used by every chapter. กันยายน 2026 is drawn as a single imagined month throughout: 121,566.00 billed (12,240.00 น้ำ + 38,976.00 ไฟ + 70,350.00 ส่วนกลาง over 67 approved bills), 98,420.00 collected, 23,146.00 outstanding across 12 เกินกำหนด bills. Design Foundations §8.1, Monthly Billing and Reports render slices of that month at different hours of 7–8 ก.ย. 2026, after the demo approval. The Dashboard frame draws the moment before it — the seeded 66-bill state, 119,776.00 billed and ส่วนกลาง 69,300.00 — because that is what a freshly seeded clone shows and what the test plan asserts the demo approval moves (69,300.00 → 70,350.00). It reconciles (billed − collected = outstanding) and every figure divides cleanly by the seeded rates, but it is still scenery under D-44: only 80 / 67 / 13, 70,350.00 and the PRD §28 room-205 arithmetic are reproducible, and no test may assert on the rest. Room identities are fixed once, in Design Foundations §8.9; see also Appendix E.

Out of scope, and therefore absent from every screen

PRD §24 is absolute and D-05 restates it. No screen, control, empty state, error page or message in this document offers rent, a lease contract, a mobile application, a payment gateway, a card, online banking, a slip upload, a QR payment, an IoT or automatic meter, or anything driven by AI or machine learning. Where a reader might expect one of those — a payment method selector, a "scan to pay" button, an automatic reading import — its absence is deliberate and is stated where the reader would look for it.

Design Foundations & User Flows

This section is the design contract for every screen mockup in this document set. It fixes the principles, the people, the map, the flows and the component vocabulary; the screen chapters that follow spend those decisions instead of re-taking them. Everything visible to a user is Thai (D-02); every identifier in this document — page names, statuses, class names, routes — stays English, because that is what the code, the API payloads and the test selectors use.

Two stylesheets, one boundary

The documentation is styled by docs/_shared/template.html. The product UI is styled by docs/_shared/tokens.css, in which every rule is scoped under .screen and every custom property is prefixed --ui-. A mockup is therefore always written as <div class="device desktop"><div class="screen">…</div></div> (or device phone / device print). The two systems can never collide, and the same file is copied verbatim to public/css/app.css in milestone M0 — the .screen scope is kept, not stripped, because views/layout.ejs wraps the page body in <div class="screen">. See §7.

1. Design principles

Five principles, each traceable to a decision and each falsifiable — if a screen breaks one, the screen is wrong, not the principle.

1 · Mobile-first for staff, desktop-first for the office

Three surfaces are phone-sized and must work at 360 × 640 on Chrome for Android: Login, Meter Reading and the reduced Dashboard. Everything else is an office surface designed at 1280 px and not required to work below 768 px (D-45). One stylesheet serves both; the layout switches on container width, never on a user-agent sniff.

2 · One-hand meter entry

A technician stands at a meter box holding a phone in one hand. Each room is a self-contained card with its own บันทึก button and one request (D-15); nothing is a 67-room form. Inputs are inputmode="numeric", targets are ≥ 44 px, previous readings are read-only text, and a dead spot loses at most the room being typed.

3 · Error prevention on money actions

Four actions move money or send irreversible messages: อนุมัติและส่งบิล, ยืนยันรับเงินสด, ส่งประกาศ, ยกเลิกการชำระเงิน. Each states its exact effect — amount, room, billing period, recipient count — in a dialog before it runs (D-39), and each is guarded server-side as well, because a disabled button is not a guard.

4 · Thai-first copy, English identifiers

Every label, button, badge, error and LINE message is Thai and is written once in the templates — there is no language switcher and no i18n framework (D-02). English survives as data-status="Unpaid", route names and column names. Money is 1,790.00 in tables and 1,790 บาท in sentences and LINE; dates are Gregorian (D-03).

5 · Glanceable status

The office question is always "which rooms still owe money?". Status is a badge carrying a Thai word, a colour and a data-status hook — never colour alone (§9). Overdue is derived at render time from Unpaid plus a past due date and is never stored (D-21), so no screen may offer an action that "marks a bill overdue".

2. Personas

คุณนิด — Admin / นิติบุคคล

Device
Office desktop, Chrome, 1280–1920 px, mouse and keyboard
Frequency
Daily at the counter; a heavy hour once a month at approval
Owns
Rates, bills, approval, cash, receipts, tenants, announcements, users, settings

Goals — every occupied room billed with nothing missed; the cash drawer reconciles against the system at closing; know today which rooms are in arrears and for how long; answer "why is my bill 1,790?" with the meter numbers on screen.

Frustrations — an Excel file that two people edited; re-typing the same LINE message 67 times; a tenant disputing a reading with no history to show; discovering at month end that three rooms were never read.

ช่างสมชาย — Staff / เจ้าหน้าที่ช่าง

Device
Personal Android phone, 360 × 640, one hand, patchy signal in stairwells
Frequency
One reading round per month, 67 rooms, target ≤ 15 minutes (D-45)
Owns
Meter readings and meter replacements only — never an amount, a rate or a payment (D-36)

Goals — walk each floor once, in room order; never re-walk a floor because a save was lost; know at a glance which rooms are still outstanding; hand the round to the office with one button.

Frustrations — a form that loses everything when the lift kills the connection; tiny targets while wearing gloves; not remembering whether room 312 was already entered; a keyboard that is not numeric.

คุณแอน — Tenant / ผู้เช่า

Device
LINE on her phone. No login, no app, no web account — ever (D-05)
Frequency
Three or four messages a month
Owns
Nothing in the system; she is a recipient and a person at the counter

Goals — know the amount and the due date without asking; understand how the amount was reached; get proof that her cash was recorded.

Frustrations — a paper slip pushed under the door and lost; a total with no breakdown; no confirmation after paying; being chased for a bill she already settled.

The tenant is not a user of the web application

Every tenant-facing surface in this design set is a LINE message or a printed A5 sheet. No screen, route, empty state or error page is ever designed for a tenant, and no mockup may show a tenant login (PRD §4, D-05).

Sixteen page routes plus three error pages. Staff reach only Dashboard, Rooms, Room detail, Meter Reading and Change password; every other node returns the Thai 403 page (D-36, D-40).

Change password/passwordเปลี่ยนรหัสผ่านadmin · staffบันทึกรหัสผ่านใหม่

/password is the only route both roles reach that is not in either navigation's item list. It lives in the account block at the foot of the Admin sidebar and at the foot of the staff ภาพรวม, beside ออกจากระบบ — never as a fourth staff tab, because D-36's staff surface is three tabs and a fourth would change the role matrix to reach a screen a person opens twice a year. It is the realisation of D-37's "first login forces a change" together with the Dashboard banner, and it is a change, never a self-service reset: a forgotten password is still recovered only by an Admin on ผู้ใช้งานระบบ (D-46).

4. Navigation model per role

One shell, two navigations, chosen by role at render time — not two applications. Staff never see a link they cannot open, and the 403 page exists for the case where a staff user types an Admin URL directly (D-40).

RoleSurfaceNavigationItems, in orderAccount blockLanding page
AdminDesktop ≥ 768 px.sidebar, always visible ภาพรวม · จดมิเตอร์ · บิลประจำเดือน · รับชำระเงิน · ห้องพัก · ผู้เช่า · ประกาศ · รายงาน — then a .nav-section "ตั้งค่าระบบ": ตั้งค่า · ผู้ใช้งานระบบ .user, pinned to the foot of the sidebar: นิติบุคคล · admin · เปลี่ยนรหัสผ่าน · ออกจากระบบ — display name, username, then /password and POST /logout ภาพรวม
AdminPhone (occasional).topbar only Title plus actions; the sidebar collapses out below 768 px. Not a supported working surface (D-45), but nothing breaks. Same block, moved into the collapsed menu ภาพรวม
StaffPhone 360 px.bottom-tabs, three tabs จดมิเตอร์ (default) · ห้องพัก · ภาพรวม A one-line .user footer at the foot of ภาพรวม only: เจ้าหน้าที่ · staff · เปลี่ยนรหัสผ่าน · ออกจากระบบ. It is not a tab จดมิเตอร์

The tab order is deliberate: the reading round is the staff job, so it is the landing page and the first tab. The staff Dashboard shows room counts and this period's reading progress only — no money reaches a staff response body at all (D-36).

Both navigations are one block of markup each, rendered identically on every page of that role; only .nav-item.active (or .tab.active) moves. There is no per-page item list, no per-page ordering and no per-page icon set — the Admin sidebar always carries all ten items with the ตั้งค่าระบบ divider in the position above, and the staff tab bar always carries 📝 จดมิเตอร์ · 🚪 ห้องพัก · 📊 ภาพรวม in that order. The account block is part of that same block of markup, which is what makes the test-plan assertion hold: the complete href set of any staff page is a subset of {/readings, /rooms, /rooms/:id, /dashboard, /password, /logout} and every member of it answers 200 (TC-AUTH-027). Every mockup in this document renders that same block, so a frame whose order differs is a defect in the frame, not a variant. The one dynamic value in it is the .count pill on บิลประจำเดือน: COUNT(bills WHERE period = selected AND status IN ('Unpaid','Overdue')) — outstanding bills for the selected period, never Drafts, never a notification count, hidden at zero.

5. User flows

5.3 Cash payment and receipt

flowchart TD
  A["ผู้เช่ามาที่เคาน์เตอร์"] --> B["รับชำระเงิน · ค้นหาห้อง 205"]
  B --> C["เลือกบิลที่ต้องชำระ · แสดงรอบบิลเสมอ"]
  C --> D["ยอดที่ต้องชำระ 1,790.00 บาท · อ่านอย่างเดียว"]
  D --> E["ตัวช่วยหน้าจอ: รับเงิน 2,000 ไปเป็น เงินทอน 210.00 · ไม่บันทึก"]
  E --> F["กด ยืนยันรับเงินสด"]
  F --> G["กล่องยืนยัน: ยืนยันรับเงินสด 1,790 บาท ห้อง 205 รอบ กันยายน 2026"]
  G --> H["หนึ่งทรานแซกชัน: บันทึกการรับเงิน · Unpaid ไปเป็น Paid · ออกเลขใบเสร็จ"]
  H --> I["ใบเสร็จ REC-202609-000852 · หน้าจอ A5"]
  I --> J["พิมพ์ใบเสร็จ · นับจำนวนพิมพ์ก่อนสั่งพิมพ์"]
  J --> K["พิมพ์ครั้งถัดไปมีคำว่า สำเนา"]
  H --> L["เข้าคิวข้อความ LINE ยืนยันการชำระเงิน"]
  H --> M{"รับเงินผิดห้องหรือผิดรอบ?"}
  M -->|"ใช่"| N["คืนเงินสดที่เคาน์เตอร์ก่อน · ยกเลิกการชำระเงิน พร้อมเหตุผล"]
  N --> O["ใบเสร็จถูกประทับ ยกเลิก · บิลกลับเป็น Unpaid · ระงับการแจ้งเตือนอัตโนมัติ"]
D-24, D-25, D-26 and D-27. The amount is never an input, partial payment does not exist, and the void path returns cash across the counter before anything is clicked.

D-29. Staff never type a LINE User ID: the field is read-only and is filled only by this flow.

6. Interaction rules

6.1 Validation timing

MomentWhat runsWhat the user sees
While typingNothing, with two exceptions: the meter comparison (current vs previous) and the เงินทอน calculatorLive error under the meter field and a disabled บันทึก; a live change figure. No other field nags mid-typing.
On blurFormat and required checks for the field just left (phone 0XXXXXXXXX, area, dates)Inline Thai message under the field, .field.invalid, and the value kept exactly as typed.
On submitzod at the boundary, then the service rules, then the database constraints422 renders the same Thai strings inline, keyed by input name — the API message and the form message are one string (ADR §6.8).
After submitState guards that only the database can settle (already paid, later bill exists, duplicate reading)409 or 422 as a .notice.bad above the form, never a redirect and never a lost form.
The one blocking rule at the meter

When เปลี่ยนมิเตอร์ในรอบนี้ is off and the current reading is below the previous one, บันทึก is disabled and the field shows เลขมิเตอร์ปัจจุบันน้อยกว่าเดือนก่อน (1,250) กรุณาตรวจสอบ. Ticking the checkbox reveals เลขสุดท้ายของมิเตอร์เดิม, เลขเริ่มต้นของมิเตอร์ใหม่ and a required reason, and replaces the rule instead of bypassing it (D-13). The two soft flags — ใช้สูงผิดปกติ and ไม่มีการใช้งาน — never block a save.

6.2 Empty states

One .empty-state component: a muted icon, one Thai sentence, and a primary action only where there is something to do. The eight surfaces are fixed by D-40 and must be used verbatim.

SurfaceSentenceAction
ห้องพักยังไม่มีข้อมูลห้องเพิ่มห้อง
ผู้เช่ายังไม่มีผู้เช่า—
จดมิเตอร์ยังไม่มีห้องที่ต้องจดในรอบนี้—
บิลประจำเดือนยังไม่มีบิลในรอบนี้สร้างบิลฉบับร่าง
รับชำระเงินค้นหาห้องเพื่อรับชำระเงิน—
ประกาศยังไม่เคยส่งประกาศเขียนประกาศ
รายงานยังไม่มีข้อมูลในช่วงเวลานี้—
รายการค้างชำระไม่มีห้องค้างชำระ 🎉—

6.3 Errors and loading

  • Failed save — a .notice.bad above the form with ลองอีกครั้ง; every typed value is preserved. On the phone reading screen the notice lives inside the room card and never reloads the page (D-40).
  • Unauthorized — a full Thai 403 page: ไม่มีสิทธิ์เข้าถึงหน้านี้ · บัญชีของคุณเป็นเจ้าหน้าที่ กรุณาติดต่อนิติบุคคล with a button back to จดมิเตอร์. Never a silent redirect, so the permission matrix is testable row by row.
  • Lists — three to five .skeleton.row placeholders, never a centred spinner over an empty page.
  • Actions — the pressed button takes .is-loading and disabled; nothing else on the page is blocked.
  • Long jobs — the approve result screen shows a .progress bar driven by the drain loop with a running count, because a 67-room send takes up to three minutes (D-19, D-45).
  • Success — a .toast.ok for reversible, low-stakes saves; a full result screen for anything that sent a LINE message or moved money.

6.4 Confirmation dialogs

One shared .modal + .dialog component, six call sites, never the browser's native confirm(). Every dialog names the exact effect with counts and amounts, and the primary button repeats the verb instead of saying ตกลง (D-39).

#ActionDialog textPrimary buttonReason required
1Approve & Send (single)อนุมัติและส่งบิลห้อง 205 ผ่าน LINE — ยกเลิกไม่ได้อนุมัติและส่งno
2Approve & Send (bulk)อนุมัติและส่งบิล 64 ห้อง ผ่าน LINE — ยกเลิกไม่ได้อนุมัติและส่งno
3Confirm Cash Paymentยืนยันรับเงินสด 1,790 บาท · ห้อง 205 · รอบ กันยายน 2026 และออกใบเสร็จยืนยันรับเงินสดno
4Send announcementส่งประกาศถึง 67 ห้อง ผ่าน LINE — เรียกคืนไม่ได้ส่งประกาศno
5Void paymentคืนเงินสด 1,790 บาท ให้ผู้เช่าก่อนยืนยันการยกเลิกยกเลิกการชำระเงินyes, ≥ 10 chars
6End tenancy / deactivate roomสิ้นสุดสัญญาเช่าห้อง 205 · ประวัติบิลและใบเสร็จจะยังคงอยู่สิ้นสุดสัญญาเช่าyes, ≥ 10 chars

6.5 Undo — where it exists and where it deliberately does not

No toast-with-undo anywhere in this product

An undo window would force every outbound LINE message into a delay queue, and a message already delivered cannot be recalled by any API LINE offers (D-39, D-35). Instead, the two reversible money paths are explicit, audited, Admin-only actions with a required Thai reason: ตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่าง on an Unpaid or Overdue bill — available only on a room's most recent bill (D-22) — and ยกเลิกการชำระเงิน, which returns cash first, keeps the receipt number and stamps the receipt ยกเลิก (D-25). Everything else is safe because it is reversible by ordinary editing (a Draft bill recalculates on every reading save) or because it never happened yet (a dialog stands between the click and the effect).

7. Visual language

Rendered live from tokens.css. Every swatch below is the actual custom property, so this block is a regression test as much as a specification: if a token changes, this page changes.

Where the design system lives, and how it ships

The design system is delivered as docs/_shared/tokens.css. It is not a sketch and not a scratch file: it is the artefact engineering consumes, and in milestone M0 it is copied verbatim to public/css/app.css — no build step, no preprocessor, no hand-editing on the way across. The engineering specification says the same.

In this document every rule is scoped under .screen, which is what makes it safe to inline a whole product stylesheet inside a documentation page: a mockup lives in <div class="device …"><div class="screen">…</div></div> and cannot reach the prose, the tables or the table of contents around it, and the document's own styles cannot reach into a mockup. In the app that scope is not stripped — views/layout.ejs wraps the page body in <div class="screen">, so the same file works unchanged in both places and no selector is ever rewritten by hand. One file, three homes, byte-identical in all three: docs/_shared/tokens.css, the second <style> block of this document, and public/css/app.css.

8.6 LINE Official Account

v1 sends plain text only — no Flex Messages, no images, no rich menu (D-31). The .flex-card and .rich-menu classes exist in tokens.css for a post-v1 version and must not appear in a v1 screen mockup. The two messages below are the BILL and PAID templates rendered with the PRD §28 values.

🏢 ศุภาลัย เรสซิเดนซ์

8.9 Illustrative seed identities — one room, one tenant, one occupancy

Every room code that appears in more than one chapter is listed here with the tenant and occupancy it carries everywhere. A frame that shows a different name, or shows a vacant room being billed, is a defect in the frame. Two rules follow from the decision log and are what this table exists to keep true: a ว่าง room is never billed, never counted as unread and never appears in the D-23 banner (D-07, D-23), and a room whose bill is still ฉบับร่าง in the selected period can never appear in an arrears list, because arrears are computed from approved bills only (D-41). Every room is 30.00 ตร.ม. (D-44). All names are fictional.

9. Accessibility

  • Contrast — the checked set is every badge against its own -soft background, white on --ui-brand (7.8:1), the sidebar text including the .nav-section group heading (5.3:1 on the navy), and the input placeholder, which uses the same muted token as hint text rather than a border colour. The five palette values that failed the check were darkened rather than accepted: water, electricity, danger, overdue, and --ui-muted itself, which moved to #5f6672 so it clears 4.5:1 on the page background (--ui-bg) and on --ui-surface-2, not only on white. Muted text is the lightest text permitted and is never used below 12 px — the chart SVG axis, month and value labels sit at 12 px for that reason.
  • Status is never colour alone — a status is always a Thai word inside a badge, plus data-status for tests. A colour-blind cashier reads ชำระแล้ว, not "the green one"; a printed page in greyscale still says it.
  • Targets — 44 × 44 px minimum on every phone surface (--ui-tap). The base tokens are tuned for the Admin desktop, so the phone floor is enforced by a container-scoped block at the end of tokens.css that raises .chip, .month-picker and its ‹ › arrows, .btn, .btn.sm and .page-link to --ui-tap under @container app (max-width:767px). The controls that already met it unscoped — the meter inputs, .btn.lg บันทึก, .check, .search, .list-item and the bottom tabs — are unchanged. Desktop rows may use the 38 px default, but never for a destructive action.
  • Labels — every input has a real <label>; a placeholder is never the label, because it disappears exactly when a Thai speaker re-checks what they typed. Where the label would be redundant on screen it is still in the DOM, wrapped in the .ui-sr-only utility declared in tokens.css §1 — that utility is also what the skip link to .main uses. Every hint and error span carries an id and its input carries the matching aria-describedby, and .field.invalid also sets aria-invalid="true" on the input, not on the wrapper. A control that is not actionable carries the real disabled attribute (or aria-disabled="true" where the reason must stay reachable); the reason is rendered as visible .hint text, never as a title tooltip, which no keyboard or touch user can reach.
  • Focus — a visible 2 px --ui-focus ring on every interactive element, never outline:none. Focus order follows the DOM: topbar → filters → list → primary action. Opening a dialog moves focus to its title and traps it; Esc always cancels and never confirms; closing returns focus to the button that opened it.
  • Numbers — inputmode="numeric" on every meter and money field, and font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums on every money column so columns of baht line up.
  • Language and structure — <html lang="th">, one <h1> per page, tables with real <th scope>, and a skip link to .main as the first focusable element.
  • Motion — the only animation is a loading spinner; there is nothing to disable, and no auto-advancing carousel, toast queue or scroll-jacking exists.

10. Responsive breakpoints

RangeLayoutApplies to
< 768 px.sidebar hidden · sticky .topbar · .bottom-tabs visible · one-column page · KPI tiles two-upStaff phone (360 × 640 minimum), and Login on any device
768–1279 px.sidebar visible at 216 px · tables scroll inside .table-scrollTablet or a small laptop window; usable but not the design target
≥ 1280 pxFull Admin layout · page content capped at --ui-content-max 1200 px · KPI grid auto-fits at 170 px minimumThe office desktop — the surface every Admin screen is designed at (D-45)
Container queries, not viewport queries

The switch is written as @container app (max-width:767px) against .app, not as a media query. In the running app the result is identical, because .app fills the viewport. In this document set it is what makes a phone mockup inside a .device.phone frame render the phone layout while a desktop mockup on the same page renders the sidebar — one stylesheet, no duplicated mobile variants, and no chance of a mockup drifting from the product.

11. Assumptions recorded by design

  1. Page routes. The ADR fixes the naming rule (lowercase, plural, hyphenated) and quotes /bills, /bills/:id, /readings and /announcements. The remaining routes in §3 — /login, /dashboard, /rooms/:id, /tenants, /payments, /receipts/:id, /bills/:id/invoice, /reports, /settings, /users — are derived from that rule and from the routes/ and views/pages/ file lists. If engineering names one differently, this table follows the code.
  2. Brand colour. The PRD names no brand colour. Navy #1b4fa0 was chosen because it is unmistakable against all three utility accents (cyan, amber, violet) and against the paid/overdue pair, and because white text on it clears 7:1. No logo, gradient or illustration is introduced — nothing in the document set implies an asset the team has not made.
  3. Single light theme. The doc template paints every mockup screen white and the product targets one office desktop and one staff phone, so no dark palette is defined. Adding one later is a token block, not a redesign.
  4. Icons are emoji. 💧 ⚡ 🏢 📝 🧾 and the nav glyphs are Unicode emoji rendered by the platform font — zero assets, zero licensing, and they already appear in the LINE templates fixed by D-31. Any icon carrying meaning is paired with a Thai word.
  5. Illustrative Dashboard figures, in two states. Water and electricity totals in §8.1 are invented for the mockup, and §8.1 draws the post-approval moment while the Dashboard chapter draws the seeded one. Per D-44, only the room counts (80 / 67 / 13) and the common area total (70,350.00) from PRD §16 are reproducible; PRD §16's 18,500 and 62,500 are not and are never used as expected values.
  6. Demo names. ศุภาลัย เรสซิเดนซ์, the address, the tax ID and every tenant name are fictional seed values (D-44). No real tenant data appears in any mockup.
  7. Reserved LINE components. .flex-card and .rich-menu are specified because the brief asks for them, and are marked reserved in both the stylesheet and §8.6 because D-31 fixes v1 to plain text. A v1 screen using them is a defect.

Login & Dashboard

These are the two screens every session passes through: the gate that establishes who is working (and therefore which of the two roles of D-36 the rest of the application will obey) and the first screen behind it, which answers what needs doing this month. Everything visible below is Thai (D-02); the English names Login and Dashboard survive only as route and identifier, never as UI copy.

Design decisions taken in this document
  • The login identifier is ชื่อผู้ใช้ only, not "Username / Email". PRD §19 lists "Username / Email", but D-46 fixes the Users page field list with no email address and the ADR's users table has username and no email column. One identifier field satisfies the PRD; adding an email field would create a column nothing writes.
  • No "จดจำฉัน" checkbox and no "ลืมรหัสผ่าน" link that does anything. D-37 gives a rolling 8-hour session and states that Admin resetting a password from the Users page is the only recovery path in v1, so the footer is a sentence, not a link.
  • The Dashboard mockup shows the period กันยายน 2026 exactly as a freshly seeded database produces it — 66 approved bills, room 205's still ฉบับร่าง so the demo can approve it live (D-44). D-41 excludes Draft bills from every money tile, so ค่าส่วนกลาง reads 69,300.00 (66 × 30 × 35) and ยอดเรียกเก็บทั้งหมด reads 119,776.00. The annotated second state is the one figure D-44 makes binding: the moment the demo approves room 205, the tile moves 69,300.00 → 70,350.00 (67 × 30 × 35), ยอดเรียกเก็บ 119,776.00 → 121,566.00 and the count 66 → 67 ใบ — the transition the test plan asserts as TC-BILL-065, and the reason both numbers appear in this chapter. The post-approval figures are the ones Design Foundations §8.1 and Reports tab 1 draw, because those frames are taken after the demo has run. It is the same period the Monthly Billing chapter draws one day earlier, on 7 ก.ย. 2026, with three rooms still unread and three bills still ฉบับร่าง; between the two frames two of those rooms are read and their bills approved, and eleven payments are taken (43 → 54 ชำระแล้ว). The frames are one story, not several Septembers.
  • "Today" in the mockups is 8 ก.ย. 2026, three days past the 5 ก.ย. 2026 due date of PRD §28. Every bill of this period was approved on or before 5 ก.ย. and carries the frozen due date 5 ก.ย. (D-20), so the twelve bills still unpaid on 8 ก.ย. are all เกินกำหนด and none is รอชำระ — the donut shows 0 in that slice and keeps it in the legend rather than dropping the row, so a reader can see the count is genuinely zero.
  • Money in a KPI tile is written ฿119,776, in a table 2,016.00. D-03 permits the ฿ glyph in mockups where space is tight; a 22 px value inside a 179 px tile is that case. Tables and Bill Detail keep two decimals. The running application may render the tile as 119,776 with บาท in the label — the figure is what is binding, not the glyph.
  • No D-39 confirmation dialog is reachable from either screen. All six call sites live on Monthly Billing, Bill Detail, Payment, Announcements, Tenants and Rooms. Logout is a plain POST /logout with no dialog, because D-39 fixes the list at six and logging out destroys nothing.
Where the Dashboard figures come from

D-44 is explicit that PRD §16's ยอดค่าน้ำ 18,500 and ยอดค่าไฟ 62,500 are arithmetically impossible at the fixed rates (18,500 is not divisible by 18; 62,500 is not divisible by 8) and may not be used as expected values anywhere. Only 80 / 67 / 13 and 70,350.00 are reproduced from the PRD. The water and electricity totals on this page are therefore constructed so that every figure divides cleanly and the three money tiles reconcile:

Seeded state, 66 approved bills — the frame above:
น้ำ 670 หน่วย × 18.00 = 12,060.00  ·  ไฟ 4,802 หน่วย × 8.00 = 38,416.00  ·  ส่วนกลาง 66 × 30 × 35.00 = 69,300.00
เรียกเก็บ 119,776.00 − รับชำระ 96,630.00 = ค้างชำระ 23,146.00 (12 ใบ เกินกำหนด)

After the demo approves and room 205 pays — 67 approved bills:
น้ำ 680 หน่วย × 18.00 = 12,240.00  ·  ไฟ 4,872 หน่วย × 8.00 = 38,976.00  ·  ส่วนกลาง 67 × 30 × 35.00 = 70,350.00
เรียกเก็บ 121,566.00 − รับชำระ 98,420.00 = ค้างชำระ 23,146.00 (12 ใบ เกินกำหนด)

These are the two moments of the single house snapshot for กันยายน 2026, one demo approval apart. The post-approval set is what Design Foundations §8.1, Reports tab 1 and the Monthly Billing chapter's own earlier-in-the-month frame draw; the seeded set is what the Dashboard frame above draws and what a fresh clone actually shows. Only the difference between them — 1,790.00 on one room — separates the two, and only ส่วนกลาง 70,350.00 is binding under D-44. The same twelve-month series feed the three charts — the water and electricity series are the units, and the revenue series is 18 × น้ำ + 8 × ไฟ + 70,350 for each fully-collected past period, so the third chart is derivable from the first two and cannot drift from them. Any test that asserts a Dashboard number must assert against these, never against PRD §16.

Login — GET /login · POST /login

Juristic Platform

5

ลืมรหัสผ่าน? ติดต่อนิติบุคคลเพื่อตั้งรหัสผ่านใหม่

ศุภาลัย เรสซิเดนซ์ · นิติบุคคลอาคารชุด

  1. Brand block. H1 Juristic Platform plus the Thai subtitle, verbatim from D-01 — the login page is the one surface that shows the product name, because the apartment name is not yet known (Settings is behind the session). No logo image: the design system ships no asset and one is not needed. Static; not interactive.
  2. ชื่อผู้ใช้. A single identifier field, name="username", 44 px tall with 16 px text so iOS and Android do not zoom on focus. autocapitalize="none" and spellcheck="false" because a username typed on a phone keyboard is otherwise capitalised. Validation: required. Empty on submit renders .field.invalid and the message กรุณากรอกชื่อผู้ใช้ under the input; the typed value is preserved on every failure (D-40). No live validation while typing — the first error appears on submit.
  3. รหัสผ่าน. type="password", autocomplete="current-password". Required; empty on submit gives กรุณากรอกรหัสผ่าน. The value is never echoed back into the field after a failed attempt, on any error path. No show/hide toggle in v1 and no minimum-length hint on this screen — password rules belong to the Users page (D-46), not the gate.
  4. เข้าสู่ระบบ. The single primary action, full width and 44 px (.btn.primary.lg.block), also the submit target of Enter from either field. On submit it becomes .btn.is-loading and disabled, so a double submit is impossible before the response lands. Success is a 302: admin to /dashboard, staff to /readings — staff never lands on a page they are 403'd from. The form posts a CSRF token issued on the GET (D-37).
  5. Recovery footer. A sentence, not a link. D-37 and D-46 make Admin's ตั้งรหัสผ่านใหม่ on the Users page the only recovery path in v1, so a self-service link would lead nowhere. The second line is the apartment name from Settings, rendered server-side; it is the one hint that the reader is at the right building's URL.

Alternate state — phone 390 px, sign-in rejected

Juristic Platform

ระบบจัดการค่าน้ำ ค่าไฟ และค่าส่วนกลาง

6 ชื่อผู้ใช้หรือรหัสผ่านไม่ถูกต้อง กรุณาตรวจสอบแล้วลองอีกครั้ง
7
8

ลืมรหัสผ่าน? ติดต่อนิติบุคคลเพื่อตั้งรหัสผ่านใหม่

  1. One banner, above the form. .notice.bad re-rendered by the same POST handler (D-40: never a redirect, never a lost form). The wording never says which half was wrong — an attacker must not learn that a username exists — so there is no per-field message here, and the banner is the only place the failure is stated.
  2. Both fields carry .field.invalid, neither carries a message. Marking only the password would leak exactly what the banner refuses to say. The username keeps its typed value; the password is cleared and takes focus. After the tenth failed attempt from one IP inside 15 minutes the banner is replaced by the rate-limit text and the submit button takes the real disabled attribute (D-37).
  3. The phone layout is the same card. .login-card is max-width:360px, so a 390 px device shows the desktop composition unchanged with 24 px of padding either side — there is no separate mobile login template. Inputs stay 44 px with 16 px text, which is what keeps Chrome for Android from zooming (D-45).

States — Login

StateTriggerWhat the screen doesThai copy
Empty First GET /login Both fields blank, ชื่อผู้ใช้ focused, button enabled. No banner. This is the default screenshot above. —
Loading Submit pressed Button becomes .btn.is-loading (spinner, label hidden) and disabled; both inputs go readonly. No skeleton — there is no list to load. Button label while loading: กำลังเข้าสู่ระบบ… (screen-reader text only; the spinner hides it visually)
Error — validation Empty field on submit 422 from the zod schema; the field gets .field.invalid and an inline .error. No banner. Typed values preserved. กรุณากรอกชื่อผู้ใช้ · กรุณากรอกรหัสผ่าน
Error — credentials Unknown user, or wrong password 401, page re-rendered with the banner; both fields .field.invalid, password cleared and focused. The attempt is counted by the IP rate limiter. ชื่อผู้ใช้หรือรหัสผ่านไม่ถูกต้อง / กรุณาตรวจสอบแล้วลองอีกครั้ง
Error — account disabled Correct password, users.status = 'Inactive' (D-46) 401 with its own banner and no session. Deliberately distinguishable from a wrong password, because the person holds valid credentials and needs to know who to ask. บัญชีนี้ถูกปิดการใช้งาน / กรุณาติดต่อนิติบุคคลเพื่อเปิดใช้งานอีกครั้ง
Error — rate limited 11th attempt from one IP inside 15 minutes (D-37) 429, banner replaces the credential banner, primary button disabled. The limiter keys on the real client IP via trust proxy, so one wrong password does not lock the whole building out. พยายามเข้าสู่ระบบไม่สำเร็จหลายครั้งเกินไป / กรุณารอ 15 นาที แล้วลองใหม่อีกครั้ง
Error — session expired Arriving at /login after an 8-hour rolling session lapsed, or after a CSRF token mismatch .notice.warn above the form; the intended URL is remembered and used after a successful sign-in. เซสชันหมดอายุ / กรุณาเข้าสู่ระบบอีกครั้ง
Error — server 500 (database unreachable, session store down) .notice.bad above the form, values preserved, ลองอีกครั้ง offered. The stack trace goes to stdout only. ระบบขัดข้องชั่วคราว / กรุณาลองอีกครั้งในอีกสักครู่
Success Valid credentials, Active account Session row written to MySQL, cookie set (httpOnly, SameSite=Lax, Secure in production), 302 to /dashboard for admin and /readings for staff. No toast: the next screen is the confirmation. —
Permission denied An already-signed-in user opens /login Not an error: 302 straight to the role's landing page. The Login screen itself has no 403 state, because it is the only route that requires no session. —

Change password — เปลี่ยนรหัสผ่าน · GET /password · POST /password

Role admin · staff — the only Admin-and-Staff form in the product Thai title เปลี่ยนรหัสผ่าน Entry points the account block at the foot of the Admin sidebar; the account footer of the staff ภาพรวม; the Dashboard first-login banner Primary action บันทึกรหัสผ่านใหม่ Devices desktop 1280 px+ and Chrome for Android 360 px — a staff member changes their password on the same phone they read meters with Exits 303 back to /password with a success flash; the session is kept

This screen exists because D-37 says "first login forces a change" and the schema has no must_change_password column to force it with (engineering specification §3.7 item 8). The resolution is a reachable screen plus a banner rather than a gate: nobody is trapped, and the seeded password still gets replaced on day one. It is a change and never a reset — every field asks for something only the account's owner knows, and a forgotten password is recovered by an Admin on ผู้ใช้งานระบบ (D-46), which is the sentence the Login footer already carries.

app.juristic.local/password
ศุภาลัย เรสซิเดนซ์Juristic Platform
📊 ภาพรวม 📝 จดมิเตอร์ 🧾 บิลประจำเดือน 12 💵 รับชำระเงิน 🚪 ห้องพัก 👤 ผู้เช่า 📢 ประกาศ 📈 รายงาน
ตั้งค่าระบบ
⚙️ ตั้งค่า 👥 ผู้ใช้งานระบบ
1นิติบุคคล · admin · เปลี่ยนรหัสผ่าน · ออกจากระบบ
เปลี่ยนรหัสผ่านบัญชี admin
เปลี่ยนรหัสผ่านของคุณ
5ลืมรหัสผ่านใช้หน้านี้ไม่ได้หากจำรหัสผ่านปัจจุบันไม่ได้ กรุณาติดต่อนิติบุคคลเพื่อตั้งรหัสผ่านใหม่
  1. The account block is the only entry point in the navigation. .user at the foot of the sidebar reads นิติบุคคล · admin · เปลี่ยนรหัสผ่าน · ออกจากระบบ — display name, username, then the two account actions. It is rendered identically on every Admin page (Design Foundations §4), and the staff equivalent is a one-line footer on ภาพรวม, never a fourth tab.
  2. รหัสผ่านปัจจุบัน is what makes this a change and not a takeover. autocomplete="current-password", required. A wrong value returns 422 with the form re-rendered and password_hash byte-identical — an unlocked, unattended browser cannot be turned into a password change (TC-AUTH-020).
  3. รหัสผ่านใหม่, minimum 8 characters, stated as a hint before the person types rather than as an error afterwards. autocomplete="new-password" so a password manager offers to generate rather than to fill. There is no strength meter, no character-class rule and no expiry: D-37 fixes the policy at bcrypt cost 10 and a length floor, and inventing more here would be a rule nothing enforces.
  4. ยืนยันรหัสผ่านใหม่ is compared server-side, not in the browser, so the message is the same string on both surfaces (D-40). On a mismatch only this field carries .field.invalid; both password values are cleared and focus returns here.
  5. The recovery sentence, repeated from Login. A person who opens this screen because they have forgotten their password is in the wrong place, and the screen says so before they try three times. It is a sentence, not a link — D-37 and D-46 make Admin's ตั้งรหัสผ่านใหม่ the only recovery path in v1.

Alternate state — staff phone 390 px, both errors at once

เปลี่ยนรหัสผ่าน
บัญชี staff
6 บันทึกไม่สำเร็จ กรุณาตรวจสอบข้อมูลที่กรอกแล้วลองอีกครั้ง
7เจ้าหน้าที่ · staff · เปลี่ยนรหัสผ่าน · ออกจากระบบ
📝จดมิเตอร์ 🚪ห้องพัก 📊ภาพรวม
  1. All three errors are drawn together for reference; a real response shows only the ones that fired. The two verbatim strings the test plan asserts are รหัสผ่านต้องมีอย่างน้อย 8 ตัวอักษร and รหัสผ่านไม่ตรงกัน (TC-AUTH-021); the third, รหัสผ่านปัจจุบันไม่ถูกต้อง, is this screen's own and belongs to the 422 of TC-AUTH-020. Unlike Login, per-field messages are correct here — the person is already authenticated, so there is no account to enumerate.
  2. No fourth tab. The staff tab bar is the three items of Design Foundations §4 and none of them is active on this screen: /password is reached from the account footer of ภาพรวม, so the person came from the third tab and returns to it with ยกเลิก. This is why TC-AUTH-027's staff href set contains /password while the tab list does not.

314นายสิทธิชัย เรืองศรีก.ย. 20261,884.00 5 ก.ย. 20263 วัน เกินกำหนด ผูกแล้ว รับชำระเงิน
512นางสาวพัชรี ทองอินทร์ก.ย. 20261,880.00 5 ก.ย. 20263 วัน เกินกำหนด ผูกแล้ว รับชำระเงิน
แสดง 6 ห้องที่ค้างมากที่สุด · รวมเกินกำหนดทั้งงวด 12 ห้อง 23,146.00 บาท — หกแถวนี้รวม 12,206.00 บาท เปิดรายงานเพื่อดูอีก 6 ห้อง
  1. Sidebar, built from the role matrix. The header shows the apartment name from Settings with Juristic Platform in 11 px beneath it (D-01). The eight working pages carry the D-02 Thai labels in workflow order; ผู้ใช้งานระบบ and ตั้งค่า sit under a ระบบ divider because they are configured once, not used daily. The .count pill on บิลประจำเดือน has exactly one formula everywhere it appears: COUNT(bills WHERE period = selected AND status IN ('Unpaid','Overdue')) — the outstanding-bill count for the selected period, here 12. It is not a notification badge, it never counts Drafts, and it disappears at zero. The sidebar markup itself is identical on every Admin page (Design Foundations §4); only .nav-item.active moves. Staff render a three-item version of this list; the items are never rendered-and-hidden, because D-36 requires the amounts behind them to be absent from staff responses, not merely invisible.
  2. Month picker — the only control on the page. D-41 scopes every tile and the donut to one selected billing period, defaulting to the current calendar month. ‹ and › step one month and reload with ?period=YYYY-MM; the label is the D-03 display form กันยายน 2026 while the query string stays ISO. Stepping past the newest period with data disables ›. The three 12-month charts deliberately do not follow the picker — they always end at the selected period and look eleven months back, which is what makes the current column comparable.
  3. Room tiles — the only figures reproduced verbatim from PRD §16. ห้องทั้งหมด = COUNT(rooms); มีผู้เช่า = COUNT(rooms WHERE status='Occupied'); ห้องว่าง is the difference, never a third stored count. They are period-independent, which is why they are also the only money-free tiles staff can see. Not clickable in v1 — filtering ห้องพัก by status is that page's job.
  4. Utility tiles carry their own arithmetic in the .sub line. Each is SUM(bill_items.amount) by item_type over approved bills of the period, and the sub-line restates it as units × rate so an examiner can divide on the spot. The left rule is the utility accent (water / elec / fee) — the same three colours the LINE bill and the receipt use, so the colour means "ค่าน้ำ" everywhere in the product, never "series 1".
  5. ยอดเรียกเก็บทั้งหมด excludes ฉบับร่าง, and says so. "ไม่รวมฉบับร่าง" is not decoration: D-41 defines approved as the stored status being Unpaid or Paid, so a Draft bill contributes to no money tile. Without the sub-line the first question at the demo is why the total moved when nothing was paid. In this mockup 66 of the 67 occupied rooms carry an approved bill and room 205's is still ฉบับร่าง, so the three utility tiles sum exactly to this one: 12,060.00 + 38,416.00 + 69,300.00 = 119,776.00. Approving room 205 live moves all four figures by that bill — 12,240.00 + 38,976.00 + 70,350.00 = 121,566.00 — which is the single most watchable moment of the demo and the one the test plan asserts.
  6. ค้างชำระสะสมทุกงวด is the one deliberately all-time tile (D-41). It equals the period tile here only because D-44's seed leaves every earlier period fully paid; the sub-line says so, otherwise the repeated ฿23,146 reads as a copy-paste defect. In a real second month of operation the two diverge, and that divergence is the number the office chases.
  7. The two 12-month usage charts, inline SVG, one series each. Units, not baht — a chart never carries two y-axes, so money gets its own chart below rather than a second axis here. One series means no legend: the card title names it. Only two bars are labelled, the twelve-month peak (เม.ย. 2026, hot season) and the selected period, so the eye lands on the comparison instead of reading twelve numbers. The other bars sit at 55% opacity, which is emphasis, not a second category. Bars are anchored to the baseline with 4 px rounded tops and a 12 px gap. Each chart's aria-label states the series, the peak with its month and the current period, so a screen-reader user hears what the two direct labels show. In the running application the same series arrive from GET /api/dashboard/charts?period=2026-09 and are drawn by Chart.js; hovering a bar shows "เม.ย. 2026 · 745 หน่วย".
  8. รายรับรายเดือน — the third chart D-41 requires, and the one PRD §17 names รายรับแต่ละเดือน. One series, SUM(bills.total) WHERE status='Paid' per period over the trailing twelve periods ending at the selected one — collected, not billed, which is why the current column (96,630.00) sits visibly below its neighbours while the month is still being paid. Same geometry, same two direct labels, the --ui-paid green that means ชำระแล้ว everywhere else in the product. It is baht, so the badge reads บาท · 12 เดือน rather than หน่วย. ADR A-14's cut order lists this chart and the water chart as the two that survive a scope cut, so it is the last thing on the page to drop, not the first.
  9. Bill-status donut. Counts, not amounts — the money is already in three tiles above, and D-41 specifies this chart as a Paid vs Unpaid+Overdue count. The centre carries the total (66 ใบ — approved bills only, so room 205's ฉบับร่าง is outside the ring as it is outside every money tile) so the ring never has to be measured. On this date the period splits 54 ชำระแล้ว / 0 รอชำระ / 12 เกินกำหนด; the empty slice keeps its legend row at 0 rather than vanishing, because a disappearing row reads as a rendering fault. เกินกำหนด is hatched as well as red: red and amber sit closer together than a colourblind reader can resolve, and the palette itself is fixed by the design system, so texture is the available fix. Every slice is also named and counted in the legend, so identity is never carried by colour alone. Segments are separated by a 4 px gap.
  10. The reconciliation block — the identity D-41 requires to hold on screen. Billed − collected = outstanding, printed as a subtraction rather than left for the reader to do across three tiles. If these three ever disagree, a bill in the period is missing one of its three bill_items, which is a defect with its own assertion. Two decimals here, because this is a money statement and not a headline tile (D-03).
  11. ทางลัด — navigation, not new actions. Four links to pages that already exist, ordered by how often the office opens them: รับชำระเงิน is .btn.primary because taking cash at the counter is the daily job; บิลประจำเดือน is .btn.secondary because it is the monthly one. Nothing here performs an action, so nothing here opens a D-39 dialog — a "generate bills" button on the Dashboard would be a destructive action two clicks from a login.
  12. ห้องที่ค้างชำระ — the one list on the page. Sorted by amount descending and capped at the six largest of the period's twelve overdue bills; the ghost link opens D-42's full arrears report, which ages every Unpaid bill across all periods into 1-7 / 8-30 / 31-60 / 61+ buckets. Every status cell carries data-status="Overdue" (D-02) so a test asserts the English identifier while the reader sees เกินกำหนด. The LINE column exists because D-44 seeds 13 tenants with no binding and D-30 defines a ไม่มี LINE path — a room that cannot be reminded is a room the office must phone. The row action jumps to รับชำระเงิน with the room pre-filled; it never confirms a payment from this page. The footer states that the six rows are the top six of twelve and carries both sums, so 12 ใบ / 23,146.00 in the tile above reconciles with the 12,206.00 visible here. Every room in this table has a bill for the period by definition; the three rooms that D-23's banner reports as unread on the Monthly Billing page can therefore never appear in it.

Alternate view — staff phone 390 px, the reduced ภาพรวม of D-36

The same route, the same template, a different role. Shown on 3 ก.ย. 2026, mid-round: the desktop frame above is the same billing period five days later, after the reading round finished and all 67 bills were approved.

ไปจดมิเตอร์
15 เจ้าหน้าที่ · staff · เปลี่ยนรหัสผ่าน · ออกจากระบบ
16 📝จดมิเตอร์ 🚪ห้องพัก 📊ภาพรวม
  1. The reduction is stated, not hidden. One .notice explains why there are three tiles instead of ten. D-36 is emphatic that money is absent from staff responses rather than hidden in the template, so this banner is the honest UI for a server response that genuinely contains no amounts — and a tester can assert that no baht figure appears anywhere in the staff response body.
  2. Reading progress replaces the money block. The counter is the same X/Y that D-15 recomputes after every per-room save and that D-45's first acceptance target measures (67 rooms in ≤ 15 minutes). The denominator is billable rooms only — the 13 vacant rooms are excluded, which is why the ห้องว่าง tile carries "ไม่ต้องจด". The remaining-floors line tells the person holding the phone where to walk next; ไปจดมิเตอร์ is the one 44 px primary action and lands on จดมิเตอร์ with the ยังไม่จด filter already applied (D-15's default).
  3. The account block, and the only route to /password a staff member has. A one-line .user footer — เจ้าหน้าที่ · staff · เปลี่ยนรหัสผ่าน · ออกจากระบบ — pinned under the last card of ภาพรวม, mirroring the foot of the Admin sidebar. It is deliberately not a fourth tab: D-36's staff surface is three tabs, and a screen a person opens twice a year does not earn a permanent 25 % of the tab bar. This footer is why TC-AUTH-027's staff href set contains /password and /logout while the tab list contains neither, and both still answer 200.
  4. Bottom tabs, three items, in the Design Foundations §4 order จดมิเตอร์ · ห้องพัก · ภาพรวม. ภาพรวม is the tab that is active here, not the tab that comes first — the staff landing page is จดมิเตอร์, and a staff member reaches this screen by tapping the third tab. The sidebar is not narrowed on a phone, it is replaced: the layout switch is a container query on .app at 767 px, so this same markup renders the sidebar on the desktop frame and the tab bar here. Every tap target is at least 44 × 44 px (D-45). A staff user typing an admin URL by hand still gets the Thai 403 page of D-40, not a hidden menu item.

States — Dashboard

StateTriggerWhat the screen doesThai copy
Empty — no bills A period whose bills are all still ฉบับร่าง, or a period before the system went live Room tiles keep their real values (they are period-independent); the six money tiles render ฿0 with the sub-line explaining why; the donut, the arrears card and the reconciliation block are replaced by one .empty-state with a primary action to Monthly Billing. The three 12-month charts still draw, with a zero column for this period. Money tile sub-line: ยังไม่มีบิลที่อนุมัติในรอบนี้
Empty state: ยังไม่มีบิลในรอบนี้ + button ไปที่บิลประจำเดือน
First login — default password The signed-in user's password_hash still matches the value db/seed.sql wrote from SEED_ADMIN_PASSWORD — checked once at sign-in and carried on the session, since the schema has no must_change_password column (D-37, engineering specification §3.7 item 8) One .notice.warn above the KPI grid on both the Admin and the reduced staff ภาพรวม, carrying a single link to /password. It is dismissible for the session and returns on the next sign-in until the password is actually changed. Nothing is blocked — no redirect, no disabled navigation, no modal — because a gate with no column to drive it would be a lie, and locking an admin out of their own Dashboard on demo morning is a worse failure than a stale password on a fictional dataset. คุณยังใช้รหัสผ่านเริ่มต้นอยู่ — กรุณาเปลี่ยนรหัสผ่าน + link เปลี่ยนรหัสผ่าน
Empty — no arrears Every bill of the period is ชำระแล้ว Only the arrears card swaps to its empty state; the donut becomes a single full ring and the ค้างชำระ tile reads ฿0. Nothing else changes. ไม่มีห้องค้างชำระ 🎉 (verbatim from D-40)
Loading Page render, and every month change Tiles render server-side, so they arrive complete — there is no tile skeleton. The three charts and the donut are the only asynchronous parts (/api/dashboard/charts); each shows .skeleton at the chart's height until the series land. The arrears table shows six .skeleton.row placeholders. Month-picker arrows are disabled during the fetch. —
Error — charts failed The chart endpoint returns 500, or the fetch times out Partial failure is contained: the tiles, the arrears table and the reconciliation block stay on screen, and only the four chart bodies are replaced by a .notice.bad with a retry. The page is never blanked because a chart failed. โหลดกราฟไม่สำเร็จ / ลองอีกครั้ง
Error — page failed 500 from services/reports.js The shared Thai 500 page inside the normal shell, so the sidebar still works and the user is one click from any other page. ระบบขัดข้องชั่วคราว / กรุณาลองอีกครั้งในอีกสักครู่
Error — bad period ?period= is malformed, or names a future month 422 handled in place: the page renders the current month and shows a warning banner rather than a blank error page. The picker never produces this; only a hand-edited URL does. ไม่พบข้อมูลของงวดที่เลือก / แสดงข้อมูลของ กันยายน 2026 แทน
Success Normal admin load The default frame. No toast and no flash message — arriving on a correct Dashboard is its own confirmation. A flash does appear when the user was redirected here after an action on another page (for example a completed bulk approval). —
Permission denied A staff session opens /dashboard Not a 403: D-36 grants staff a reduced ภาพรวม, so the same route renders the three room tiles and the reading progress, and the response body contains no amount at all. The 403 page applies to the routes staff genuinely may not open — ผู้เช่า, บิลประจำเดือน, รับชำระเงิน, ใบเสร็จ, ประกาศ, รายงาน, ผู้ใช้งานระบบ, ตั้งค่า. 403 page: ไม่มีสิทธิ์เข้าถึงหน้านี้ / บัญชีของคุณเป็นเจ้าหน้าที่ กรุณาติดต่อนิติบุคคล + button ไปที่จดมิเตอร์
Session expired The 8-hour rolling session lapses while the page is open The next navigation or chart fetch returns 401 and the browser is sent to /login with the session-expired banner; the intended URL is restored after signing in. เซสชันหมดอายุ / กรุณาเข้าสู่ระบบอีกครั้ง

Handover notes for the engineer
  • Ten tiles, one query. All ten KPI values come from services/reports.js in the page render, not from /api/dashboard/charts; only the four chart series are fetched. That keeps the D-45 target of a list page in ≤ 2 seconds honest even when the chart endpoint is slow.
  • The staff branch is a different query, not a different template. views/pages/dashboard.ejs renders whatever tiles the controller passes; the controller asks services/reports.js for the staff set when req.user.role === 'staff', so no amount is ever serialised into a staff response (D-36).
  • Charts in production are Chart.js, not this SVG. The inline SVG here exists so the mockup needs no library; the shipped page uses the same geometry — one series per chart, the current period as the last column, direct labels only on the peak and the current period. D-41 fixes the set at four: 12-month water units, 12-month electricity units, 12-month collected revenue, and the status donut for the selected period. Three of the four are units-or-baht on the y-axis and one is a count; none of them ever gets a second axis.
  • The hatched เกินกำหนด segment is a requirement, not decoration. --ui-overdue and --ui-unpaid are 11.6 ΔE apart in normal vision and 5.3 ΔE under protanopia — below the readable floor. The design system's status colours are fixed, so the segment carries a 45° texture and the legend carries the count. Reproduce both in Chart.js with a pattern fill, or the chart is unreadable for roughly one examiner in twelve.
  • Every status element needs data-status. Six table cells and three legend rows on this page. It is what lets the test plan assert on Draft / Unpaid / Paid / Overdue while the screen stays Thai (D-02).

Rooms & Tenants

Five screens hold the master data every other screen reads: the room a bill is addressed to, the area the common-area fee is computed from, the meter baselines the very first reading resolves against, and the tenant whose LINE account receives the message. Nothing here computes money, but every number here ends up on a receipt, so the screens are built around three rules the decision log makes non-negotiable: room status is derived from the tenant link and is never a field (D-07), baselines live on the room and are frozen the moment the room has a reading (D-06), and a LINE User ID is never typed by a human — it arrives through the six-character code flow (D-29).

How to read these screens

Every mockup is real HTML using only the tokens.css product classes, rendered at the surface it is designed for: the Admin desktop frame is ~1,000 px of a 1,280 px browser, the phone frame is 390 px. Numbered markers on the mockup match the list beneath it. All names, phone numbers and LINE display names are fictional seed values (D-44); room 205 carries the PRD §28 demo data verbatim.

1. Rooms list — GET /rooms

9 ‹123 …10› แสดง 1–8 จาก 80 ห้อง
  1. 1 Admin sidebar, ห้องพัก active. Staff never render this markup — their shell is the three bottom tabs shown in the alternate frame below (D-36).
  2. 2 เพิ่มห้อง — the one page-level primary action, admin only. Opens the Rooms form (screen 3). The button is absent, not disabled, for staff: a control a role can never use is not rendered.
  3. 3 Search. One box matching room code or tenant name, submitted as ?q= on Enter or blur. No live search: the list is 80 rows and a keystroke-per-request loop is a cost with no benefit. Typing 205 and pressing Enter lands directly on the filtered list, not on the detail page.
  4. 4 Floor filter — reads rooms.floor, the stored column, never a substring of the room code, so A-205 filters correctly (D-47). Combines with the search and the status chip as ?q=&floor=2&status=all.
  5. 5 Status chips with live counts, 80 / 67 / 13 / 0. ปิดใช้งาน is the only way to see deactivated rooms — they are excluded from every other view (D-47). Clicking a chip reloads the list; the active chip is also the page's ?status= value, so the URL is shareable.
  6. 6 Room code is the row's identity and the link target (/rooms/41). The whole row is clickable; the trailing ดูรายละเอียด button exists for keyboard and screen-reader users, who never get a row-click affordance.
  7. 7 ผู้เช่าปัจจุบัน renders the single Active tenant (D-10) and links to that tenant. A vacant room shows an em dash, never an empty cell, so a missing value is distinguishable from a rendering fault.
  8. 8 Status badge. Derived from the tenant link and read-only everywhere in the product — there is no status control on any room form (D-07). ว่าง is grey, never red: an empty room is a fact, not a failure.
  9. 9 Pagination, 8 rows per page over 80 rooms. Server-side LIMIT/OFFSET; the counter reads แสดง 1–8 จาก 80 ห้อง so the operator can tell a filtered list from a short one. Target: this page renders in ≤ 2 s against the full seed (D-45).

Alternate state — the same page for a staff login on a 390 px phone. The layout switch is a container query on .app, so this is the same template and the same stylesheet, not a mobile variant (D-45).

4 📝จดมิเตอร์ 🚪ห้องพัก 📊ภาพรวม
  1. 1 อ่านอย่างเดียว badge replaces the primary action. Staff have no เพิ่มห้อง, no แก้ไขห้อง and no ปิดใช้งานห้อง, and a direct POST is refused by requireRole('admin'), not merely hidden (D-36).
  2. 2 Rows collapse to .list-item grouped by .group-head floor headers — the same grouping the reading round uses, so a technician sees one mental model on both tabs.
  3. 3 No money, ever. The staff response body carries no rate, no bill total and no arrears figure — the columns are absent from the query, not hidden with CSS (D-36).
  4. 4 Three bottom tabs, จดมิเตอร์ first because it is the staff job and the staff landing page.

9💧 หน่วยน้ำ 12 เดือนหน่วย

รอบนี้ 10 หน่วย · เฉลี่ย 9.8 หน่วย/เดือน

⚡ หน่วยไฟ 12 เดือนหน่วย

รอบนี้ 70 หน่วย · สูงสุด 81 หน่วย (มิ.ย. 2026)

11✅ บันทึกข้อมูลห้อง 205 แล้ว
  1. 1 Title carries the room code and the derived status badge. The room code, not the surrogate id, is what a human recognises — the id appears only in the URL.
  2. 2 ปิดใช้งานห้อง — .btn.danger, admin only, and the sixth D-39 confirmation (alternate frame below). A room with no bills at all offers ลบห้อง instead; once any bill references the room, deactivation is the only option, because deleting it would break the reprint guarantee (D-09, D-47).
  3. 3 แก้ไขห้อง opens the edit form (screen 3) pre-filled. Staff see neither this button nor the two panels on the right that carry money.
  4. 4 สถานะ is a badge, not a control, with the sentence กำหนดจากผู้เช่าโดยอัตโนมัติ beside it. There is no way to set Occupied by hand anywhere in the product; only the tenant transaction moves it (D-07).
  5. 5 Baselines — baseline_water 1,140, baseline_electric 4,510, baseline_date 30 ก.ย. 2025, the seeded rooms row of engineering spec §2.7. They are shown here because the office needs to know how old the opening numbers are, and marked ล็อกแล้ว because this room already has readings: D-06 permits editing them only while the room has no meter_readings row at all.
  6. 6 ผู้เช่าปัจจุบัน — the single Active tenant (D-10). For a vacant room this card becomes an .empty-state reading ห้องนี้ยังไม่มีผู้เช่า with a เพิ่มผู้เช่า action for admins.
  7. 7 LINE binding shown, never edited here. ผูกแล้ว plus the LINE display name and the linked date; the actions that change it live on the tenant screen (D-29).
  8. 8 Meter history, newest first, both utilities on one row so a reader can see that the electricity spike in มิ.ย. was not matched by water. Previous / current / units are the stored values, not recomputed — a reading is previous_reading, current_reading, units exactly as saved (D-12).
  9. 9 Mini usage charts, inline SVG, one per utility with its own scale — a shared axis would flatten 10 water units against 70 electricity units into a straight line. The current period is the solid bar. These are static server-rendered SVG, not Chart.js: the four Chart.js charts belong to the Dashboard (D-41).
  10. 10 Bill history with real statuses. ฉบับร่าง for the current period; a bill whose due date has passed while still Unpaid renders เกินกำหนด — a display value only, computed at render time, never stored (D-21). Amounts come from the bill's own snapshot, so changing the room's area today does not move a single historical figure (D-09).
  11. 11 Toast after returning from a successful edit: ✅ บันทึกข้อมูลห้อง 205 แล้ว. No undo link — the change is reversible by editing again, and nothing was sent to a phone (D-39).

Alternate state — ปิดใช้งานห้อง confirmation (D-39, call site 6). Shown on room 108, a vacant room that still has paid bills in its history. The primary button stays disabled until the reason reaches 10 characters.

1ปิดใช้งานห้อง 108

ห้องนี้จะไม่ถูกสร้างบิล ไม่ปรากฏในหน้าจดมิเตอร์ และไม่แสดงในรายการห้อง ยกเว้นเมื่อเลือกตัวกรอง ปิดใช้งาน

2ประวัติบิลและใบเสร็จจะยังคงอยู่

4 กรอกเหตุผลอย่างน้อย 10 ตัวอักษรก่อน
  1. 1 The dialog names the room in its title and states the exact effect in the body, with counts where there are any. The shared component is the only confirmation pattern in the product — never the browser's confirm() (D-39).
  2. 2 What survives is stated in bold: ประวัติบิลและใบเสร็จจะยังคงอยู่. Deactivation is not deletion; the room row stays and every bill that references it keeps rendering (D-09, D-47).
  3. 3 Reason is required, minimum 10 characters, and is written to audit_log.reason with action ROOM_DEACTIVATE (D-38). Collecting a reason and discarding it would be a defect.
  4. 4 The primary button repeats the verb — ปิดใช้งานห้อง, not ตกลง — and carries the real disabled attribute, not just the class, until the reason is long enough; the requirement is printed beside it rather than hidden in a tooltip. Esc and ยกเลิก both close without acting; focus returns to the button that opened the dialog.

5เลขมิเตอร์เริ่มต้นแก้ไขได้ก่อนจดมิเตอร์ครั้งแรกเท่านั้น
ทำไมต้องกรอก รอบแรกของห้องนี้จะคิดหน่วยจากเลขที่กรอกไว้ตรงนี้ ถ้ากรอกผิด บิลใบแรกจะผิดทั้งใบ — ให้ลอกเลขจากหน้าปัดมิเตอร์ ณ วันที่เริ่มต้น
6สถานะห้อง

สถานะห้องกำหนดจากผู้เช่าโดยอัตโนมัติ — ห้องที่ยังไม่มีผู้เช่าจะเป็น ว่าง และเปลี่ยนเป็น มีผู้เช่า เมื่อเพิ่มผู้เช่าให้ห้องนี้

7
  1. 1 หมายเลขห้อง — free text up to 10 characters, unique system-wide. The hint says A-205 is accepted so nobody invents a numeric-only rule that the column does not have (D-04). Uniqueness is a database constraint, so the error can only be reported after submit (annotation 3 of the alternate frame).
  2. 2 ชั้น is entered, never parsed from the room code — a select of 1–5 seeded from the existing floors. Two features read this column directly: the floor grouping on จดมิเตอร์ and the floor audience on ประกาศ (D-47).
  3. 3 ประเภทห้อง is optional and display-only. The hint says so out loud, because a "type" field is exactly the kind of column somebody later tries to price against — and pricing per type is not in this product.
  4. 4 ขนาดพื้นที่, DECIMAL(6,2), admin only, audited on every change (ROOM_AREA_CHANGE). The hint shows the arithmetic it drives so the operator can sanity-check their own entry against a bill they have seen (D-09, D-38).
  5. 5 The baselines get their own card and their own explanation. This is the highest-consequence field pair on the screen: entered wrong, the room's first bill charges 1,260 units instead of 10. They are written to rooms, not to meter_readings, so nothing can collide with the room's first real reading (D-06).
  6. 6 No status control. The card explains the rule instead of offering a select; there is no way to make a room Occupied except by giving it an Active tenant (D-07).
  7. 7 บันทึก POSTs and, on success, redirects to the new room's detail page with a toast. On failure the same page re-renders with every typed value preserved — never a redirect, never a lost form (D-40).

Alternate state — edit mode with a rejected save. Room 108, whose code was changed to one that already exists and whose area was typed with a stray character. The values the operator typed are still on screen.

แก้ไขห้อง 108 6
1 บันทึกไม่สำเร็จ — ตรวจสอบ 2 ช่องด้านล่าง ข้อมูลที่กรอกไว้ยังอยู่ครบ แก้ไขแล้วกด บันทึก อีกครั้ง
ข้อมูลห้อง
4 การแก้ไขขนาดพื้นที่มีผลกับบิลรอบถัดไปเท่านั้น บิลและใบเสร็จที่ออกไปแล้วใช้ขนาดพื้นที่ที่บันทึกไว้ในบิลใบนั้น และจะไม่เปลี่ยนตาม · การแก้ไขนี้จะถูกบันทึกลงประวัติการแก้ไข
5เลขมิเตอร์เริ่มต้นล็อกแล้ว
ห้องนี้มีการจดมิเตอร์แล้ว 11 รอบ จึงแก้ไขเลขเริ่มต้นไม่ได้ — หากมิเตอร์ถูกเปลี่ยน ให้ใช้ เปลี่ยนมิเตอร์ในรอบนี้ ในหน้าจดมิเตอร์
  1. 1 One .notice.bad above the form with the number of bad fields, and the promise that nothing was lost. The page was re-rendered, not redirected, so the browser's back button is not part of the recovery path (D-40).
  2. 2 Duplicate room code — the one error that cannot be caught before submit, because it is uq_rooms_room_code. The service catches ER_DUP_ENTRY and maps it to this field's message rather than letting a 500 escape.
  3. 3 Field-level Thai message keyed by input name. The same string is the 422 payload's message for that field — the API message and the form message are one definition in lib/schemas.js, so they cannot drift apart.
  4. 4 Area-change warning appears only in edit mode, and only states what is true: past bills keep their own snapshot and this edit is audited (D-09, D-38). It is a warning, not a confirmation dialog — the change is reversible and reaches no phone.
  5. 5 Baselines are readonly here, dashed borders, with the count of readings that locked them and the correct alternative named: a replaced meter is handled by เปลี่ยนมิเตอร์ในรอบนี้ on จดมิเตอร์, which records the swap instead of silently rewriting history (D-06, D-13).
  6. 6 The pressed button carries .is-loading and is disabled; the rest of the page stays interactive so the operator can keep reading their own entry while the request is in flight.

ประเสริฐ ชูเกียรติ086-303-99153121 มี.ค. 2024 – 31 ส.ค. 2026สิ้นสุดสัญญาแล้ว
8 ‹123 …9› แสดง 1–8 จาก 68 รายชื่อ
  1. 1 เพิ่มผู้เช่า — admin only, opens the tenant form (screen 5). Creating a tenant is what makes a room Occupied; there is no other route to that status (D-07, D-10).
  2. 2 Standing LINE-coverage banner, 54 of 67. This is the number D-35's announcement preview quotes and the number the demo asserts is still 54 after a full bulk approve, so it is worth a permanent line rather than a report nobody opens (D-44).
  3. 3 Search across name, phone and room, submitted on Enter. The room column is the fastest handle a cashier has: a tenant at the counter says their room number, not their surname.
  4. 4 Filter chips. กำลังเช่า is the default view on load — a list that mixes 67 active tenants with every past tenant is unusable. This frame deliberately shows ทั้งหมด instead, because it is the only view in which the last row (ประเสริฐ ชูเกียรติ, greyed, สิ้นสุดสัญญาแล้ว) can legitimately appear, and that row is what the frame exists to demonstrate. Under กำลังเช่า the count is 67 and that row is absent; the pagination footer always states the count of the active filter, which is why it reads 68 here. สิ้นสุดสัญญาแล้ว is how a departed tenant is found on their own, because tenant records are never deleted (D-10).
  5. 5 สถานะ LINE, three renderings. ผูกแล้ว with the LINE display name and the linked date; ยังไม่ผูก; รอผูก with the outstanding code and its expiry. The LINE User ID itself is never shown — it is meaningless to a human and useless to type (D-29).
  6. 6 สร้างรหัสผูก LINE is the row action for an unlinked tenant. It POSTs to /tenants/:id/link-code, generates a six-character code from ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789 — no O, no 0, no I, no 1 — valid 7 days, single use, and redirects to the tenant's detail page where the code and its printable slip live (screen 5, alternate frame).
  7. 7 ยกเลิกการผูก clears the binding for this tenancy only. It is deliberately not one of the six confirmation dialogs (D-39): it destroys nothing, is undone by issuing a new code, and reaches no phone. It POSTs and returns a toast.
  8. 8 An ended tenancy is greyed, not hidden — its bills, payments and receipts stay attached to it forever and are reachable through ดูประวัติ (D-08).

Alternate state — a staff login opening /tenants directly. This is the whole permission model made visible: a page a role cannot use returns a page, not a redirect (D-40).

5. Tenant form and detail — GET /tenants/new · GET /tenants/:id

Page: Tenants (form and detail) · เพิ่มผู้เช่า / ข้อมูลผู้เช่า Roles: admin only Entry points: เพิ่มผู้เช่า · row click on ผู้เช่า · ดูข้อมูลผู้เช่า on a room Primary action: บันทึก (form) · สร้างรหัสผูก LINE / สิ้นสุดสัญญาเช่า (detail)
app.juristic.local/tenants/new
ศุภาลัย เรสซิเดนซ์Juristic Platform
📊 ภาพรวม 📝 จดมิเตอร์ 🧾 บิลประจำเดือน 12 💵 รับชำระเงิน 🚪 ห้องพัก 👤 ผู้เช่า 📢 ประกาศ 📈 รายงาน
นิติบุคคล · admin · เปลี่ยนรหัสผ่าน · ออกจากระบบ
เพิ่มผู้เช่า
ข้อมูลผู้เช่า
การผูก LINEทำหลังบันทึก
6 สถานะห้อง 312 จะเปลี่ยนเป็น มีผู้เช่า ทันทีที่บันทึก และห้องนี้จะถูกรวมในการสร้างบิลของรอบถัดไป
  1. 1 ชื่อ-นามสกุล, required. The hint states the consequence: this string is snapshotted onto every bill generated during the tenancy, so a typo corrected next year does not rewrite a receipt already issued (D-09).
  2. 2 เบอร์โทรศัพท์ — Thai mobile format 0XXXXXXXXX, 10 digits, validated on blur. The error shows the accepted shape rather than the rejected one; separators are stripped before validation but the field keeps exactly what was typed (D-10, D-40).
  3. 3 วันที่เข้าอยู่ is required and is display only — no proration exists anywhere in this product, so this date changes no amount on any bill (D-08).
  4. 4 ห้อง lists vacant rooms only. An occupied room is not offered, because the database enforces one Active tenant per room through UNIQUE(active_room_id) — the select simply does not present a choice that the constraint would reject anyway (D-10).
  5. 5 LINE User ID is readonly and empty on purpose. A tenant cannot read their own userId anywhere in LINE, so staff literally cannot type it; the field exists to say so and to point at the code flow (D-29).
  6. 6 The status consequence is stated before the save, not discovered after it: saving this form flips room 312 to มีผู้เช่า and pulls it into the next generation run (D-07, D-18).

Tenant detail — the LINE link code panel. Room 207's tenant has an outstanding code. This is the whole of D-29's flow on one screen: generate, hand over, tenant sends it in chat, binding lands.

    4
  1. ให้ผู้เช่าสแกน QR เพิ่มเพื่อนบัญชีทางการของนิติบุคคล
  2. ให้ผู้เช่าพิมพ์รหัส K7QM9X ส่งเข้าไปในแชท
  3. ระบบตอบกลับว่าผูกสำเร็จ และชื่อ LINE จะขึ้นในหน้านี้ทันที
