น นิติบุคคล ศุภาลัย เรสซิเดนซ์
K7QM9X09:41
✅ ผูกบัญชีสำเร็จ คุณจะได้รับบิลและประกาศของห้อง 207 ผ่าน LINE นี้09:41
❌ ไม่พบรหัสนี้ หรือรหัสหมดอายุแล้ว กรุณาติดต่อสำนักงานนิติบุคคลตัวอย่างกรณีรหัสผิด
  1. 1 สิ้นสุดสัญญาเช่า is disabled here, because this tenant's ก.ย. 2026 bill is เกินกำหนด. D-08 blocks ending a tenancy until a final reading exists for both utilities and the bill covering it is Paid; the reason is printed under the button as a .hint — ปิดสัญญาไม่ได้: ห้อง 207 ยังมีบิลค้างชำระ 1 ใบ — never as a title tooltip, which neither a keyboard nor a touch user can reach. Disabling it with a stated reason is what turns an office rule into something the system can actually hold.
  2. 2 The code panel is the payload of the whole flow: six characters from ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789, set in the monospace face at 28 px with wide letter-spacing so it can be read aloud across a counter. No O/0 and no I/1 exist in the alphabet, so there is nothing to misread (D-29).
  3. 3 Single use, 7 days, with the expiry spelled out both as a date and as a remaining-days count. An expired code is not silently reused: สร้างรหัสใหม่ issues a fresh one and invalidates the old.
  4. 4 The three steps are printed on the screen the admin is looking at, in the order the tenant will perform them, because the person explaining the flow at the counter is reading this panel, not the manual. พิมพ์ใบผูกบัญชี prints the same three lines plus the OA QR code on a slip.
  5. 5 The two replies the tenant can receive, quoted verbatim from D-31's template set so support can recognise a screenshot a tenant sends them. v1 sends plain text only — no Flex message, no rich menu.
  6. 6 วันที่ย้ายออก is shown but never editable. It is written by the สิ้นสุดสัญญาเช่า action as the date that action was taken, and is never typed and never future-dated (D-10).
  7. 7 Bills follow the tenant, not the room. This list is the tenant's own bills, so a departed tenant's history stays reachable after a new tenant moves in (D-08). Statuses carry data-status; เกินกำหนด is computed at render time from an Unpaid bill with a past due date and is never stored (D-21).

Alternate state — สิ้นสุดสัญญาเช่า (D-39, call site 6). Room 312's departing tenant: final readings recorded, final bill Paid, so the action is available. The dialog spells out the four things that happen in one transaction.

สิ้นสุดสัญญาเช่าห้อง 312

เมื่อยืนยัน ระบบจะทำสี่อย่างพร้อมกัน

    2
  • เปลี่ยนสถานะผู้เช่าเป็น สิ้นสุดสัญญาแล้ว
  • บันทึกวันที่ย้ายออกเป็น 31 ส.ค. 2026 (วันที่กดปุ่มนี้)
  • ยกเลิกการผูก LINE ของผู้เช่ารายนี้
  • เปลี่ยนสถานะห้อง 312 เป็น ว่าง

3ประวัติบิลและใบเสร็จจะยังคงอยู่

5
  1. 1 The precondition is shown as a green banner, not assumed. D-08's gate is checked before the button is enabled, and the evidence — the final reading round and the paid bill's receipt number — is named on screen so the admin can see why the action is available.
  2. 2 Four effects, listed. The dialog does not say "are you sure"; it says exactly what will change, including the two changes that are easy to forget — the LINE unbinding and the room flipping to ว่าง. All four happen in one transaction, with the audit row written on the same connection (D-10, D-38).
  3. 3 What survives, in bold — ประวัติบิลและใบเสร็จจะยังคงอยู่. A tenant record is never deleted; bills, payments and receipts reference it forever (D-10).
  4. 4 Reason required, ≥ 10 characters, stored as audit_log.reason under action TENANCY_END. The button enables only once the field is long enough.
  5. 5 The primary button repeats the verb and is .btn.danger. After it commits, the page reloads showing the tenant as สิ้นสุดสัญญาแล้ว with วันที่ย้ายออก filled in — 31 ส.ค. 2026, the day the button was pressed, never a typed date (D-10) — and room 312 becomes ว่าง and therefore available in the ห้อง select of the tenant form. The create-form frame that follows is dated 1 ก.ย. 2026 and offers 312 for exactly that reason: UNIQUE(active_room_id) (ENG-1) forbids two Active tenants in one room, so the new tenancy can only begin on or after the day the old one closed.

6. Link-code slip — ใบรหัสเชื่อมต่อ LINE · GET /tenants/:id/link-code/slip

Page: the printable A5 slip · ใบรหัสเชื่อมต่อ LINE Roles: admin only (D-36) Device: device print — A5 portrait, 148 × 210 mm Entry point: พิมพ์ใบผูกบัญชี on the LINE panel of the tenant detail above — the only way in; there is no menu item Primary action: the browser's own print dialog; the page carries no button of its own

This sheet is the whole distribution channel. D-29 gives the office no other way to hand a tenant their one-time code — no email, no SMS, no tenant login — so the slip is not a convenience print, it is the product surface where the LINE binding actually begins. It is drawn here rather than described because the two things that make it work are physical: the code has to be readable across a counter by someone holding a phone, and the sheet has to still make sense a day later when the tenant finds it in a pocket without the person who explained it.

It is a print of the panel above, never a second generation. Opening this route does not issue a code and cannot: it renders the code the panel already shows, so a slip and a screen can never disagree about what the tenant should type. Re-printing the same slip twice is harmless; getting a new code is a separate, deliberate press of สร้างรหัสใหม่, which invalidates the old one.

สแกน QR เพิ่มเพื่อน แล้วส่งรหัส K7QM9X ในแชท

  1. 1. สแกน QR ด้านบนเพื่อเพิ่มเพื่อนบัญชีทางการของนิติบุคคล
  2. 2. พิมพ์รหัส K7QM9X ส่งเข้าไปในแชท
  3. 3. ระบบจะตอบกลับว่าผูกบัญชีสำเร็จ จากนั้นบิลและประกาศของห้อง 207 จะส่งมาทาง LINE นี้
ศุภาลัย เรสซิเดนซ์ · โทร. 021234567 · จันทร์-ศุกร์ 09:00-17:00 น.
รหัสหมดอายุแล้ว กรุณาติดต่อสำนักงานนิติบุคคลเพื่อขอรหัสใหม่
  1. 1 A5, the same sheet as the receipt. The slip reuses the .receipt print rules unchanged — 148 mm wide, the centred letterhead, the same .receipt-meta grid — so the office prints two different documents on one tray setting and the sheets look like they came from the same organisation. Only three things are added, because a receipt has no equivalent: the 24 pt code, the QR box and the tear-off footer.
  2. 2 The QR is the Official Account's, not the tenant's. One image for the whole building, exported once from the LINE console and stored as a static asset; nothing here is generated per tenant, and no QR encodes the code. It is drawn as a placeholder module grid in this document — the real asset is the OA add-friend QR (D-29). Scanning it only adds the friend; the binding still needs the code, which is what makes the code single-use and auditable.
  3. 3 The code is the largest thing on the sheet — 24 pt monospace, wide tracking. Monospace so 6 characters are 6 equal cells the eye can count; the alphabet ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789 has no O/0 and no I/1, so a code read aloud across a counter cannot be misheard into a different valid code (D-29). It is boxed rather than merely bold because a tenant scanning the page for "the thing I have to type" finds a rectangle faster than a font weight.
  4. 4 One sentence carries the whole flow — สแกน QR เพิ่มเพื่อน แล้วส่งรหัส K7QM9X ในแชท — with the code repeated inside the sentence, not only in the box. A slip folded in half, or photographed badly, still has the instruction and the code together in one line. The numbered steps below it are the same three steps the tenant-detail panel prints, in the same order (D-29), so the person at the counter and the person at home are reading the same script.
  5. 5 Expiry printed as a date, not as a countdown. The panel on screen can say "อีก 5 วัน" because it re-renders; paper cannot, so the slip prints ใช้ได้ครั้งเดียว · หมดอายุ 12 ก.ย. 2026 — seven days from issue, single use (D-29). ออกให้เมื่อ in the header gives the other end of that window, which is what lets an office decide whether a slip found in a drawer is worth handing over.
  6. 6 The footer is the apartment, and the way back. Name, phone and office hours come from the Settings profile (D-04); the software is never named, exactly as on the receipt (D-27). The second line is the reissue instruction, printed on every slip rather than only on an expired one, because the sheet a tenant is holding when the code fails is the sheet that has to tell them what to do next — รหัสหมดอายุแล้ว กรุณาติดต่อสำนักงานนิติบุคคลเพื่อขอรหัสใหม่, the paper half of the ❌ ไม่พบรหัสนี้ หรือรหัสหมดอายุแล้ว reply the webhook sends (D-31).
Link-code slip — states

Valid codeThe sheet above: room, tenant, QR, the code at 24 pt, the instruction line, expiry and the footerThe default and only common case. The route renders the stored code; it never issues one, so opening the slip twice cannot produce two codes.
Expired codeThe same sheet with .receipt-stamp across it reading รหัสหมดอายุ in red, and the reissue line in the footer promoted to bold above the fold. The code itself is still printed, greyed, so the Admin can match the sheet to the tenant recordรหัสหมดอายุ · กรุณาติดต่อสำนักงานนิติบุคคลเพื่อขอรหัสใหม่ — the paper twin of the webhook's ❌ reply. Reached by opening the slip after expires_at has passed (7 days, D-29); the fix is สร้างรหัสใหม่ on the tenant detail, which invalidates this sheet.
EmptyCannot be reached from the UI — พิมพ์ใบผูกบัญชี only exists once a code exists. Typed directly for a tenant with no code, the route is a Thai 404 (D-40), not a blank sheetยังไม่มีรหัสผูกบัญชีของห้องนี้ — กรุณาสร้างรหัสจากหน้าข้อมูลผู้เช่าก่อน
Already linkedThe slip still renders, with a note under the code, because the office may be printing it for a tenant who is re-binding a new phoneห้องนี้ผูก LINE แล้ว — the code remains usable until it expires or is used (D-29).

7. Assumptions recorded by these screens

  1. Form routes. The Design Foundations site map counts fifteen navigable pages and the ADR's views/pages/ list names rooms.ejs, room-detail.ejs and tenants.ejs. The four form and detail surfaces here — /rooms/new, /rooms/:id/edit, /tenants/new, /tenants/:id, plus /tenants/:id/edit — are treated as form modes of those same templates, not new page files, and every mutation is an ordinary form POST with POST-Redirect-GET. No new JSON endpoint is introduced: the ADR fixes the API at exactly seven, and an eighth is a decision, not a convenience.
  2. Room 205's meter baselines are the seeded ones, not this document's. baseline_water 1,140 and baseline_electric 4,510 at 30 ก.ย. 2025 — the rooms row of the engineering specification's §2.7 seed excerpt, which is also what its previous_period = 'baseline' worked example resolves against (first period 2025-10, water 1,150 − 1,140 = 10). This document previously drew 1,143 / 4,559 and chained twelve months off that; the chain has been re-derived from 1,140 / 4,510 and still lands exactly on the PRD §28 readings 1,250 → 1,260 and 5,280 → 5,350, with 2026-08 at the seeded 1,240 → 1,250 and 5,210 → 5,280. Twelve periods of water 10, 12, 9, 11, 8, 10, 10, 9, 12, 9, 10, 10 (120 หน่วย) and electricity 70, 63, 60, 66, 71, 79, 64, 73, 87, 67, 70, 70 (840 หน่วย) run oldest-first from ต.ค. 2025; the room therefore averages exactly 10 หน่วย, 70 หน่วย and 1,790.00 บาท a month, which is the PRD §28 row. None of it is an expected value in any test — only the September arithmetic is.
  3. Tenant name for room 205. สมชาย ใจดี, phone 081-234-5678, moved in 1 ต.ค. 2025, LINE display name สมชาย linked 15 ส.ค. 2026 — tenants.id 37 of the engineering spec's §2.7 seed excerpt, which is the source of record for the PRD §28 demo row and which every payload example and every test case in the sibling documents already addresses. อารยา ศรีสุวรรณ takes room 202 with phone 089-555-0142, moved in 15 พ.ค. 2025 and LINE display name Araya. The Staff persona is written ช่างสมชาย and the ช่าง prefix is what keeps the two apart on screen. All of it is fictional seed data (D-44).
  4. Room codes and ids. The URL bar shows /rooms/41 and /tenants/37, following the data model's seed ids. The room code is the only room identifier a human ever reads; the surrogate id appears nowhere on screen.
  5. Uniform 30.00 ตร.ม. Every room in the list carries the same area and type because D-44 seeds a uniform studio block — that is what makes PRD §16's 70,350.00 = 67 × 30 × 35 come out exactly. It is a seed property, not a product constraint: the area field is per-room and free.
  6. Fictional data. All tenant names, phone numbers, LINE display names and the link code K7QM9X are invented for the mockups. Amounts other than room 205's 180 / 560 / 1,050 / 1,790 are illustrative and are never expected values in a test (D-44).
  7. Row actions on the tenants list. ยกเลิกการผูก is rendered as a plain POST with a toast rather than a confirmation dialog, because D-39 fixes the dialog at exactly six call sites and unlinking destroys nothing that a new code cannot restore.

Meter Reading (Staff, mobile)

Meter Reading (จดมิเตอร์, route /readings) is the only screen a Staff account uses to do real work, and it is the only screen in the product designed phone-first. Everything on these four screens is derived from four binding decisions: the round mechanics (D-15), the reading row and its edit window (D-12), the below-previous block and the meter-replacement branch (D-13), and the Staff permission boundary (D-36).

Binding constraints these screens obey
  • D-36 — no money on the Staff surface. Staff never see a rate, an amount or a bill total; the figures are absent from the API response, not merely hidden by CSS. The live calculation Staff see is units only (10 หน่วย, 70 หน่วย). The 180 / 560 / 1,050 / 1,790 THB preview exists on the same route for an Admin session and is shown here as a clearly labelled variant frame.
  • D-15 — per-room save. One card per room, one บันทึก button per card, one request per room. A room counts as จดแล้ว only when both water and electricity are stored for the period. Four filter chips, default ยังไม่จด. Rooms grouped by rooms.floor under a sticky floor header, ordered by room code.
  • D-13 — hard block, not a rollover guess. There is no automatic rollover detection. A current reading below the previous one is a hard block with Save disabled; a rolled dial or a swapped meter is entered through the explicit เปลี่ยนมิเตอร์ในรอบนี้ branch, which both roles may use because Staff are the ones standing at the meter.
  • D-11 / D-12 — no period lock, no submitted state. Nothing is ever "closed". A reading is editable exactly while its room's bill for that period is Draft or does not yet exist, and frozen the moment the bill is approved: บิลอนุมัติแล้ว แก้ไขไม่ได้. The only way back is Admin reverting the bill to ฉบับร่าง (D-22).
  • D-18 — one submit button. ส่งให้นิติตรวจสอบ runs bill generation for the period. It is not one of the six confirmation dialogs in D-39, so the review screen is its confirmation step.

1 · Reading round — period and room list GET /readings?period=2026-09

Role staff (admin sees the same page) Entry tab จดมิเตอร์ · login redirect for staff · D-23 room links from Monthly Billing Primary action ส่งให้นิติตรวจสอบ Reads rooms, tenants, meter_readings, bills.status

📝จดมิเตอร์ 🚪ห้องพัก 📊ภาพรวม
  1. 1Month picker — defaults to the current calendar month in Asia/Bangkok and is displayed as "กันยายน 2026" (D-03); the value on the wire is the period key 2026-09. The arrows step one month and reload the page with ?period=. There is no open/close state and no lock (D-11): stepping back to สิงหาคม 2026 shows that month's rooms, editable only where the bill is still ฉบับร่าง or absent.
  2. 2Search — filters the visible list client-side on room code and tenant name; it does not change the chip counts or the X/Y counter, which always describe the whole period. Clearing it restores the active chip's full list. 44 px tall, 16 px text, so iOS does not zoom the page on focus.
  3. 3Filter chips (D-15) — ทั้งหมด / ยังไม่จด / จดแล้ว / ห้องว่าง, defaulting to ยังไม่จด because that is the working set of a round. The first three cover billable rooms only, so 67 = 25 + 42; ห้องว่าง lists the 13 vacant rooms with the badge ไม่ออกบิล for a voluntary reading (D-07) and never enters the counter. Selecting a chip re-renders the list without losing typed, unsaved values in a card that stays visible.
  4. 4Round progress — "จดแล้ว 42/67 ห้อง". A room counts as จดแล้ว only when both water and electricity are stored for the period (D-15). The value is recomputed from the server's response after every per-room save, never incremented client-side, so a failed save can never inflate it. The bar turns green at 67/67, the state the ≤ 15 min acceptance target in D-45 is measured against.
  5. 5Sticky floor header — groups come from rooms.floor, a real column, never parsed from the room code (D-47), and rooms are ordered by room code inside a group. The header stays pinned while the floor scrolls, so the walker always knows which floor the card on screen belongs to.
  6. 6Room card — one card per room holding Previous (read-only) and Current for both utilities plus its own บันทึก button, saved alone through one POST /api/readings (D-15). Nothing else on the page is submitted with it, so losing signal in a stairwell loses at most the room being typed.
  7. 7Live units — current − previous, recomputed on every keystroke, integer only. Units are the only derived figure a Staff session sees: no rate, no amount, no total (D-36). Previous is read-only text resolved by D-14 (the greatest earlier period for that room and utility, otherwise the room's baseline); the hint line names the source period so a catch-up entry after a skipped month is explained rather than disputed.
  8. 8Sticky round bar — carries the counter and the single page action ส่งให้นิติตรวจสอบ, which is enabled as soon as one room in the period has both readings (D-18). It opens the review screen (§3); it never posts directly, because the review screen is this action's confirmation step. The bar sits above the tab bar, so neither ever covers the other.

Alternate state — round complete, filter ทั้งหมด. Saved rooms collapse to a จดแล้ว summary row (D-15) with an แก้ไข affordance that re-expands the card while the bill is still ฉบับร่าง or absent.

States — reading round list

StateTriggerWhat the screen shows
Empty No billable room in the period, or the active chip matches nothing Shared empty-state component (D-40): muted icon, ยังไม่มีห้องที่ต้องจดในรอบนี้, no primary action. Search returning nothing shows ไม่พบห้องที่ค้นหา with a ล้างการค้นหา ghost button. The round bar stays, showing 0/0 with ส่งให้นิติตรวจสอบ disabled.
Loading First paint of /readings Chips and month picker render immediately from the URL; the list is six .skeleton.row blocks under one skeleton floor header. The progress bar renders at 0 with the label replaced by a skeleton. Target ≤ 2 s (D-45).
Error List request fails (5xx, or the free instance was asleep) .notice.bad above the list: โหลดรายการห้องไม่สำเร็จ plus a ลองอีกครั้ง button. Never a redirect and never a blank page.
Success A per-room save returns 200 The card collapses to a .list-item.is-done summary row, the chip counts and the X/Y counter are replaced from the response, and one toast appears: บันทึกห้อง 205 แล้ว. No undo (D-39) — the row's แก้ไข button is the correction path.
Permission denied An admin-only URL reached from this page, or a stale session Full Thai 403 page (D-40), never a silent redirect — ไม่มีสิทธิ์เข้าถึงหน้านี้ · บัญชีของคุณเป็นเจ้าหน้าที่ กรุณาติดต่อนิติบุคคล with a กลับไปหน้าจดมิเตอร์ button. An expired session on the JSON save returns 401 and the page shows เซสชันหมดอายุ กรุณาเข้าสู่ระบบใหม่ with the typed values preserved.

2 · Reading entry — Room 205 GET /readings?period=2026-09&room=205

Role staff (and admin) Entry tapping a card in the round · the D-23 room links · the returned-for-correction notice Primary action บันทึก → POST /api/readings Writes one meter_readings row per utility, UPSERT on (room_id, period, utility_type)

Same page, same card component, scoped to a single room so the deep links in D-23 and the returned-for-correction notice land somewhere useful. This is the PRD §28 room: water 1,250 → 1,260 (10 units), electricity 5,280 → 5,350 (70 units).

📝จดมิเตอร์ 🚪ห้องพัก 📊ภาพรวม
  1. 1Context banner — period, floor and tenant, plus the one sentence that explains the edit window without inventing a state machine: แก้ไขได้จนกว่านิติบุคคลจะอนุมัติบิล (D-11, D-12). When the bill is already approved this banner is replaced by the frozen notice (see §3, alternate B).
  2. 2Previous reading, read-only — a dashed read-only input carrying the value resolved by D-14: the current reading of the greatest earlier period for this room and utility, otherwise the room's baseline_water / baseline_electric (D-06). The hint names the source period. When it is not the immediately preceding month the hint reads ครอบคลุมการใช้ตั้งแต่รอบ มิ.ย. 2026; when it is the room's opening value it reads ค่าเริ่มต้นของห้อง. Staff can never edit it — baselines are Admin-only and only before the room's first reading exists.
  3. 3Current reading input — 44 px tall, 16 px text, inputmode="numeric" so Android raises the numeric keypad (D-45). INT UNSIGNED, whole units only (D-16): a decimal point, a minus sign and any non-digit are rejected on input, and the server re-validates with zod. The field shows raw digits (1260) while every read-only figure on the page is formatted with a thousands separator (1,250) — a separator inside a numeric field fights the keypad.
  4. 4Live units — 5,350 − 5,280 = 70, recomputed on every keystroke and cleared to — while the field is empty. Units, not money: the Staff API response contains no rate and no amount (D-36).
  5. 5เปลี่ยนมิเตอร์ในรอบนี้ — available to both roles because Staff are the ones at the meter (D-13). Ticking it reveals the replacement branch (alternate B) and suspends the below-previous block. There is deliberately no automatic rollover detection: a dial that has rolled past its maximum is entered here, with the old meter's final number and the new meter's start number, so the arithmetic is recorded rather than guessed.
  6. 6บันทึก — saves this room alone through one POST /api/readings carrying both utilities, and returns the room's saved state plus the round's X/Y counter (D-15). The write is an UPSERT on UNIQUE(room_id, period, utility_type), so a double tap is a no-op rather than a duplicate (D-12); the button shows .btn.is-loading and is disabled for the duration. Saving recalculates that room's Draft bill in place immediately (D-18).
  7. 7Reading history — the three most recent periods for this room, units only. It is what lets a walker sanity-check a reading on the spot and is the same data D-13's soft usage flag is computed from. A missed month appears as a gap, never as a zero row (D-14).

Alternate A — current below previous (hard block, D-13). The walker typed 1,180 into a meter whose previous reading was 1,250.

Alternate B — meter replacement branch (D-13). Room 210's electricity meter was swapped mid-round. The previous reading was 1,830, the old meter finished at 1,926, the new meter started at 0 and now reads 96, so units = (1,926 − 1,830) + (96 − 0) = 96 + 96 = 192. The form renders that arithmetic line by line, so the walker can check it against the two dials in front of them before saving.

📝จดมิเตอร์ 🚪ห้องพัก 📊ภาพรวม
  1. 8Inline failure banner — a failed save never redirects and never reloads the phone screen; every typed value survives (D-40). The banner summarises how many rows were refused and points at the one legitimate escape hatch, the meter-replacement checkbox.
  2. 9Below-previous hard block — the exact Thai string is เลขมิเตอร์ปัจจุบันน้อยกว่าเดือนก่อน (1,250) กรุณาตรวจสอบ, identical in the form and in the API envelope (error code READING_BELOW_PREVIOUS, HTTP 422) so one string is testable on both surfaces. The field takes .field.invalid and aria-invalid="true"; the units read-out clears rather than showing a negative, and CHECK (units >= 0) in the schema is the last word. The electricity field beside it shows the empty-field message กรุณากรอกเลขมิเตอร์: both utilities are required, because a room is only จดแล้ว with both.
  3. 10The escape hatch stays visible — the checkbox is not hidden behind the error. Ticking it clears the block for this room immediately and re-validates against the replacement rules instead.
  4. 11Save disabled — while any field on the card is invalid the button carries the real disabled attribute, not merely the .is-disabled class: a class alone leaves the button focusable and still activated by Enter, and assistive technology announces it as available. The reason sits under the button as visible .hint text wired with aria-describedby, never in a title. All of that is still presentation only: the API rejects the same payload with 422 for every role, so a disabled button is never the security boundary.
  5. 12Checkbox ticked — reveals three required fields and switches the validation rules. Unticking it hides them, discards their values and restores the below-previous block.
  6. 13Mode warning — states plainly that the ordinary guard is off, so nobody uses this branch to force a bad number through. The row will carry meter_replaced = 1 and be flagged for Admin at the approval gate that already exists.
  7. 14Replacement fields — เลขสุดท้ายของมิเตอร์เดิม (old_final) and เลขเริ่มต้นของมิเตอร์ใหม่ (new_start, defaulting to 0). Validation: old_final ≥ previous_reading — เลขสุดท้ายของมิเตอร์เดิมต้องไม่น้อยกว่า 1,830 — and current_reading ≥ new_start — เลขปัจจุบันต้องไม่น้อยกว่าเลขเริ่มต้นของมิเตอร์ใหม่ (0). Both keep units ≥ 0.
  8. 15Reason, required — free Thai text stored on the reading row and written to audit_log with action METER_REPLACEMENT and the entering user's name (D-38). Empty reason blocks the save with กรุณาระบุเหตุผลการเปลี่ยนมิเตอร์.
  9. 16Arithmetic shown, not hidden — units = (old_final − previous_reading) + (current_reading − new_start), rendered as two lines and a total so the walker can check it against the two physical dials before saving. This is the same expression the service computes; the screen never sends a units value.

Admin variant of the same route. An Admin session on /readings additionally sees the Draft bill preview, because Admin may see rates and amounts (D-36). This is the PRD §28 arithmetic and it is the only place amounts appear anywhere in this section.

States — reading entry

StateTriggerWhat the screen shows
Empty A room with no reading yet for the period Both Current fields empty with placeholder 0, both units read-outs showing —, บันทึก disabled until both fields hold a valid integer. A room with no earlier reading at all shows its baseline as Previous with the hint ค่าเริ่มต้นของห้อง (D-06).
Loading บันทึก pressed The button becomes .btn.is-loading (spinner, label hidden) and is disabled; the inputs stay enabled and keep their values so a slow network never blocks re-reading them.
Error Validation (422) or transport failure Validation: per-field .field.invalid plus the exact Thai message from the API's fields map; the summary banner counts the failures. Transport: .notice.bad — บันทึกไม่สำเร็จ กรุณาลองอีกครั้ง with a ลองอีกครั้ง button that re-posts the values already on screen. No reload, no lost typing (D-40).
Success 200 from POST /api/readings Card collapses to the จดแล้ว summary row, the counter and chip counts are replaced from the response, toast บันทึกห้อง 205 แล้ว. A double tap is an UPSERT on the unique key and produces the identical row (D-12). If the room's bill for the period is already ฉบับร่าง, it is recalculated in place on the same request (D-18).
Permission denied The bill for this room and period is approved (Unpaid / Paid / displaying Overdue) Both Current fields render as read-only inputs, the checkbox is removed, บันทึก is gone, and a .notice.warn states บิลอนุมัติแล้ว แก้ไขไม่ได้ (verbatim D-12) with the sub-line ต้องให้นิติบุคคลตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่างก่อน. A staff account that reaches an admin URL gets the Thai 403 page instead (D-40).

Confirmation dialogNone. Saving a reading is not one of the six confirmed actions in D-39, and it is fully reversible while the bill is ฉบับร่าง. Adding a dialog here would put a modal between a walker and 67 rooms.

3 · Review & submit the period GET /readings?period=2026-09&view=review

Role staff (and admin) Entry ส่งให้นิติตรวจสอบ in the round bar Primary action ส่งให้นิติตรวจสอบ → POST /api/bills/generate Effect creates / recalculates ฉบับร่าง bills — no LINE message, nothing irreversible

D-18 puts bill generation behind one button, and D-39 deliberately does not give it a confirmation dialog. This screen is therefore the confirmation: it states what will and will not be generated, surfaces the two soft flags from D-13 while they can still be re-walked, and lists the rooms that will simply be skipped (D-23).

จะสร้างบิล 42 ห้อง

📝จดมิเตอร์ 🚪ห้องพัก 📊ภาพรวม
  1. 1Three-step stepper — จดมิเตอร์ → ตรวจทาน → นิติอนุมัติ. It shows where the round sits without implying a stored state: there is no submitted state and no period lock (D-11, D-18). Step 3 is another person's job and is never marked done on this screen.
  2. 2Round summary — the four figures a walker is accountable for. All of them are counts of rooms; none is money (D-36). ควรตรวจสอบ is the sum of the two soft flags and is advisory only.
  3. 3Skipped rooms (D-23) — the exact banner sentence used on Monthly Billing, so the office reads one string in two places: ยังไม่ได้จดมิเตอร์ 25 ห้อง: 101, 106, 113 … — ยังไม่ได้สร้างบิล. Codes link to /readings?period=2026-09&room=101. Submission is not blocked: a re-run after the stragglers are read creates only their bills, at the same rate for the period (D-17).
  4. 4Soft flags, non-blocking (D-13) — ใช้สูงผิดปกติ fires only when the room's previous-period usage for the same utility was ≥ 10 units and current usage exceeds 3× it (312: 68 → 240; 116: 11 → 96). ไม่มีการใช้งาน fires on 0 units for a room with an Active tenant. Neither disables anything; each row's แก้ไข jumps straight to that room's card while the bill is still ฉบับร่าง. The floor of 10 units is what keeps the flag from firing on every room coming back from a vacant month.
  5. 5Effect statement — this paragraph is the confirmation. It names exactly what the button does and, more usefully, what it does not do: no LINE message, no charge, nothing frozen. Approval and delivery are Admin's single gate (D-19), not this one.
  6. 6ส่งให้นิติตรวจสอบ — posts to /api/bills/generate for the period. Enabled once at least one room has both readings (D-18); pressing it twice is harmless because bills carry UNIQUE(room_id, period) and a re-run recalculates Drafts and never touches an approved bill. No confirm(), no dialog (D-39).

Alternate A — result of the generation run. The exact summary string from D-18, rendered where the walker is standing rather than on an Admin screen they cannot open.

Alternate B — frozen rooms and a room Admin returned for correction (D-12, D-22). Both states live in the same list, which is why they are drawn in one frame: room 205's bill was approved and is now untouchable; room 312's bill was reverted to ฉบับร่าง with a reason, and is editable again.

📝จดมิเตอร์ 🚪ห้องพัก 📊ภาพรวม
  1. 7Returned-for-correction banner — appears whenever a bill for the selected period was reverted from Unpaid to ฉบับร่าง by Admin (D-22). It quotes the Admin's required reason (minimum 10 Thai characters) verbatim, because a returned room without a reason is a walk up five floors for nothing. The banner is derived from the audit_log row with action BILL_REVERT, so no new column and no new table is needed (D-38).
  2. 8Frozen room — room 205's bill is approved, so its readings are frozen for everyone including Admin (D-12). The row keeps the จดแล้ว styling but loses its แก้ไข button and carries แก้ไขไม่ได้; the meta line states the reason in the exact D-12 words, บิลอนุมัติแล้ว แก้ไขไม่ได้. Tapping the row opens the read-only variant of §2, never an editable form that would fail on save.
  3. 9Returned room, editable again — the revert unfroze this room's readings, so the card is live once more with the reason pinned inside it. The button reads บันทึกและส่งตรวจอีกครั้ง: saving recalculates the room's Draft bill immediately (D-18), and the room re-enters the ordinary flow. Nothing about "returned" is stored on the reading — the room is editable simply because its bill is ฉบับร่าง again, which is the one rule the whole screen runs on.

States — review & submit

StateTriggerWhat the screen shows
Empty No room in the period has both readings The KPI row still renders (0 / 67 / 0 / 0), the anomaly and replacement sections are omitted entirely rather than shown empty, and the round bar reads ยังไม่มีห้องที่จดครบ with ส่งให้นิติตรวจสอบ disabled (D-18 enables it at one complete room).
Loading ส่งให้นิติตรวจสอบ pressed Button becomes .btn.is-loading; the page is not replaced until /api/bills/generate returns, because the summary counts are the whole point of the next screen. Generation over 67 rooms is one transaction and is expected well inside the five-second budget in D-45.
Error Generation fails .notice.bad — ส่งไม่สำเร็จ ยังไม่มีการสร้างบิล กรุณาลองอีกครั้ง with a retry button. The transaction is all-or-nothing, so a failed run leaves no half-built period and a retry is always safe.
Success 200 from /api/bills/generate Alternate A, carrying the D-18 string verbatim: สร้างบิลใหม่ 42 ใบ · อัปเดต 0 ใบ · ข้าม (อนุมัติแล้ว) 0 ใบ · ยังไม่ได้จด 25 ห้อง. Bills are ฉบับร่าง; no LINE message is sent at this step (that is D-19's Admin gate).
Permission denied Every bill in the period is already approved The run is still permitted and simply reports ข้าม (อนุมัติแล้ว) 42 ใบ — approved bills are never touched (D-18). Individually, each approved room shows as frozen (alternate B). A staff account opening /bills or /bills/:id gets the Thai 403 page with the กลับไปหน้าจดมิเตอร์ button, and the response body carries no amount at all (D-36, D-40).

4 · Staff bottom tab navigation and the reduced ภาพรวม GET /dashboard

Role staff only (admin uses the desktop sidebar) Entry every staff page; login lands here Primary action จดมิเตอร์ Shown under @container app (max-width:767px)

The tab bar is not a screen of its own — it is the persistent chrome under every Staff page, produced by the container query in the design system, which is why a phone frame inside this desktop-width document still renders the phone layout. It carries exactly the three pages the D-36 matrix grants Staff. Anything else is a 403, not a hidden link.

4 บัญชีเจ้าหน้าที่หน้านี้ไม่แสดงค่าบริการ ยอดเงิน หรืออัตราค่าน้ำค่าไฟ หากต้องการดูบิล กรุณาติดต่อนิติบุคคล
5 📝จดมิเตอร์ 🚪ห้องพัก 📊ภาพรวม
  1. 1Top bar — page title, the signed-in identity, and ออกจากระบบ. Logout lives here rather than in a fourth tab: the tab bar is reserved for the three pages the role matrix grants, and a destination that is not a page does not deserve a tab.
  2. 2Reduced ภาพรวม (D-36) — room counts and this period's reading progress, and nothing else. No revenue tile, no arrears tile, no chart: those figures are not merely hidden from the template, they are absent from the response a staff session receives.
  3. 3Progress card — the same X/Y counter as the round bar, computed the same way (both readings stored ⇒ จดแล้ว), plus the per-floor remainder so the walker can plan the route. Its button is the one primary action on the page.
  4. 4The boundary, stated — one sentence explaining why there are no amounts, so the absence reads as a rule rather than a bug and nobody files it as a missing feature.
  5. 5Bottom tabs — exactly three, in the order Design Foundations §4 fixes: จดมิเตอร์ /readings, ห้องพัก /rooms (read-only list and detail, no rates and no amounts), ภาพรวม /dashboard. จดมิเตอร์ leads because it is both the staff job and the staff landing page; the markup and the icon set (📝 · 🚪 · 📊) are identical in every staff frame in this document and only .tab.active moves. Each tab is at least 44 × 44 px (D-45); the active tab is brand-coloured and semibold, and colour is never the only signal because the label is always visible. The bar is position:sticky; bottom:0 and the round bar parks above it, so neither covers the other. It appears only under the design system's @container app (max-width:767px) rule — an Admin at 1280 px gets the sidebar instead.

Staff navigation matrix (D-36)

Tab / pageRouteStaffWhat Staff may do
ภาพรวม (Dashboard)/dashboardyes Room counts and this period's reading progress. No money figure of any kind.
ห้องพัก (Rooms)/rooms, /rooms/:idyes Read-only list and detail plus the room's reading history. No area edit, no baselines, no rates.
จดมิเตอร์ (Meter Reading)/readingsyes Create and edit readings while the bill is ฉบับร่าง or absent, record a meter replacement, press ส่งให้นิติตรวจสอบ.
ผู้เช่า · บิลประจำเดือน · รายละเอียดบิล · รับชำระเงิน · ใบเสร็จ · ประกาศ · รายงาน · ตั้งค่า · ผู้ใช้งานระบบ /tenants, /bills, /bills/:id, /payments, /receipts/:id, /announcements, /reports, /settings, /users 403 No tab, and a direct URL returns the Thai 403 page — never a silent redirect, so the matrix is testable row by row (D-40).

States — navigation

StateTriggerWhat the screen shows
EmptySeed not loaded / no rooms KPI values render as 0 and the progress card is replaced by the shared empty state ยังไม่มีข้อมูลห้อง. Staff get no เพิ่มห้อง action — creating rooms is Admin-only.
LoadingFirst paint Tab bar and top bar paint immediately from the session; the KPI values and the progress bar are skeletons. The tab bar is never skeletonised — navigation must be usable before data arrives.
ErrorDashboard query fails .notice.bad — โหลดข้อมูลไม่สำเร็จ plus ลองอีกครั้ง. The จดมิเตอร์ tab stays reachable, because a broken overview must not block the round.
SuccessRound finished Progress card badge flips to ครบแล้ว, the bar turns green (.progress.ok), and the card action becomes ตรวจทานและส่งให้นิติ.
Permission deniedStaff opens an admin route Full-page Thai 403 (D-40) — ไม่มีสิทธิ์เข้าถึงหน้านี้ · บัญชีของคุณเป็นเจ้าหน้าที่ กรุณาติดต่อนิติบุคคล with the primary button กลับไปหน้าจดมิเตอร์. The tab bar stays visible so the user is never stranded. Session expiry sends them to เข้าสู่ระบบ with เซสชันหมดอายุ กรุณาเข้าสู่ระบบใหม่.

Assumptions recorded for these screens

Design decisions taken here that the PM log does not settle
  1. Amounts are absent from every Staff frame. The brief for this section asked for live computed units and amounts on the reading screen; D-36 forbids Staff any amount or rate. D-36 wins. Units are live on the Staff screens, and the 180 / 560 / 1,050 / 1,790 preview is shown once, in a clearly labelled Admin frame on the same route.
  2. No rollover confirmation dialog exists. The brief mentioned a "rollover/replacement confirm"; D-13 states there is no automatic rollover detection and no dialog — a rolled dial is entered through เปลี่ยนมิเตอร์ในรอบนี้. The checkbox branch is the whole mechanism.
  3. Deep-link shape /readings?period=YYYY-MM&room=205. D-23 requires room codes that link "straight to the Meter Reading page for that room" but names no URL. A query parameter on the existing route keeps the ADR's seven-endpoint and route conventions intact and adds no page.
  4. Review screen shape ?view=review. D-18 places ส่งให้นิติตรวจสอบ on the Meter Reading page and D-39 gives it no dialog, so the review step is a mode of the same route rather than a new page, a new endpoint or a modal.
  5. Staff-side surfacing of a returned bill. D-22 defines the Admin revert and its mandatory reason but does not say how Staff learn about it. The banner and the per-card reason are derived from the existing audit_log BILL_REVERT row (D-38) — no new column, no notification table, no push.
  6. Three tabs, logout in the top bar. D-36 grants Staff exactly three pages; a fourth "account" tab would be a destination that is not one of them.
  7. Numeric inputs hold raw digits. Read-only figures use the D-03 thousands separator (1,250); the editable Current field shows 1260, because a separator inside a field driven by the Android numeric keypad has to be stripped on every keystroke.
  8. Reading history is capped at three periods on the phone. The room's full history lives on ห้องพัก → room detail; three rows are what fits above the fold and what a sanity check needs.
  9. Example rooms other than 205. Rooms 108, 112, 116, 210, 312 and 409 and their Thai tenant names are illustrative, consistent with the D-44 seed shape (80 rooms across 5 floors, 67 occupied, 13 vacant, all 30.00 ตร.ม.) and with the seed-identity table in Design Foundations §8.9, which is the single source for every room code that appears in more than one chapter. Room 205 is not one of the illustrative rooms: its tenant is สมชาย ใจดี here exactly as in every other chapter, and only room 205's figures are binding.

Monthly Billing & Bill Detail

These two screens are the money spine of the product: Monthly Billing is where an Admin sees a whole period at once and pushes a batch of Draft bills through the single approval gate (D-19), and Bill Detail is where one bill is checked line by line, approved, delivered over LINE, paid, and — when something is wrong — corrected. Both are Admin-only surfaces; Staff never reach them and never receive an amount in an API response (D-36). Every figure below is the PRD §28 demo room reproduced exactly: room 205, 30 ตร.ม., water 1,250→1,260 = 10 units × 18 = 180, electricity 5,280→5,350 = 70 units × 8 = 560, common area 30 × 35 = 1,050, total 1,790 THB, due 5 ก.ย. 2026, paid 3 ก.ย. 2026, receipt REC-202609-000852.

Design decisions taken in these two screens (recorded, not asked)
  • The list mockup is dated 8 ก.ย. 2026, the detail mockups 1–3 ก.ย. 2026. One period page cannot show all four statuses on the day of approval, because Overdue is derived from due_date < CURDATE() (D-21). The list is therefore shown a week into the period, when the first batch (approved 1 ก.ย., due 5 ก.ย.) has aged into เกินกำหนด, a second small batch (approved 7 ก.ย., due 10 ก.ย.) is still รอชำระ, three late-read rooms are fresh ฉบับร่าง, and room 205 — approved 1 ก.ย., paid 3 ก.ย. — is ชำระแล้ว. All four badges are real states of the same period, not a display sampler.
  • Generation has no confirmation dialog. The brief asked for one, but D-39 fixes the confirmation list at exactly six call sites and generation is not among them. Generation is idempotent, never touches an approved bill and only ever writes or rewrites Drafts (D-18), so it runs on click and reports the D-18 summary as an inline .notice. Adding a seventh dialog would contradict a binding decision; the destructive act in this period is approval, and that one is guarded.
  • Checkboxes exist only on Draft rows. D-19 enables the bulk button when at least one selected bill is Draft; rather than letting an Admin select 43 paid bills and silently skip them, non-Draft rows render a disabled checkbox with a Thai title. The selected count and the button count are therefore always the number of bills that will actually change.
  • The revert-to-Draft dialog is specified in the copy table, not drawn. It is the shared D-39 dialog with a required reason field, identical in shape to the void-payment dialog; a fourth Bill Detail frame would add no information the copy table does not carry.
  • Every mockup is Admin. The Staff view of both routes is the Thai 403 page (D-40), specified in each states table rather than drawn twice.

‹ 1 2 3 4 5 6 › แสดง 13–24 จาก 64 บิล · เลือกไว้ 3 16
  1. 1Month picker (.month-picker) — the period is the whole page's query, ?period=2026-09, rendered "กันยายน 2026" and defaulting to the current calendar month in Asia/Bangkok (D-11). The arrows step one month and reload the page server-side; there is no period lock and no "closed" state, so any month can be opened at any time. Choosing a month with no bills lands on the empty state.
  2. 2สร้าง/อัปเดตบิลฉบับร่าง — runs generation for the selected period (POST /api/bills/generate, D-18). It creates a Draft for every billable room that has both readings and no bill, recalculates existing Drafts in place, and never touches an approved bill. It runs on click with no dialog (see the decision callout) and answers with annotation 4's summary notice. While it runs the button takes .is-loading and is disabled; a second click can therefore not start a second run, and the unique key (room_id, period) makes a race harmless anyway.
  3. 3อนุมัติและส่งบิล (3 ห้อง) — the page's one primary action and the only irreversible one. Its count mirrors the checked Draft rows live; with nothing selected it renders [disabled] with the hint "เลือกบิลฉบับร่างอย่างน้อย 1 ใบ". Clicking opens the D-39 bulk dialog (frame 1b). Admin only — the button is not merely hidden from Staff, the whole route is (D-36).
  4. 4Generation result notice (.notice.ok) — the verbatim D-18 summary "สร้างบิลใหม่ {a} ใบ · อัปเดต {b} ใบ · ข้าม (อนุมัติแล้ว) {c} ใบ · ยังไม่ได้จด {d} ห้อง". It is a flash rendered after the POST-Redirect-GET, so it survives the reload and disappears on the next navigation. A run that changes nothing still reports honestly ("สร้างบิลใหม่ 0 ใบ"), which is the visible proof that re-running is safe.
  5. 5Unread-rooms banner (.notice.warn, D-23) — persistent, not dismissible, and present for as long as any billable room in this period lacks a bill. Each room code is a link to Meter Reading for that room and period. It never blocks approval: the office approves the bills that exist and re-runs generation when the missing rooms are read. It disappears exactly when the count reaches 0, which is the tester's assertion for "ทุกห้องที่มีผู้เช่าได้รับบิล".
  6. 6Completeness line (D-11) — derived, never stored: "บิลครบทุกห้อง 64/67 · อนุมัติแล้ว 61 · ชำระแล้ว 43", where 67 is the count of billable rooms (Occupied with exactly one Active tenant, D-07) on the generation date. It replaces the period open/close state machine the system deliberately does not have.
  7. 7KPI tiles (.kpi-grid, D-41) — the three money tiles aggregate over the stored status column, status IN ('Unpaid','Paid'), so a Draft is not yet "billed" and an aged Unpaid bill is still counted in ยอดเรียกเก็บ; that is a deliberate aggregation, not a violation of the display rule in D-21. The fourth tile shows the Draft total separately, and is labelled so nobody adds it to the first tile by eye.
  8. 8Status filter chips (.chip-row) — client-side filters over the loaded page plus a server query string; counts are for the whole period, not the visible page. Every count is computed through the shared display-status helper (D-21), so a bill whose due date passed at midnight moves from รอชำระ to เกินกำหนด with no job and no drift. ทั้งหมด is the default and is the only chip that can be active together with a search term.
  9. 9Search (.search) — filters by room code or tenant name inside the selected period; it never crosses periods. Typing narrows the table on keyup with a 200 ms debounce; the selection is preserved while filtering, and the primary button's count keeps counting hidden-but-selected rows, so a filter can never silently shrink a batch.
  10. 10Select-all-in-view — selects only the selectable (Draft) rows of the current page, never all 64 bills of the period, so the count on the primary button is always exactly what will change. With some but not all Draft rows checked it renders indeterminate. Paging away clears the selection; the batch is one page, which is also what keeps the approve transaction small (D-19).
  11. 11Status column — one .badge per bill carrying data-status="Draft|Unpaid|Paid|Overdue" (D-02) so tests assert the English identifier while the human reads Thai. Only three of the four exist in the database; เกินกำหนด is derived at render time from status='Unpaid' AND due_date < CURDATE() (D-21). Nothing on this page can edit a status directly.
  12. 12LINE delivery column (D-30) — per-bill delivery state, independent of money state: ส่งแล้ว, ยังไม่ได้ส่ง — ไม่มี LINE, ส่งไม่สำเร็จ, and "—" for a Draft that has not been sent at all. A LINE outage can never change what this column's row is worth in baht: approval commits first, delivery happens after and outside the transaction.
  13. 13Failed delivery — title carries the reason, the timestamp and attempt_count ("LINE API 500 · 1 ก.ย. 2026 14:32 น. · ลองแล้ว 3 ครั้ง"). Automatic retries stop at three attempts; the Admin's manual ส่งแจ้งเตือนอีกครั้ง on Bill Detail is never capped. The word "blocked" appears nowhere: LINE reports a blocked recipient as a success, and that gap is a listed limitation (D-05 #9).
  14. 14Room 205, the demo row — the room code and ดูบิล both open /bills/118. The whole row is a link target; tr.is-selected here marks the row the Admin arrived from, not a checkbox selection. Common area is 1,050.00 on every row because all 80 seeded rooms are 30.00 ตร.ม. (D-44).
  15. 15A Draft whose tenant has no LINE binding — flagged before approval, not after, so the office knows it will be handing this room a paper ใบแจ้งค่าใช้จ่าย. Approving it still works; the notification row is written with status='skipped_no_line' and appears in the result summary's ยังไม่ได้ส่ง count (frame 1c). 13 of the 67 seeded tenants deliberately have no binding (D-44).
  16. 16Pagination and selection count — 12 rows per page over up to 67 bills; the page info repeats the live selection count so the primary button's number is verifiable without scrolling back up. Sorting is by room code ascending and is not user-configurable in v1.

อนุมัติและส่งบิล 3 ห้อง ผ่าน LINE — ยกเลิกไม่ได้

ห้อง209, 211, 214
รอบบิลกันยายน 2026
ยอดรวมทั้งชุด5,886.00 บาท
กำหนดชำระที่จะถูกล็อก11 ก.ย. 2026
ส่ง LINE ได้2 ห้อง
ไม่มี LINE (ต้องพิมพ์ใบแจ้งหนี้)1 ห้อง · 211
18

หลังอนุมัติแล้ว เลขมิเตอร์ อัตรา และยอดเงินของบิลชุดนี้จะถูกล็อก แก้ไขได้เฉพาะการตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่างพร้อมระบุเหตุผล

19
  1. 17The shared confirmation dialog (.modal + .dialog) — one component, six call sites, never the browser's native confirm() (D-39). The title carries the count, the body opens with the verbatim D-39 sentence "อนุมัติและส่งบิล {N} ห้อง ผ่าน LINE — ยกเลิกไม่ได้", and the primary button repeats the verb instead of saying ตกลง. Esc and the backdrop both cancel; cancelling changes nothing and leaves the selection intact.
  2. 18What will actually happen, stated before it happens — the rooms, the batch total, the due date that will be frozen, and the split between recipients who will get a LINE message and those who will not. The due date is the D-20 rule made visible: MAX(day-of-month 5, approval 8 ก.ย. + 3 days) = 11 ก.ย. 2026, so a late approval can never produce an unpayable window, and every bill in one batch shares one due date. The ไม่มี LINE line is the office's cue to print an ใบแจ้งค่าใช้จ่าย for room 211.
  3. 19อนุมัติและส่ง — POSTs to /api/bills/approve. The transaction flips all three bills Draft→Unpaid, freezes each one (readings, units, rates, area, tenant name, amounts, due date), writes one audit row and one queued notification row per bill, and returns in under five seconds (D-45 target 4). Delivery is a second round trip, so a LINE outage cannot roll back an approval. The button takes .is-loading on click and the dialog cannot be submitted twice; a duplicate request finds no Draft rows left and approves nothing.

1c. Alternate state — approval result and the LINE send loop (D-19)

app.juristic.local/bills?period=2026-09&batch=418

21

ตัวหารคือจำนวนรายการที่เข้าคิวส่งจริง ห้องที่ไม่มี LINE จะไม่ถูกนับ (แสดงในการ์ดด้านล่าง) · ระบบส่งครั้งละไม่เกิน 20 รายการต่อรอบ หากปิดหน้านี้ระหว่างส่ง รายการที่เหลือจะถูกส่งโดยงานประจำวันเวลา 09:00 น. โดยอัตโนมัติ

ห้องที่ยังไม่ได้รับข้อความ (1 ห้อง)
211 นายวรพล จันทร์เพ็ญ · ยังไม่ได้ผูก LINE ยังไม่ได้ส่ง — ไม่มี LINE พิมพ์ใบแจ้งหนี้ ดูบิล

ผูก LINE ให้ผู้เช่าได้ที่หน้า ผู้เช่า แล้วกด ส่งแจ้งเตือนอีกครั้ง ที่หน้ารายละเอียดบิล

22
  1. 20Result summary — the verbatim D-19 string "อนุมัติ {N} ใบ · ส่ง LINE สำเร็จ {x} · ยังไม่ได้ส่ง (ไม่มี LINE) {y} · ส่งไม่สำเร็จ {z}". It is a page, not a toast, because it must survive scrolling, printing and being read out to a colleague; there is no undo anywhere in this flow (D-39), so the summary is the record of what happened.
  2. 21The drain loop — the page calls POST /api/notifications/drain repeatedly, each call pushing at most 20 queued messages and returning running counts, so no request runs longer than about twenty seconds and a dropped connection loses nothing: the queued rows survive and the 09:00 daily job would finish them anyway (D-19, D-32). While running, the bar is .progress in brand colour with the label "กำลังส่ง {x}/{N}"; on completion it becomes .progress.ok. A 67-bill batch commits in ≤ 5 s and is fully delivered within ≤ 3 minutes (D-45).
  3. 22Unsent and failed rooms, listed and actionable — every room the tenant will not hear from is named, with the paper fallback one click away (D-28, D-30). A ส่งไม่สำเร็จ row would appear in the same list with the error and a ส่งแจ้งเตือนอีกครั้ง button instead. Approval is never blocked by delivery: these bills are already รอชำระ and already count in the period's ยอดเรียกเก็บ.

1d. Monthly Billing — states

StateWhat rendersExact Thai copyBehaviour and resulting state
Empty (period selected, no bills) .empty-state replacing the table; KPI tiles render 0.00; chips render 0; the unread-rooms banner still shows if rooms are unread "ยังไม่มีบิลในรอบนี้" + primary button "สร้างบิลฉบับร่าง" The button runs the same generation call as สร้าง/อัปเดตบิลฉบับร่าง. If no room in the period has both readings, generation answers "สร้างบิลใหม่ 0 ใบ · อัปเดต 0 ใบ · ข้าม (อนุมัติแล้ว) 0 ใบ · ยังไม่ได้จด 67 ห้อง" and the empty state stays.
Loading Eight .skeleton.row bars in place of the table body; KPI values render as short .skeleton bars; chips and toolbar stay interactive — Server-rendered page, so this is only visible on a slow first paint and while a chip filter re-queries. Any list page must paint in ≤ 2 s against the full 800-bill seed (D-45 target 2).
Error (generation or approval failed) .notice.bad above the toolbar, table untouched, selection preserved "บันทึกไม่สำเร็จ กรุณาลองอีกครั้ง" + button "ลองอีกครั้ง"; for a partial LINE failure "ส่งไม่สำเร็จ {z} ห้อง — บิลอนุมัติแล้วและยอดเงินถูกต้อง สามารถกด ส่งแจ้งเตือนอีกครั้ง ได้ที่หน้ารายละเอียดบิล" Never a redirect and never a lost selection (D-40). Money state and messaging state are independent: a failed send leaves the bills Unpaid and correct, and only the notification rows carry status='failed'.
Success Generation: .notice.ok flash (annotation 4). Approval: the result page 1c "สร้างบิลใหม่ {a} ใบ · อัปเดต {b} ใบ · ข้าม (อนุมัติแล้ว) {c} ใบ · ยังไม่ได้จด {d} ห้อง" · "อนุมัติ {N} ใบ · ส่ง LINE สำเร็จ {x} · ยังไม่ได้ส่ง (ไม่มี LINE) {y} · ส่งไม่สำเร็จ {z}" No toast and no undo for approval (D-39): an undo window would force outbound LINE messages into a queue. Toasts are reserved for low-stakes confirmations elsewhere in the product.
Permission denied (Staff opens /bills) Full-page Thai 403, no chrome from this page, nothing of the bill list in the response body "ไม่มีสิทธิ์เข้าถึงหน้านี้ · บัญชีของคุณเป็นเจ้าหน้าที่ กรุณาติดต่อนิติบุคคล" + button "ไปที่จดมิเตอร์" Never a silent redirect, so the permission matrix is testable row by row (D-40). The API answers 403 with the standard JSON error envelope, and no amount appears anywhere in a Staff response body (D-36).
Nothing selectable (all bills approved) Primary button [disabled]; select-all checkbox disabled Button "อนุมัติและส่งบิล" with hint "เลือกบิลฉบับร่างอย่างน้อย 1 ใบ" Not an error state: a fully approved period is the normal end state. The completeness line reads "อนุมัติแล้ว 64" and the ฉบับร่าง chip reads 0.

🔔 ค่าใช้จ่ายประจำเดือน กันยายน 2026 ห้อง 205 💧 ค่าน้ำ 10 หน่วย × 18 บาท = 180 บาท ⚡ ค่าไฟ 70 หน่วย × 8 บาท = 560 บาท 🏢 ค่าส่วนกลาง 30 ตร.ม. × 35 บาท = 1,050 บาท 💰 ยอดรวม 1,790 บาท 📅 กรุณาชำระภายในวันที่ 5 กันยายน 2026 ชำระเงินสดที่สำนักงานนิติบุคคล เวลา จันทร์-ศุกร์ 09:00-17:00 น.ตัวอย่าง
31

ข้อความจริงจะถูกส่งเมื่อกด อนุมัติและส่งบิล · ขณะนี้โหมดทดสอบเปิดอยู่ ข้อความจะไปที่บัญชีทดสอบของทีม

  1. 23พิมพ์ใบแจ้งหนี้ — disabled while the bill is Draft, because an unapproved amount must never leave the office on paper; the title says "พิมพ์ได้เมื่ออนุมัติบิลแล้ว". Once approved it opens the A5 ใบแจ้งค่าใช้จ่าย route — the receipt layout minus the receipt number, payment date and signature line — and is the hand-over path for a tenant with no LINE binding (D-28, D-30).
  2. 24อนุมัติและส่งบิล — the single approval gate for this bill (D-19). Visible only while the bill is Draft, Admin only, and it opens the D-39 dialog in frame 2b rather than acting on the click. There is no separate "approve the readings" step: reviewing the readings and approving the bill are the same act.
  3. 25Status stepper — ฉบับร่าง → อนุมัติและส่ง → รอชำระ → ชำระแล้ว, the PRD §20 flow made literal. A bill that ages past its due date shows step 3 as เกินกำหนด in --ui-overdue; nothing in the stepper is clickable, it is a read-out of state, and the only backwards move in the product is Admin's ตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่าง.
  4. 26Draft banner — states where editing actually happens. Readings are edited on Meter Reading, not here: this screen never contains an editable amount, in Draft or otherwise. Saving a corrected reading recalculates this Draft in place immediately (D-12, D-18), so the Admin can leave, fix a digit, come back and see the new total without pressing anything on this page.
  5. 27The calculation, in the PRD §10 order — previous reading, current reading, units, rate, amount, for all three items. All three bill items always exist, even at 0 units and 0.00 บาท, so the shape of this table never changes and the receipt always prints three lines (D-18, D-41). The common-area row has no meter, so its reading cells render "—" and its quantity is the room area. Each amount is ROUND(quantity × unit_price, 2) half-up, stored rounded (D-16).
  6. 28ยอดรวม 1,790.00 บาท — the sum of the three already-rounded items, never a second rounding, so the printed receipt always equals the sum of its printed lines (D-16). Two decimals here and in tables; LINE messages and sentence text write "1,790 บาท" and drop the trailing .00 (D-03).
  7. 29Bill metadata, including the rate snapshot — the rates shown are the ones copied onto this bill's items at generation, resolved against the first day of the period month, not against the moment the button was pressed (D-17). A later rate change in Settings can never move this figure. "กำหนดชำระ 5 ก.ย. 2026 (ยืนยันเมื่ออนุมัติ)" is the verbatim D-20 Draft label: the date is provisional until approval recomputes and freezes it.
  8. 30Tenant and LINE binding — a snapshot of the tenant name is frozen onto the bill at approval, so a later tenancy change never rewrites an issued bill or receipt (D-22). "การส่งบิลใบนี้" is the per-bill delivery state (D-30) and reads ยังไม่ส่ง while Draft. If this card showed ไม่มี LINE instead, approval would still be allowed and the office would print the paper bill.
  9. 31Thai LINE preview (.bubble.oa) — the exact BILL template of D-31 rendered with this bill's own values; plain text only, no Flex Message, no image, no rich menu in v1. The office reads the message before it is irreversible. The โหมดทดสอบ LINE: เปิด badge reflects the Settings switch that defaults to ON in every environment (D-34) and is turned off only for the rehearsed demo — without it on screen, a student would demo a send that quietly went to a test account. Where the previous reading came from a gap, the preview and the sent message both carry the extra line "หมายเหตุ: ยอดนี้ครอบคลุมการใช้ตั้งแต่รอบ {previous_period_th}" (D-14).

🔔 ค่าใช้จ่ายประจำเดือน กันยายน 2026 ห้อง 205 💧 ค่าน้ำ 10 หน่วย × 18 บาท = 180 บาท ⚡ ค่าไฟ 70 หน่วย × 8 บาท = 560 บาท 🏢 ค่าส่วนกลาง 30 ตร.ม. × 35 บาท = 1,050 บาท 💰 ยอดรวม 1,790 บาท 📅 กรุณาชำระภายในวันที่ 5 กันยายน 2026 ชำระเงินสดที่สำนักงานนิติบุคคล เวลา จันทร์-ศุกร์ 09:00-17:00 น.

  1. 32Dialog title and the D-39 sentence — the same shared component as the bulk dialog, with the room code in both the title and the verbatim body sentence "อนุมัติและส่งบิลห้อง 205 ผ่าน LINE — ยกเลิกไม่ได้". Behind it the page is dimmed and the trigger button holds .is-loading, so a second Enter press cannot reach it. Esc, the ยกเลิก button and the backdrop all cancel and change nothing.
  2. 33Recipient, amount, and the due date about to be frozen — three facts, because those are the three that cannot be taken back. The due date is recomputed once here by D-20's rule, MAX(5 ก.ย., 1 ก.ย. + 3 days) = 5 ก.ย. 2026, which is exactly the PRD §28 figure; on a live demo run the same expression correctly yields approval + 3 days, which is behaviour, not a defect (D-44).
  3. 34The message preview inside the dialog — the office confirms what the tenant will read, not an abstraction of it. This is the same rendered D-31 BILL template as the page behind, repeated at the point of no return; if the tenant had no LINE binding, this block would be replaced by "ผู้เช่ารายนี้ยังไม่ได้ผูก LINE — ระบบจะไม่ส่งข้อความ กรุณาพิมพ์ใบแจ้งหนี้ให้ผู้เช่า" and the approval would still be allowed (D-30).
  4. 35อนุมัติและส่ง — commits Draft→Unpaid, freezes readings, units, rates, area, tenant name, amounts and due date, writes the audit row and queues one notification row, then pushes the LINE message outside the transaction (D-19, D-22, D-30). Target: the tenant has the message within 30 seconds (D-45 target 3). On success the page reloads into state 2c; on a delivery failure the bill is still approved and the LINE card shows ส่งไม่สำเร็จ with ส่งแจ้งเตือนอีกครั้ง.

บิลนี้ชำระแล้ว ไม่มีข้อความแจ้งเตือนให้ส่ง 41
การแก้ไขบิล

บิลที่อนุมัติแล้วแก้ไขโดยตรงไม่ได้ ต้องคืนเงินสดที่เคาน์เตอร์ก่อน แล้วยกเลิกการชำระเงิน จากนั้นจึงตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่างเพื่อแก้ไขและอนุมัติใหม่

ยกเลิกการชำระเงินก่อนจึงจะตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่างได้ 42
  1. 36ดูใบเสร็จ — appears only once a payment exists, and opens the A5 receipt route. The receipt renders as the original while print_count = 0; the พิมพ์ใบเสร็จ button there POSTs to increment the counter before window.print(), so every later render carries the สำเนา mark and the button reads พิมพ์สำเนา (D-27). พิมพ์ใบแจ้งหนี้ stays available for any non-Draft bill.
  2. 37Approval and delivery timestamps, and the freeze — one line answers the two questions the office actually asks about an approved bill: was it sent, and can I still change it. Everything the amount depends on is a snapshot from the moment of approval and no endpoint can edit it (D-22). The Thai text names the frozen fields rather than saying "locked", so nobody has to guess what is included.
  3. 38The same calculation, now read-only (.amount-rows) — identical figures to the Draft view, rendered from the bill items' own snapshots of quantity and unit price. A rate change in Settings after this date does not touch it (D-17), which is what makes an old receipt reprintable years later and what makes the monthly report explainable against one figure.
  4. 39Payment history — one live payment per bill (D-24, D-26), full amount only, always cash. A voided payment is never deleted: it stays in this table with ยกเลิกแล้ว, its reason and its original receipt number, and a fresh payment adds a second row with a new number. That is what voiding rather than deleting buys — the sequence is unique and monotonic, but explicitly not gapless (D-05 #10, D-27).
  5. 40Audit timeline (.timeline, D-38) — literally SELECT * FROM audit_log WHERE bill_id = ? ORDER BY created_at, with reading edits made while the bill was Draft carrying the same bill_id. Admin-only, read-only, no cross-entity join, no global audit browser in v1. The two .is-key dots are the money events: approval and payment. Reverts appear here with their Thai reason.
  6. 41ส่งแจ้งเตือนอีกครั้ง — the Admin's uncapped manual resend (D-33), the tool of last resort on the day an automatic send failed. Disabled here because the bill is paid and there is nothing to chase; on an Unpaid or Overdue bill it is enabled and pushes the current PRE_DUE or OVERDUE template. Automatic reminders are shown as หยุดแล้ว: PRE_DUE at due − 2, OVERDUE at due + 1, + 8, + 15, nothing after day 15, nothing to a Paid bill, and nothing at all for a bill whose most recent payment was voided (D-25, D-33).
  7. 42The correction path (D-22) — a Paid bill is corrected across the counter, not in the database: the cash goes back to the tenant first, then ยกเลิกการชำระเงิน (Admin, required Thai reason of at least 10 characters, dialog D-39 #5 "คืนเงินสด 1,790.00 บาท ให้ผู้เช่าก่อนยืนยันการยกเลิก") voids the payment and the receipt — both kept, the receipt stamped ยกเลิก — and returns the bill to Unpaid. ตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่าง is therefore disabled until then, with the reason in its title. On an Unpaid or Overdue bill it is enabled instead, and is refused server-side when a later bill exists for the same room: "มีบิลรอบถัดไปแล้ว ไม่สามารถแก้ไขย้อนหลังได้ — กรุณาแก้ในรอบล่าสุด" (verbatim D-22), because the next bill's previous reading is a snapshot of the value being edited. After the fix the bill is re-approved and the tenant receives BILL_CORRECTED (D-31). The system never holds a refund, a credit or a balance (D-05 #4).

2d. Bill Detail — states

StateWhat rendersExact Thai copyBehaviour and resulting state
Empty (bill id does not exist, or the room has no bill in that period) Full-page 404 inside the app chrome; no partial bill is ever rendered "ไม่พบบิลที่ต้องการ" + button "กลับไปที่บิลประจำเดือน" A room with readings but no bill is not an empty Bill Detail — it has no detail route at all; the office reaches it through Monthly Billing's สร้าง/อัปเดตบิลฉบับร่าง instead (D-18, D-23).
Empty sub-state (approved bill, no payment yet) Payment history card keeps its heading and shows the shared empty component; ดูใบเสร็จ is absent, ตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่าง is enabled "ยังไม่มีการชำระเงิน" + button "ไปที่หน้ารับชำระเงิน" This is the normal Unpaid/Overdue state, not an error. The correction path here is the direct one: ตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่าง with a reason, no payment to void first (D-22).
Loading .skeleton bars inside the calculation and payment cards; the LINE preview card renders last — Server-rendered, so this is a first-paint state only. Actions during a request use .btn.is-loading with the button disabled — never a full-page spinner, never a lost page (D-40).
Error (approve, revert or void failed) .notice.bad at the top of the page, the dialog stays open with the typed reason preserved "บันทึกไม่สำเร็จ กรุณาลองอีกครั้ง" + "ลองอีกครั้ง"; revert refused for a stale bill: "มีบิลรอบถัดไปแล้ว ไม่สามารถแก้ไขย้อนหลังได้ — กรุณาแก้ในรอบล่าสุด" Nothing is half-applied: approval, revert, payment and void are each one transaction. A LINE failure is not a save failure — the bill stays approved and only the delivery badge turns ส่งไม่สำเร็จ (D-30).
Success Approve → the page reloads into state 2c with the ok notice and the sent timestamp; revert → back to state 2a with a notice naming the reason; void → status returns to รอชำระ / เกินกำหนด and the receipt row shows ยกเลิกแล้ว "อนุมัติและส่งบิลเรียบร้อย · ส่ง LINE สำเร็จ 1 ก.ย. 2026 14:32 น." · "ตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่างแล้ว · เหตุผล: {reason}" · "ยกเลิกการชำระเงินแล้ว · ใบเสร็จ REC-202609-000852 ถูกประทับว่า ยกเลิก" No undo and no toast-with-undo anywhere in this flow (D-39). A voided bill additionally shows ระงับการแจ้งเตือนอัตโนมัติ so the system never chases a tenant who paid and did nothing wrong (D-25).
Permission denied (Staff opens /bills/118) Full-page Thai 403; no amount, no rate and no tenant contact detail appears anywhere in the response body "ไม่มีสิทธิ์เข้าถึงหน้านี้ · บัญชีของคุณเป็นเจ้าหน้าที่ กรุณาติดต่อนิติบุคคล" + "ไปที่จดมิเตอร์" Money is absent from Staff responses, not merely hidden in the navigation (D-36). The audit timeline is Admin-only even within this page (D-38).

Assumptions recorded by these two screens
  • Fictional tenant names, phone numbers and LINE display names throughout; the demo seed carries the same shape (D-44). Room 205's tenant is สมชาย ใจดี in every document of this set, taken from the engineering spec's §2.7 seed row (tenants.id 37); อารยา ศรีสุวรรณ is room 202.
  • Bill id 118, payment and receipt id 96, room id 41 and tenant id 37 are the seeded fixture ids of engineering spec §3.1.10, not illustrative ones, and every frame below addresses them. Batch id 418 remains illustrative — the spec fixes no batch id. The seeded values that must reproduce are room code 205, the arithmetic 10/70/180/560/1,050/1,790 and the receipt number REC-202609-000852 (D-44).
  • All rooms are 30.00 ตร.ม., so ค่าส่วนกลาง is 1,050.00 on every row of the list; that is the seed, not a rendering shortcut, and it is why PRD §19's 35 ตร.ม. example row is not reproduced anywhere (D-44).
  • Office hours "จันทร์-ศุกร์ 09:00-17:00 น." and the apartment name come from Settings and are placeholders in the message template, never literals in code (D-31).
  • Twelve rows per page and a 200 ms search debounce are design choices, not decisions from the log; they may change without contradicting anything binding.
  • The Monthly Billing surface targets desktop Chrome and Edge at 1280 px and above and is not required to be usable below 768 px (D-45); the container query in the design system still degrades it to a single column with the bottom tab bar rather than breaking it.

Payment & Receipt

Three screens close the money loop of the product: the counter takes cash against exactly one Unpaid bill, the receipt is issued and printed on A5, and every settled payment stays findable and reprintable afterwards. Together they implement PRD §12, §13 and §18 functions 22–25, under the decisions that govern them — D-24 (full payment only, amount never an input), D-25 (void, never delete), D-26 (the database is the guarantee, not the button), D-27 (receipt fields and numbering), D-28 (A5 through the browser's own print dialog), D-39 (two of the six confirmation dialogs live here) and D-40 (empty, loading, error and 403 treatments).

The counter is Admin, not Staff

PRD §12 says เจ้าหน้าที่กด Confirm Cash Payment. That sentence contradicts PRD §4, which grants รับชำระเงิน and ออกใบเสร็จ to Admin only, and it is treated as a drafting error (D-36). Every screen in this section is Admin-only; a staff session reaching any of these routes gets the Thai 403 page, and no amount appears anywhere in a staff API response.

What the mockups are, and are not

All figures are the PRD §28 demo values (room 205, 30 ตร.ม., 10 หน่วย × 18, 70 หน่วย × 8, 30 × 35, total 1,790 THB, due 5 Sep 2026, paid 3 Sep 2026, receipt REC-202609-000852) and the D-44 seed identities. The calendar dates are documentation values: the live demo reproduces the arithmetic against dates computed from the run date, never this calendar (D-44). Tenant names, phone numbers and the apartment address are fictional.

10
ระบบจะออกใบเสร็จและส่งข้อความยืนยันผ่าน LINE ให้อัตโนมัติ
  1. 1Search field. A plain GET /payments?q=; Enter or ค้นหา submits. The server matches room_code by prefix and the active tenant's name by substring, and returns rooms of either status — a Vacant room can still hold an Unpaid bill left by a finished tenancy (D-07, D-08). One match renders the room directly; several render a chooser list; none renders the empty state (never a blank page, D-40). The query stays in the URL so the browser Back button returns the cashier to the same room.
  2. 2Room identity panel. Room code, floor, area and the room's current Active tenant. It is here so that a cashier who typed 250 instead of 205 reads the wrong tenant's name before money changes hands. Read-only; nothing on this panel is editable from the counter.
  3. 3LINE binding badge. ผูก LINE แล้ว or ไม่มี LINE, from the tenant's binding (D-29). Informational only: it predicts whether the ✅ confirmation message can be delivered after the payment (D-30). It never blocks or delays taking cash.
  4. 4Open bills list. Every bill of this room whose stored status is Unpaid, oldest period first. Draft bills never appear (they have not been approved and were never sent to the tenant) and Paid bills never appear. The status cell runs through the shared display helper, so a bill past its due date reads เกินกำหนด with data-status="Overdue" and no stored value changes (D-21).
  5. 5Bill selection. One radio, one bill — no multi-select, no "pay everything". Selecting a row applies tr.is-selected and rewrites the panel on the right; nothing is submitted yet. Every row shows its billing period because a room in arrears has several open bills, and paying the wrong month is precisely the mistake the void path exists to undo (D-25, D-39 #3).
  6. 6Frozen breakdown. Units, unit prices and line amounts are snapshots taken at Approve & Send and are not recomputed at the counter; no field here is editable through any endpoint (D-22). The three lines are always present, even at zero units, so the panel always sums to the total (D-41).
  7. 7ยอดที่ต้องชำระ — text, not an input. The stored payment amount is fixed at the bill total. Partial payment and overpayment do not exist in v1: a tenant who is short returns with the full amount and the bill stays รอชำระ / เกินกำหนด (D-24, D-05 #2). This is what lets Paid always mean "paid in full" without a fifth status.
  8. 8รับเงิน / เงินทอน cashier aid. Browser-side only (public/js/payment-change.js), recomputed on every keystroke. Non-numeric characters are ignored; a value below the total hides the change figure and shows รับเงินน้อยกว่ายอดที่ต้องชำระ as a hint but never disables the confirm button, because a cashier may skip the aid entirely. Neither value is submitted, stored, printed on the receipt or sent to LINE (D-24).
  9. 9ผู้รับเงิน and timestamp. Server-supplied and read-only (dashed border = system value): the signed-in Admin's ชื่อที่แสดง (D-46) and the server clock in Asia/Bangkok, 24-hour with the น. suffix (D-03). They are displayed because both are printed on the receipt.
  10. 10ยืนยันรับเงินสด — the one page action. Disabled while no bill is selected. It opens the confirmation dialog (marker 12) rather than posting immediately. On submit it takes .is-loading and disabled — a courtesy, never the guarantee: the guarantee is UNIQUE(active_bill_id) plus a conditional status update in the database (D-26). Resulting state on success: one payments row, bill Unpaid → Paid, one receipt number allocated, one LINE message queued, one audit row — one transaction, then a redirect to the receipt.
  11. 11Recent payments. The room's last settled bills with their receipt numbers, each linking to /receipts/:id. It answers "เดือนที่แล้วจ่ายหรือยัง" at the counter without leaving the screen, and it is the reprint path for a tenant who lost last month's slip.

ยืนยันรับเงินสด

13ยืนยันรับเงินสด 1,790 บาท · ห้อง 205 · รอบ กันยายน 2026 และออกใบเสร็จ

ระบบจะเปลี่ยนสถานะบิลเป็น ชำระแล้ว ออกเลขที่ใบเสร็จ และส่งข้อความยืนยันผ่าน LINE

15 14
  1. 12The shared dialog component. One .modal + .dialog partial, six call sites in the product, never the browser's native confirm() (D-39). It is the last stop before an irreversible effect: the amount is committed, and the LINE message cannot be recalled.
  2. 13Amount, room and period, verbatim. D-39 #3 requires all three. The period is in the sentence because a room in arrears has several open bills; a cashier reading "รอบ กันยายน 2026" here is the cheapest defence against the mistake that D-25's void exists to repair. Money in sentence text is written 1,790 บาท — thousands separator, no trailing .00, unit after the number (D-03).
  3. 14Primary button repeats the verb. ยืนยันรับเงินสด, never ตกลง. One press posts once; the button then carries disabled and a spinner. A second press that still reaches the server finds UPDATE bills SET status='Paid' WHERE id=? AND status='Unpaid' matching zero rows and returns 409 บิลนี้ถูกชำระไปแล้ว (D-26) — one payment, one receipt number, always.
  4. 15Cancel is free. ยกเลิก and the Escape key both close the dialog and return to the page with the selected bill and the typed รับเงิน value intact. Nothing was written, so nothing has to be undone — and there is deliberately no toast-with-undo anywhere in this product, because an undo window would force outbound LINE messages into a delay queue (D-39).

4 ยืนยันการชำระผ่าน LINEส่งแล้ว
ส่งเมื่อ 3 ก.ย. 2026 · 14:30 น. ถึง สมชาย ใจดี
5
6 พิมพ์แล้ว 0 ครั้ง · พิมพ์ครั้งต่อไปจะมีคำว่า สำเนา
9
8
ใช้เมื่อรับเงินผิดห้องหรือผิดรอบเท่านั้น
  1. 1Success banner. Rendered once after the redirect from the payment POST and carries the three things the cashier must read back to the tenant: room, period and receipt number. A full result screen rather than a toast, because money moved and a LINE message was queued (D-40).
  2. 2Receipt number. REC-<payment month YYYYMM>-<6-digit sequence>, allocated inside the payment transaction from one counter that never resets (D-27). It is unique and monotonic but not gapless — a rolled-back transaction consumes a number, and the seed starts at an offset to reproduce 000852 — so no screen, document or test may claim gaplessness (D-05 #10).
  3. 3Payment facts. Amount, method (เงินสด — the only method that exists in v1), the Admin who took it and the server timestamp. The bill's badge is ชำระแล้ว with data-status="Paid"; because the payment amount always equals the bill total, Paid needs no qualifier (D-24).
  4. 4LINE delivery state. Read from this bill's notifications row: ส่งแล้ว (ok), ยังไม่ได้ส่ง — ไม่มี LINE (neutral), ส่งไม่สำเร็จ (bad, error text on hover). In the latter two a ส่งข้อความอีกครั้ง button appears; the manual resend is never capped, unlike the three automatic attempts (D-30). Delivery state never affects the money state.
  5. 5พิมพ์ใบเสร็จ. POSTs /api/receipts/:id/print to increment print_count, then calls window.print(); the browser's own dialog handles paper and printer (D-28). After the first press the label becomes พิมพ์สำเนา and every later render carries the สำเนา mark. Printing from the browser's menu bypasses the counter and yields an unmarked copy — a listed limitation, not a defect (D-05 #11).
  6. 6Print counter. The only thing that decides whether the sheet coming out is an original or a copy. It is shown in plain words so the office can see the state before pressing, and it is why the copy mark is testable at all (D-27).
  7. 7A5 preview. The same markup the print route renders — what is on screen is what comes out of the printer. On screen the sheet is shown at its natural width without the full 200 mm page height; the print output is the complete A5 page shown below (D-28).
  8. 8ยกเลิกการชำระเงิน. Admin only, styled destructive and placed away from the print button. Opens dialog #5 of D-39, which insists the cash goes back across the counter first. This is the only correction path for a payment: there is no edit, no refund, no credit and no balance inside the system (D-25, D-05 #4).
  9. 9กลับไปหน้ารับชำระเงิน. Returns to /payments with the room still in the query, so the next tenant in the queue is one search away. The counter's normal loop is search → confirm → print → back.

Alternate state — Void payment dialog (D-39 #5)
