💵 รับชำระเงิน 🧾 บิลประจำเดือน 12 📈 รายงาน
ใบเสร็จ
REC-202609-000852 · ห้อง 205 · รอบ กันยายน 2026 ชำระแล้ว
รับเงิน 1,790.00 บาท · เงินสด · 3 ก.ย. 2026 14:30 น. · ผู้รับเงิน นิติบุคคล
ยกเลิกการชำระเงิน

10คืนเงินสด 1,790 บาท ให้ผู้เช่าก่อนยืนยันการยกเลิก

ใบเสร็จเลขที่ REC-202609-000852 จะถูกประทับว่า ยกเลิก และบิลห้อง 205 รอบ กันยายน 2026 จะกลับเป็นสถานะ รอชำระ

12กรอกเหตุผลอย่างน้อย 10 ตัวอักษรก่อน
  1. 10Cash first, click second. The dialog's first line is an instruction, not a description: the money is handed back across the counter before anything is confirmed, because the system holds no refund, credit or balance (D-25, D-39 #5). The sentence names the exact amount.
  2. 11Reason, required, minimum 10 characters. Validated on input; the field shows .field.invalid and the Thai error until it passes. The text is stored on the payment row (void_reason) and copied into the audit row with before/after JSON (D-38), so the reconciliation question "why is receipt 852 void?" has an answer six months later.
  3. 12Destructive primary, disabled until valid. On confirm, in one transaction: the payment row is marked voided (never deleted), the receipt row is marked voided and keeps its number, the bill returns to Unpaid — displaying as เกินกำหนด automatically if the due date has passed (D-21) — and an audit row is written. No LINE message is sent on the void; the office explains in person. The bill is then excluded from automatic reminders and carries the badge ระงับการแจ้งเตือนอัตโนมัติ, so the system never chases a tenant who paid and did nothing wrong (D-25).

Print layout — ใบเสร็จรับเงิน, A5 portrait

One route serves both the screen view above and the paper: window.print() with @page { size: A5 portrait; margin: 10mm } and a print stylesheet that hides the app chrome. No PDF library, no server-side rendering, no thermal-printer layout, no printer-driver assumptions (D-28). Fitting on exactly one A5 page with all three line items, the total and the number is a QA check.

18
ลงชื่อผู้รับเงิน · นิติบุคคล
  1. 13Letterhead — the apartment, never the software. Name, address and phone come from Settings; เลขประจำตัวผู้เสียภาษี is the one optional line and renders only when Settings holds one — settings.tax_id is seeded NULL (engineering spec §2.7), so the default sheet above has no such line at all and the populated variant is drawn once, at annotation 19. The product name Juristic Platform never appears here, because the receipt is issued by the juristic person, not by the software (D-01, D-27).
  2. 14Title — a non-tax receipt. ใบเสร็จรับเงิน only: no VAT, no VAT breakdown, no ใบกำกับภาษี, and no toggle that could produce one (D-27, D-05 #6). The same layout titled ใบแจ้งค่าใช้จ่าย, with the receipt number, payment date, ผู้รับเงิน and signature line removed and กำหนดชำระ added, is the paper bill printed from Bill Detail (D-28).
  3. 15Meta block — D-27 blocks 3 to 7, in that order. เลขที่ · วันที่ชำระ · ห้อง · ชื่อผู้เช่า · รอบบิล, and nothing else. ชำระโดย and ผู้รับเงิน are blocks 12 and 13 and print below ยอดรวม and the amount in words, not here — the order is fixed by D-27 and read top to bottom by the P0 case TC-PAY-023, so moving either back into this block is a defect. The tenant name is a snapshot taken at generation, so a later tenancy change never rewrites a printed receipt. Dates use the full Thai month and Gregorian years (D-03); ผู้รับเงิน is the Admin's ชื่อที่แสดง (D-46).
  4. 16Always exactly three line items. Water, electricity and common area, each with quantity, unit price and amount — printed even at zero units and 0.00 THB, so the sheet always reconciles and the layout never shifts (D-41). Quantities carry their unit (หน่วย, ตร.ม.), money columns are right-aligned tabular figures.
  5. 17Total and amount in words. The total is the sum of the already-rounded line amounts, so the printed total always equals the sum of the printed lines — there is no second rounding (D-16). The words come from one shared helper with both forms: …บาทถ้วน for an integer total and …บาท…สตางค์ when satang exist, which they can because a rate may be 7.50 (D-27).
  6. 18Signature line. ลงชื่อผู้รับเงิน with the Admin's display name printed beneath the rule; the tenant keeps the sheet as proof. This block, the receipt number and the payment date are exactly what the ใบแจ้งค่าใช้จ่าย variant drops (D-28).

  1. 19The optional tax-id line, and the only frame in this document that draws it. settings.tax_id is seeded NULL (engineering spec §2.7), so every other receipt, invoice and Settings preview above is drawn without เลขประจำตัวผู้เสียภาษี — that is the default sheet, and the line is absent, not blank. This frame is the same route after an Admin saves a 13-digit id in Settings; the value shown, 0105512345678, is the one the test plan sets in TC-PAY-023 and clears again. Everything else on the sheet is byte-identical, so the tax id is the only difference an examiner has to look for (D-01, D-27).

Left: every render at print_count ≥ 1 carries the grey สำเนา mark and the button reads พิมพ์สำเนา (D-27). Right: a voided receipt keeps its number and prints stamped ยกเลิก in red, on screen and on paper, for as long as the record exists (D-25). Both variants are the same route and the same markup — only the stamp element differs. That is a rule, not a description: every field of the original is present on both, including the four-column table with its ราคา/หน่วย column and the ชำระโดย / ผู้รับเงิน pair beneath the total. Neither carries a tax-ID letterhead line, because the seed leaves settings.tax_id NULL; the third sheet is that same markup with an id saved. D-09 guarantees that a reprint shows the billed data — including the rates — identically forever, and the rate is precisely the number a tenant disputing a copy is asking about, so a reprint that dropped it would be the one document in the product that cannot answer its own question.

9 123 แสดง 1–25 จาก 63 รายการ

The mockup shows the first six rows of page 1; the real page renders 25.

  1. 1Two tabs, one page. History is the second tab of Payment at /payments/history, not a new sidebar entry and not a fifth report — D-42 fixes the report set at four. It satisfies PRD §18 function 25 (ดูประวัติการชำระ) where the cashier already is.
  2. 2Month picker — filters by payment date. ?period=YYYY-MM over payments.paid_at, not over the billing period: a September payment settling an August bill (row 6 in the table) belongs to September here. This is deliberately different from the Dashboard's รับชำระแล้ว tile, which is scoped by billing period (D-41); the two figures are not expected to match and neither is wrong. The รอบบิล column makes the difference visible on every row.
  3. 3Search. Room number or receipt number, matched exactly enough that pasting REC-202609-000852 from a tenant's photo finds the row. Combines with the month filter; clearing it restores the full month.
  4. 4Status chips with live counts. ทั้งหมด / ปกติ / ยกเลิกแล้ว. Voided payments are visible by default and never hidden away, because reconciling the cash drawer against a receipt number that no longer represents money is exactly what the office does at the end of the day (D-25).
  5. 5Total line. The sum of non-voided payments in the current filter, shown next to the row count so a wrong filter is obvious. Voided rows are excluded from the sum but counted in the chip.
  6. 6Columns. วันที่ชำระ carries the time (24-hour with น.), because two payments on one day are ordered by it; ผู้รับเงิน is the Admin's ชื่อที่แสดง from payments.received_by_user_id and stays correct even after that user is set Inactive (D-46). Money is right-aligned tabular.
  7. 7Voided row. Badge ยกเลิกแล้ว with the reason beneath it. The row is never deleted and keeps its receipt number — that, and not gaplessness, is what voiding rather than deleting buys (D-25, D-27). Its bill has returned to รอชำระ and appears again on the Payment screen.
  8. 8Row actions. ดูใบเสร็จ opens /receipts/:id. พิมพ์สำเนา is the same button as on the receipt screen — it increments print_count and prints a sheet marked สำเนา (or ยกเลิก for a voided one), so a reprint requested at the counter never produces something indistinguishable from the original (D-27).
  9. 9Pagination. Server-side, 50 rows per page, newest payment first; the page info states the range and the total so a month with 63 payments is legible at a glance. No CSV export here — the four reports are the export surface (D-42).

Assumptions recorded by these screens

  1. Payment history lives at /payments/history as the Payment page's second tab. PRD §18 function 25 requires it, the site map's fifteen routes do not name it, and D-42 fixes the report set at four. Making it a sub-route of Payment adds no navigation entry, no export surface and no new permission row.
  2. Payment search matches a room code by prefix and a tenant name by substring, across rooms of both statuses. A Vacant room can still hold an Unpaid bill from a finished tenancy (D-07, D-08), so restricting the search to Occupied rooms would hide money owed.
  3. History is filtered by payment date, the Dashboard tile by billing period. Both are correct and they will differ whenever a bill is settled in a later month; the รอบบิล column exists so the difference is visible rather than mysterious (D-41).
  4. If POST /api/receipts/:id/print fails, printing proceeds anyway with a warning. D-27 fixes the order (increment, then print) but not the failure path. The tenant is at the counter; blocking the print would be worse than an unmarked sheet, which is already an accepted outcome when printing from the browser menu (D-05 #11).
  5. The รับเงิน cashier aid never blocks the confirm button. A value below the total shows a hint only. The aid is optional scratch arithmetic, and D-24 states plainly that neither value reaches the API.
  6. Receipt routes are keyed by the receipt's row id, not its number (/receipts/96 renders REC-202609-000852). The number is a document identifier that must survive voiding; the id is the route key.
  7. The receipt preview on the view screen is shown at natural width without the full 200 mm page height. The printed sheet is the A5 page shown in the print frame; the preview is a legibility aid, not a page-fidelity proof — that is a QA check on the real print output (D-28).

Announcements, Reports & Settings

Three Admin-only surfaces that sit outside the billing spine: the one place the office speaks to every tenant at once (Announcements), the four reports the office opens when someone disputes a number (Reports), and the two pages that decide what every future bill will cost and who may press the buttons (Settings and the Users page it links to). All four screens are desktop-only: D-45 commits the phone surface to Login, จดมิเตอร์ and the reduced ภาพรวม, and D-36 keeps every screen in this section out of the Staff role entirely — so there is no 390 px variant to design, and the only phone-shaped artefact here is the LINE bubble preview, which is a picture of the tenant's phone, not a page of ours.

Decisions this section renders, and the four assumptions it adds

Rendered verbatim: D-35 (audience is two options only — ทุกห้องที่มีผู้เช่า or เลือกชั้น; live preview; live room + linked count; immutable history), D-31 §6 (the ANNOUNCEMENT template), D-34 (multicast for announcements, the 500-message line, LINE test mode default ON), D-39 §4 (the one confirmation dialog on this section's only irreversible action), D-42 (exactly four reports, each with a CSV export), D-41 (money figures read approved bills), D-21 (Overdue is derived, never stored), D-17 (insert-only effective-dated rates plus the Draft-recalculation notice), D-20 (due day 1–28, and the MAX(day, approval + 3) rule), D-33 (the reminder schedule), D-32 (the manual job button and its result line), D-46 (the Users field list, Inactive instead of delete, ตั้งรหัสผ่านใหม่ as the only recovery path), D-40 (403, empty, failed-save, loading), D-03 (formats), D-44 (every figure below reconciles against the seed).

Assumption A-D1 — routes. The ADR fixes page routes as lowercase, plural, hyphenated and puts reports/*.ejs in its own view folder, but names no report path. This section uses /announcements, /reports/monthly, /reports/arrears, /reports/top-usage, /reports/room-history, /settings, /users, with /reports redirecting to /reports/monthly. Each CSV is the same path plus .csv, so the export button is an ordinary link and needs no eighth JSON endpoint.

Assumption A-D2 — export filenames. D-42 fixes report-{name}-{period}.csv. Two reports are not single-period: the monthly summary writes the range as report-monthly-2026-04_2026-09.csv, and the per-room history names the room, report-room-history-205-2026-09.csv. Both keep the prefix, the separator and the extension the helper already produces.

Assumption A-D3 — reminder days are shown, not edited. The brief asks for reminder days in Settings; D-33 fixes them as constants (due − 2, then due + 1, 8, 15, all at 09:00 Asia/Bangkok). Settings therefore renders the schedule read-only next to the manual trigger of D-32. Making it editable would create a setting no test asserts and a fifth message nobody decided to send.

Assumption A-D4 — no receipt-footer field. The brief asks for a receipt footer; the settings table has no such column and D-27 fixes the receipt's fields exactly. Settings instead renders a live letterhead preview of the four fields that do print (name, address, optional tax ID, phone), so the Admin can see the effect of an edit without a free-text field that would let a VAT line back onto a non-tax receipt.

One deviation from the brief, on purpose: the brief lists an audience of “all rooms / selected rooms / floor”. D-35 allows exactly two options and forbids per-room selection. The decision log wins.

Announcements — ประกาศ (/announcements)

Role: Admin only (D-36); Staff receives the Thai 403 page Route: GET/POST /announcements · live count GET /api/announcements/preview-count Entry points: sidebar 📢 ประกาศ · Dashboard has no shortcut — an announcement is never an accident Primary action: ส่งประกาศ (one confirmation dialog, then multicast)

One page, three jobs: compose, see exactly what 54 phones will show, and read the delivery record of everything sent before. The form is deliberately narrow — 60 characters of title, 400 of body, two audience options — because every field on this page is a field that cannot be taken back once the button is pressed.

ศ ศุภาลัย เรสซิเดนซ์
📢 แจ้งปิดน้ำชั่วคราว ชั้น 3 นิติบุคคลจะปิดน้ำชั่วคราวเพื่อล้างถังเก็บน้ำของชั้น 3 วันพฤหัสบดีที่ 10 กันยายน 2026 เวลา 09:00–15:00 น. ขออภัยในความไม่สะดวก กรุณาสำรองน้ำใช้ล่วงหน้า หากมีข้อสงสัยติดต่อสำนักงานนิติบุคคล 021234567 — นิติบุคคล ศุภาลัย เรสซิเดนซ์ตัวอย่าง

ข้อความธรรมดาเท่านั้น ไม่มีปุ่มและไม่มีรูปภาพ (D-31)

ประกาศที่ส่งแล้วประกาศที่ส่งแล้วแก้ไข ส่งซ้ำ หรือเรียกคืนไม่ได้
8หัวข้อ กลุ่มผู้รับส่งเมื่อห้อง 9ผลการส่ง
แจ้งกำหนดชำระค่าน้ำค่าไฟ รอบกันยายน 2026 ทุกห้องที่มีผู้เช่า1 ก.ย. 2026 09:20 น.67 ส่งแล้ว 54 ไม่มี LINE 13 ดู
แจ้งล้างถังเก็บน้ำ 25 ส.ค. 2026 ทุกห้องที่มีผู้เช่า22 ส.ค. 2026 10:05 น.67 ส่งแล้ว 53 ไม่มี LINE 13 ส่งไม่สำเร็จ 1 ดู
แจ้งซ่อมลิฟต์ ชั้น 4–5 ชั้น 4, 512 ส.ค. 2026 16:40 น.27 ส่งแล้ว 22 ไม่มี LINE 5 ดู
แจ้งเปลี่ยนเวลาทำการสำนักงาน ทุกห้องที่มีผู้เช่า3 ส.ค. 2026 09:00 น.67 ส่งแล้ว 54 ไม่มี LINE 13 ดู
‹12›แสดง 1–4 จาก 9 ประกาศ
  1. 1หัวข้อ · title VARCHAR(60). Required, hard-capped at 60 characters; the counter turns --ui-warning at 55 and the field goes .field.invalid at 61 with the input refusing further keystrokes. Every keystroke re-renders marker 7 and nothing else — the title is not part of the recipient query, so it never re-fires the count request.
  2. 2เนื้อหา · body VARCHAR(400). Required, 400 characters, newlines preserved (white-space: pre-line in the bubble, so what is typed is what is sent). 400 + 60 + the template's fixed characters stays far under LINE's 5,000-character message limit, which is why no truncation logic exists anywhere in the send path.
  3. 3กลุ่มผู้รับ — exactly two options (D-35). ทุกห้องที่มีผู้เช่า is the default and resolves to rooms.status = 'Occupied'; เลือกชั้น reveals marker 4. There is no per-room picker and no “ห้องที่ค้างชำระ” audience — arrears are chased per bill by the D-33 job, never by a group message that names debtors on a shared floor.
  4. 4Floor multi-select over rooms.floor (D-47). Chips are toggles; at least one must be on, and the count on each chip is that floor's occupied-room count, so the Admin can see before choosing that ชั้น 5 is a smaller send. Selecting a floor writes audience='floors' and the comma-separated audience_floors. Clearing every chip disables the primary button and shows the field error.
  5. 5Live recipient count. Debounced GET /api/announcements/preview-count on every audience change, rendering D-35's sentence verbatim. The second number is the load-bearing one: 14 rooms will be told, 11 phones will ring. While the request is in flight the notice keeps the previous numbers and shows an inline .spinner — it never blanks, because a blank count next to an irreversible button is worse than a stale one.
  6. 6ส่งประกาศ. Disabled until title, body and a non-empty audience all validate. Opens D-39's fourth dialog (second frame below). On confirm: INSERT the announcements row, INSERT one notifications row per recipient tenant, commit, and only then multicast in chunks of 500 (D-34) — one API call for this send. Tenants with no line_user_id get a skipped_no_line row and no call. The button then goes .btn.is-loading and the page redirects to the history table with the new row on top.
  7. 7Live preview, rendered through the real D-31 ANNOUNCEMENT template. The preview calls the same lib/templates.js function the sender calls, so a template edit cannot make the preview lie. The signature line — นิติบุคคล ศุภาลัย เรสซิเดนซ์ is appended from Settings and is not editable here (D-01).
  8. 8History — immutable (D-35). Newest first, 50 per page. There is no edit, resend or recall control anywhere in this table, because LINE offers no recall and a resend would double every recipient's notification row. ดู opens a read-only view of the sent text.
  9. 9ผลการส่ง, counted from notifications, one row per recipient (D-30). The three badges always sum to the ห้อง column — that is the report's own consistency check. ส่งไม่สำเร็จ carries the LINE error on hover. The word “blocked” appears nowhere: LINE returns 200 for a blocked recipient, so a message the tenant never saw is counted here as sent, and that gap is limitation 9 of D-05.

ส่งประกาศถึง 14 ห้อง ผ่าน LINE — เรียกคืนไม่ได้

ผู้เช่าที่ผูก LINE แล้ว 11 ราย · อีก 3 ห้องจะไม่ได้รับ

3
  1. 1Shared confirm-dialog.ejs, never window.confirm(). One component, six call sites (D-39); this is call site four. Escape and the backdrop both cancel; focus is trapped and starts on ยกเลิก, so a stray Enter cannot send.
  2. 2The sentence is D-39's, verbatim, with the live count substituted. The count comes from the same response as marker 5 above, re-fetched when the dialog opens — if a tenant linked LINE in the meantime, the number the Admin confirms is the number that will actually be pushed.
  3. 3The primary button repeats the verb (ส่งประกาศ), never ตกลง. On confirm the button goes .is-loading, the dialog stays open until the transaction commits, and only then does the page redirect. A double-click is absorbed by the disabled state and, behind it, by uq_notifications(dedupe_key) — the same key that stops a bill message going out twice in one day (ENG-3).

Reports — รายงาน (/reports)

Role: Admin only (D-42); Staff receives the Thai 403 page Routes: /reports/monthly · /reports/arrears · /reports/top-usage · /reports/room-history (A-D1) Entry points: sidebar 📈 รายงาน · Dashboard tiles deep-link into the matching tab · Bill Detail links to ประวัติรายห้อง Primary action: ส่งออก CSV — the page is read-only; nothing on it changes a bill

Four reports, four tabs, one filter bar. D-42 fixes the set exactly: no report builder, no column chooser, no PDF. Every figure below reconciles against the D-44 seed — 67 occupied rooms of 30 ตร.ม., water 18.00, electricity 8.00, common 35.00 — and the September column is the state of the demo after room 205 has paid: 55 rooms settled for 98,420.00 and 12 rooms overdue for 23,146.00, which sum to the 121,566.00 billed.

8การตรวจสอบในตัวทุกแถว: ค่าน้ำ + ค่าไฟ + ค่าส่วนกลาง = เรียกเก็บ และ รับชำระ + ค้างชำระ = เรียกเก็บ · แถวรวมต้องเท่ากับผลบวกของทุกงวด
  1. 1Four tabs, four routes. Each is a real page load, not a client-side switch, so a report is bookmarkable and the browser Back button behaves. The active tab is derived from the path, never from JavaScript state. /reports alone redirects to /reports/monthly.
  2. 2Month-range filter. Two <select> elements over the periods that actually have bills — never a free date picker, because a period is a CHAR(7) key, not a date (ADR §6.7). The range is inclusive, capped at 12 periods to keep the query and the chart bounded, and it is carried in the query string so the CSV link inherits it unchanged.
  3. 3ส่งออก CSV. A plain link to /reports/monthly.csv with the same query string — no eighth JSON endpoint (ADR §6.2). One shared helper writes UTF-8 with BOM so Excel opens Thai correctly, and the filename is report-monthly-2026-04_2026-09.csv (A-D2). The export contains exactly the rows on screen, including the totals row.
  4. 4KPI tiles are the table's totals row, promoted. Money is summed over bills whose stored status is Unpaid or Paid — Draft bills are excluded, and reading the stored column here is explicitly not a D-21 violation because the tile means “approved”, not “what a human sees”. The three money tiles reconcile on screen by construction: 730,590.00 + 23,146.00 = 753,736.00.
  5. 5Chart. In the running app this is Chart.js from cdnjs, fed by the same query as the table; the inline SVG here is the static stand-in for this document. Two series, not a stacked bar, because the question the office asks is “how big is the gap”, and a stack hides exactly that. September is the only period with a gap, and the dashed rule labels it with the same 23,146.00 the tile shows.
  6. 6The three-way split is always present. D-41 guarantees every generated bill carries exactly three bill_items, even at 0 units and 0.00 — enforced by uq_bill_items(bill_id, item_type) (ENG-2) — so these columns are never null and never need a COALESCE. A period whose split does not sum to its billed column is a defect with its own assertion, not a rounding artefact: amounts are rounded once, in SQL, at item level (ADR §6.4).
  7. 7Bill counts use the display status (D-21). This column is read by a human, so it goes through the shared helper: Unpaid AND due_date < CURDATE() renders เกินกำหนด. On 8 ก.ย. 2026 every unsettled September bill is past its 5 ก.ย. due date, so รอชำระ is absent from this table — that is correct, not a missing badge.
  8. 8The consistency notice is a feature, not decoration. It states the two identities the test plan asserts on this report, so an examiner can check the arithmetic on screen without opening the database.

รวม 12 รายการ23,146.00

5ผลรวมของทั้ง 4 ช่วงอายุหนี้ต้องเท่ากับ 12 รายการ และ 23,146.00 บาทเสมอ

  1. 1Scope banner. This is the one report that ignores the month filter — D-42 defines it as every Unpaid bill across every period — so the page says so before the reader assumes otherwise. The as-of stamp matters because the ageing is computed against CURDATE() and changes at midnight with no job (D-21).
  2. 2Four non-overlapping ageing buckets. 1–7 / 8–30 / 31–60 / 61+ วัน, computed from due_date, never stored. Against the D-44 seed — history fully paid, only the current period outstanding — every row lands in the first bucket and the other three read zero. That is the honest rendering of the seed, and it is precisely why the three empty tiles use .kpi.neutral rather than a red variant: an empty ageing bucket is good news. The other buckets are exercised by the test plan's fixtures, not by the demo data.
  3. 3Phone number is on the report on purpose. This is the sheet the office prints and works down; without the number the operator opens twelve tenant records to make twelve calls. Note that 13 of the 67 tenants have no LINE binding, so the phone column is the only channel that reaches every debtor.
  4. 4เลยกำหนด is derived, and this report ages the stored column. D-42 ages every Unpaid bill; the badge next to it goes through the D-21 display helper. The two agree here because all twelve are past due — a bill that is Unpaid but not yet due would carry รอชำระ and no bucket, and is excluded from the row count by the due_date < CURDATE() predicate.
  5. 5The stated invariant is the test. Bucket counts sum to the row count; bucket amounts sum to the report total. One assertion each in the test plan, and both are visible on screen.

4ทำไมไม่มีอันดับตามยอดเงินอัตราเดียวกันทุกห้องและทุกห้อง 30 ตร.ม. เท่ากัน อันดับตามหน่วยกับอันดับตามยอดเงินจึงเป็นอันดับเดียวกัน · ค่าส่วนกลางไม่เข้ารายงานนี้เพราะไม่ได้ขึ้นกับการใช้งาน
  1. 1Single-period picker, not the range. D-42 defines this report as top 10 for the selected period; the arrows step one month and reload. The period is in the query string so the CSV link and a bookmark carry it.
  2. 2งวดก่อน is each room's own previous period, not the building average. That is the comparison that answers the only question this report is opened to answer — “is 38 units normal for this room?”. A room with no previous reading (baseline month, D-06) renders an em dash and no delta, never a fake +0.
  3. 3The abnormal-usage flag comes from the reading screen, not from this report. Room 312 at 240 units against 68 crossed D-13's 3× threshold with its ≥ 10-unit floor when the reading was saved; this report renders the same badge so the two screens cannot disagree. The flag is advisory — the bill was still generated and approved at the recorded units.
  4. 4Deliberate omissions, stated on the page. No baht ranking (identical rates and identical 30 ตร.ม. areas make it the same ranking) and no common-fee column (it is area × rate and moves only when a room's area is edited, which D-38 audits separately). Saying so on screen prevents the “can you add…” request that would grow a fifth report D-42 does not allow.

4เฉลี่ยต่อเดือน · น้ำ 10.0 หน่วย · ไฟ 70.0 หน่วย · ยอดรวม 1,790.00 บาท

  1. 1Room picker, one room at a time. Searchable select over all 80 rooms showing code and current tenant. Bill Detail and the Rooms page both deep-link here with ?room=, because this is the screen the office opens when a tenant disputes a reading — the fastest path to it must not be a menu hunt.
  2. 2Units and money side by side, twelve rows, no chart. A dispute is settled by reading a number aloud, not by pointing at a line. The 12-period window is fixed by D-42; the month-range filter does not apply to this tab.
  3. 3Receipt numbers are shown because the tenant is holding one. Monotonic, never gapless (D-27) — the jumps between 000160 and 000852 are other rooms' receipts, not lost documents, and no copy on this page may claim the sequence is continuous. A voided receipt would render with a strikethrough and its replacement number beside it.
  4. 4Averages, not projections. Twelve rows of arithmetic and nothing more — no trend line, no forecast, no anomaly score. Anything cleverer here would be the AI/ML that PRD §24 puts out of scope. Note the totals row reconciles with the twelve row totals exactly, because each row's total is the stored bill total and the column is a plain SUM.

Alternate state — Staff opening a report (D-40)

app.juristic.local/reports/monthly
Juristic Platformเจ้าหน้าที่ · staff
1
🔒
ไม่มีสิทธิ์เข้าถึงหน้านี้

บัญชีของคุณเป็นเจ้าหน้าที่ กรุณาติดต่อนิติบุคคล

กลับไปหน้าจดมิเตอร์

รหัสข้อผิดพลาด: FORBIDDEN_ROLE

  1. 1One 403 page, four routes, one sentence. Staff hitting /announcements, /reports/*, /settings or /users lands here — never a silent redirect, so every row of the D-36 permission matrix is testable by URL. The sidebar is absent because a Staff session never renders Admin navigation, and the JSON equivalent is 403 { "error": { "code": "FORBIDDEN_ROLE" } }. Critically, no amount appears anywhere in a Staff response body: money is absent from the payload, not merely hidden by CSS.

Settings — ตั้งค่า (/settings)

Role: Admin only (D-36 — Staff never see a rate) Routes: GET/POST /settings · manual job POST /settings/run-daily-job (Admin session, CSRF-protected, POST-Redirect-GET) — it calls the same job body with job_name='daily_manual' (D-32) Entry points: sidebar ⚙️ ตั้งค่า · Bill Detail links here from the rate line of a bill Primary action: บันทึกอัตราใหม่ — the only control on the site that changes what a future bill costs

Four independent forms on one page, each saving on its own so a typo in the phone number can never roll back a rate change. The rate form is the dangerous one and is treated as such: it inserts, never updates, and the page shows the resulting history immediately underneath so the Admin can see that yesterday's bills still carry yesterday's price.

ใบเสร็จพิมพ์เฉพาะชื่อและที่อยู่ของนิติบุคคล ไม่มีชื่อซอฟต์แวร์

โหมดทดสอบ LINEเปิดอยู่
ผู้เช่าจะไม่ได้รับข้อความจริงปิดโหมดนี้เฉพาะตอนสาธิตหรือใช้งานจริงเท่านั้น · ระบบยังบันทึกผู้รับที่ตั้งใจส่งไว้ครบทุกราย
  1. 1Three rates, one form, insert-only (D-17). Saving writes new rates rows; no existing row is ever UPDATEd, which is what makes the history below complete and a reprinted receipt truthful. The hint under each field shows the rate in force today, so the Admin can see what is being changed from. Only rates that actually differ produce a row — resaving 18.00 as 18.00 is a no-op, not a duplicate.
  2. 2มีผลตั้งแต่วันที่ — the field that makes this safe. Defaults to today, accepts a future date, and is the key of UNIQUE(utility_type, effective_from), so a same-day tie is impossible at the database level. Resolution is keyed to the first day of the bill's period month, never to the moment the button was pressed — which is the one line that stops a single September carrying two electricity rates because three rooms were read late.
  3. 3The snapshot promise, stated where the risk is. Every bill item carries its own unit_price, so approved bills, the monthly report and every reprint are immune to this form. This notice is permanent, not a flash message.
  4. 4History with a future row. The 8.50 electricity rate effective 1 ต.ค. 2026 is real, saved, and not yet in force — มีผลในอนาคต distinguishes it from ใช้อยู่. The October billing run will pick it up automatically because its effective_from is ≤ 1 ต.ค. 2026. Rows are never editable or deletable from this table; correcting a mistake means inserting another row.
  5. 5วันครบกำหนดชำระ, 1–28 (D-20). A day of month, not a date, enforced by CHECK (due_day BETWEEN 1 AND 28) so February never needs a clamp in practice (the clamp is written anyway). The hint spells out the approval-time rule, because that is where an office manager's mental model breaks: a bill approved on 4 ก.ย. is due 7 ก.ย., not 5 ก.ย. There is no per-bill due-date override anywhere in the product.
  6. 6The reminder schedule is displayed, not configured (A-D3, D-33). Four fixed facts: −2 days, then +1, +8, +15, all at 09:00 Asia/Bangkok, capped at four messages per bill and never sent to a Paid bill. Showing them here answers the office's question without creating a setting that no test asserts and no code reads.
  7. 7Quota visibility, not quota machinery (D-34). The count is COUNT(*) over notifications where status='sent' this month — failed and skipped_no_line rows never inflate it. The bar turns --ui-warning above 450. Nothing is throttled or blocked: exhausting the free allowance silently stops delivery, and that is limitation 12 of D-05. Beside it, รันงานแจ้งเตือนตอนนี้ posts to POST /settings/run-daily-job — an Admin-session, CSRF-protected form POST with POST-Redirect-GET, not the scheduler's machine route POST /internal/jobs/daily, which carries no session and no CSRF token and is authenticated by X-Job-Token alone (D-32, D-37). It calls the identical job body with job_name='daily_manual', skipping the job_runs guard so a 14:00 demo still works; safety comes from the notification dedupe key.
  8. 8Apartment profile — one property, one building (D-04). These five fields are the only place the organisation names itself. The name also renders in the sidebar brand and as the LINE signature — นิติบุคคล ศุภาลัย เรสซิเดนซ์; the software name never appears in either place.
  9. 9Live letterhead preview replaces the receipt-footer field (A-D4). It renders the exact block the A5 receipt prints. Clearing the tax ID removes that half of the third line immediately, which is the fastest way to show that the field is optional (D-27) without a free-text footer that could smuggle a VAT line onto a non-tax receipt.
  10. 10LINE test mode, default ON in every environment (D-34). While it is on, every outbound message is redirected to the single whitelisted user ID while the intended recipient is still written to the notifications row — so delivery reports stay meaningful during development. The warn badge in the card head is deliberately loud: the demo is the only time this is turned off.
  11. 11The field is drawn empty because that is what a fresh clone runs (D-34). seed.sql ships settings.line_test_user_id = NULL on purpose, and the pair “test mode ON + no whitelisted recipient” is not a broken configuration — it is the deliberate swallow branch: the sender makes no LINE API call at all, writes the notifications row as status='sent' with last_error='test mode, no whitelisted recipient', and spends nothing from the monthly 500. The hint ยังไม่ได้ตั้งค่า · ข้อความจะไม่ถูกส่งออกเลย is the only place on any screen that says so before the fact; after the fact it is last_error on Bill Detail's delivery panel. Leaving the field empty is therefore never a validation error — the field has no required marker and the save is accepted — because refusing it would break the state the seeded demo depends on. Filling it in is a deliberate act, taken from the demo-day runbook before an examiner is in the room; the frame below shows the result.

Alternate frame — after whitelisting a test recipient

The same card once an Admin has pasted the team's own userId into it. Test mode is still ON, so nothing reaches a tenant; every outbound message is now redirected to that one account while notifications.recipient_user_id keeps recording the intended tenant, so the delivery badges and the quota counter stay honest (D-34). This is the state the demo is rehearsed in, and the only frame in this document that carries a filled test id.

2มีบิลฉบับร่าง 67 ใบในรอบนี้ ยังใช้อัตราเดิมอยู่บิลฉบับร่างถูกสร้างก่อนบันทึกอัตราใหม่ · กดปุ่มด้านล่างเพื่อคำนวณใหม่ตามอัตราปัจจุบัน — บิลที่อนุมัติแล้วจะไม่ถูกแตะต้อง
อัตราค่าบริการ
4บันทึกข้อมูลอพาร์ตเมนต์แล้ว
  1. 1Success is an inline notice, not a dialog. D-39 lists exactly six confirmation dialogs and a rate change is not one of them — it is reversible by inserting another row, and it touches no phone and no cash. Adding a seventh dialog here would be a designer overruling the decision log.
  2. 2D-17's Draft warning, verbatim. Shown only when Draft bills exist for a period the new rate reaches back into. คำนวณบิลฉบับร่างใหม่ recalculates Draft bills only; the sentence says so, because the office's real fear is that approved bills and issued receipts will move. This is the one control on the page that changes money already computed, and it is a secondary button, never the primary.
  3. 3UNIQUE(utility_type, effective_from) surfaced as a field error. The database refuses the duplicate; the route turns the constraint violation into a 422 with a Thai fields message, and the form re-renders with every typed value preserved (D-40) — never a redirect, never a lost form.
  4. 4Toast for the low-stakes save. Apartment-profile saves get a toast and nothing else. It carries no undo: D-39 forbids the toast-with-undo pattern product-wide, because an undo window would force outbound LINE messages into a queue.

Users — ผู้ใช้งานระบบ (/users)

Role: Admin only (D-46) Route: GET/POST /users Entry points: sidebar 👥 ผู้ใช้งานระบบ, under the ตั้งค่าระบบ group Primary action: เพิ่มผู้ใช้งาน · secondary ตั้งรหัสผ่านใหม่, the only password-recovery path in v1

ผู้ใช้งานระบบ
เพิ่มผู้ใช้งาน
ผู้ใช้งานทั้งหมด 3 บัญชีผู้ใช้งานถูกปิดใช้งานได้ แต่ลบไม่ได้
ชื่อผู้ใช้ชื่อที่แสดงบทบาทสถานะสร้างเมื่อ
adminนิติบุคคล ผู้ดูแลระบบ ใช้งานอยู่1 ต.ค. 2025 4ตั้งรหัสผ่านใหม่ — บัญชีของคุณ
staffเจ้าหน้าที่ เจ้าหน้าที่ ใช้งานอยู่1 ต.ค. 2025 5 ปิดใช้งานบัญชีนี้?
staff2สมศักดิ์ ช่างประจำ เจ้าหน้าที่ 6ปิดใช้งาน3 ก.ย. 2026 เปิดใช้งาน ตั้งรหัสผ่านใหม่
7ทำไมลบบัญชีไม่ได้ทุกใบเสร็จอ้างถึงผู้รับเงิน ทุกรายการจดมิเตอร์อ้างถึงผู้บันทึก และทุกอัตราอ้างถึงผู้ตั้งค่า · ปิดใช้งานแล้วเข้าระบบไม่ได้ แต่ประวัติทั้งหมดยังอ้างชื่อเดิมได้ถูกต้อง
  1. 1ชื่อผู้ใช้ — unique, immutable, no email anywhere (D-46). UNIQUE(username) is the database's word on it; the form checks on blur and shows the duplicate error inline. There is no email column, so there is no self-service reset and no invitation flow to build.
  2. 2ชื่อที่แสดง is not decoration — it prints on receipts. This is the string that appears as ผู้รับเงิน on every A5 receipt this user issues (D-27), which is why the hint says so on the create form rather than in a manual nobody reads.
  3. 3Two roles, no third, no per-user permissions (D-36). The select carries data-role="admin|staff" so tests assert on the English identifier while the operator reads Thai. The hint states the Staff scope in full, because choosing the wrong role here is how a meter reader ends up seeing the building's revenue — and Staff restrictions are enforced in the API payload, not by hiding fields.
  4. 4ตั้งรหัสผ่านใหม่ is the only recovery path in v1 (D-37, D-46). It reopens the card above in edit mode with only the password field enabled. An Admin can reset their own password here; if the last Admin account is lost, recovery is a database operation — the engineering spec's Lost admin password runbook procedure — and not a screen.
  5. 5Deactivation uses an inline two-step, not a seventh dialog. D-39's six confirmation dialogs are the complete set and all six guard something irreversible — cash or a LINE message. Deactivating a user is reversible by the button in the row below it, so it gets the cheapest honest confirmation: the row's action area swaps to a question and a red ยืนยัน. Cancel restores the row untouched.
  6. 6Inactive, never deleted. users.status = 'Inactive' blocks login on the next attempt and kills the session on the next request; every historical attribution survives. เปิดใช้งาน restores access with the same password.
  7. 7The reason is on the page. Three foreign keys — payments.received_by_user_id, meter_readings.recorded_by, rates.created_by — make deletion a data-integrity problem, not a policy preference. Saying so once here prevents the “can we just delete the old account” request during the demo.

Cross-checks a reviewer can run on this section
  • September reconciles: 12,240.00 + 38,976.00 + 70,350.00 = 121,566.00 billed; 98,420.00 collected + 23,146.00 outstanding = 121,566.00; 55 paid + 12 overdue = 67 occupied rooms.
  • The common fee is the seed's headline: 67 × 30 ตร.ม. × 35 = 70,350.00 every month, PRD §16's only reproducible figure (D-44).
  • Room 205 is PRD §28, unchanged: 10 × 18 = 180.00, 70 × 8 = 560.00, 30 × 35 = 1,050.00, total 1,790.00, due 5 ก.ย. 2026, paid 3 ก.ย. 2026, REC-202609-000852 — the same row in the monthly, top-usage and room-history reports.
  • Announcement counts add up: every history row's ส่งแล้ว + ไม่มี LINE + ส่งไม่สำเร็จ equals its ห้อง column, because notifications holds one row per recipient (D-30).
  • The seed's 54: 67 occupied rooms, 54 linked tenants — the number D-35's preview sentence quotes for a building-wide send, and the number the arrears report's phone column exists to cover.
  • Nothing out of scope appears: no rent, no lease, no payment gateway, no slip upload, no QR, no PDF export, no report builder, no editable reminder schedule, no third role, no recall button.

LINE Official Account Messages (Tenant)

The tenant is the only user of this product who never sees a screen we build. Everything a tenant receives arrives in one LINE chat with the apartment's Official Account, and everything a tenant does happens at the office counter with cash. This section specifies that chat: the six outbound message types, the account-linking conversation that makes them deliverable at all, the exact Thai text of each one, and what the office sees when a message does not arrive.

Binding constraints this section obeys
  • Plain text only (D-31). Seven templates, no Flex Messages, no images, no rich menu, no buttons inside a message. Every mockup below uses .bubble.oa; .flex-card and .rich-menu exist in tokens.css for a post-v1 version and a v1 mockup that uses them is a defect.
  • Braced placeholders are the template; the rendered text is an example. Each screen shows the D-31 template verbatim and then one worked example rendered with the PRD §28 values — room 205, 10 หน่วย × 18, 70 หน่วย × 8, 30 ตร.ม. × 35, ยอดรวม 1,790 บาท, ครบกำหนด 5 กันยายน 2026, ชำระ 3 กันยายน 2026, REC-202609-000852.
  • Money and dates (D-03). LINE messages write the number followed by บาท with the trailing .00 dropped (1,790 บาท), full Thai month names (5 กันยายน 2026), Gregorian years, 24-hour times with the suffix น., timezone Asia/Bangkok.
  • No product branding (D-01). The tenant sees the apartment's Official Account. The OA display name is apartment_name from Settings — ศุภาลัย เรสซิเดนซ์ in the seed — and the only signature that ever appears is — นิติบุคคล {apartment_name} on an announcement.
  • The OA is not a chatbot (D-29). Exactly three inbound behaviours exist: a string shaped like a link code, anything else, and the follow event. There is no menu, no query, no balance lookup, no tenant-facing web login (D-05, limitation 7).

How to read these screens

A LINE message has no interface states of its own — it is one immutable bubble in a chat we do not own. The five states every screen in this document set must specify therefore map onto delivery state, which is what the office can actually see and act on. The mapping below is the same on every screen, so each screen's table only fills in what is specific to that message.

Document stateMeans, for a LINE messagenotifications.statusWhat the office sees
EmptyThe tenant has no LINE binding, so no message is generated at all — the common case on day one, and 13 of the 67 seeded tenants permanently (D-44)skipped_no_lineยังไม่ได้ส่ง — ไม่มี LINE on Monthly Billing and Bill Detail
LoadingApproved and queued, not yet pushed — a bulk approval drains 20 messages per call, so a 64-room send is visibly in progress for up to three minutes (D-19)queuedProgress bar on the approve result screen: ส่ง LINE แล้ว 40/64
ErrorThe push call failed — network, 5xx, or an expired channel token. Automatic retries stop at attempt_count = 3; the Admin's manual resend is never capped (D-30, D-33)failedส่งไม่สำเร็จ, with last_error and http_status on hover
SuccessLINE accepted the push. One row, one recipient, counted once against the monthly allowance (D-34)sentส่งแล้ว; Settings shows ส่งข้อความเดือนนี้: 128/500
Permission deniedThere is no tenant-facing authorisation to deny. The two equivalents: LINE answers 400 invalid userId, which unbinds the tenant automatically (never for a seeded Useed… id) and drops the room back to Empty; or the tenant has blocked the Official Account, which LINE reports as success and we cannot detect at all (D-05, limitation 9)failed → unbound, or a false sentThe binding disappears from the Tenants row; the blocked case is invisible by design and is a listed limitation
Two things the mockups deliberately do not show

There is no read receipt we control and no delivery receipt LINE gives us. The อ่านแล้ว marks in the frames below are LINE client chrome drawn for realism only — the product never reads them, never stores them, and no screen, report or test case may claim a message was read. Likewise the input bar is the tenant's own keyboard: the product cannot pre-fill it, disable it, or attach a button to it.

LM-1 · Bill issued — BILL · POST /api/bills/approve

Role: ผู้เช่า (tenant) — LINE only, no login, no app Template: BILL (D-31 #1) Entry: Admin presses อนุมัติและส่งบิล on Bill Detail /bills/:id or in bulk on Monthly Billing /bills; the queued row is pushed by POST /api/notifications/drain Primary action: none in-message — the tenant's action is offline: bring cash to the office within office hours Delivery: Push API, one call per recipient (D-34)

7🔄 แก้ไขบิล (ยกเลิกยอดเดิม) 🔔 ค่าใช้จ่ายประจำเดือน กันยายน 2026 ห้อง 205 💧 ค่าน้ำ 12 หน่วย × 18 บาท = 216 บาท ⚡ ค่าไฟ 70 หน่วย × 8 บาท = 560 บาท 🏢 ค่าส่วนกลาง 30 ตร.ม. × 35 บาท = 1,050 บาท 💰 ยอดรวม 1,826 บาท 📅 กรุณาชำระภายในวันที่ 5 กันยายน 2026 ชำระเงินสดที่สำนักงานนิติบุคคล เวลา จันทร์-ศุกร์ 09:00-17:00 น.16:38 น.
☺ข้อความ🎤＋
Left: the BILL message, the demo's first tenant-visible event. Right, the alternate state: a reading corrected after approval reverts the bill to Draft and re-approving sends BILL_CORRECTED, whose body is the whole BILL template behind a one-line prefix — the tenant is never asked to work out which of two numbers is current (D-22, D-31 #2). The corrected figures (12 หน่วย, 1,826 บาท) are deliberately not the §28 values, because a correction that changes nothing would illustrate nothing.
  1. 1Chat header. The OA display name is settings.apartment_name (D-01) — never "Juristic Platform", never a product logo. The avatar and the Official badge are LINE's own chrome, configured once in the LINE Official Account Manager. Nothing here is rendered by our code.
  2. 2The BILL bubble. One plain-text message, sent after the approve transaction commits, never inside it (D-30) — a LINE outage cannot roll back an approval. The three item lines repeat the arithmetic rather than only the amounts, because the tenant's first question is always "why is it this much"; the numbers come from the frozen bill_items snapshot, so the message can never disagree with the printed bill or the receipt (D-17, D-22).
  3. 3Timestamp. Set by LINE at delivery, not by us. On a bulk approval of 64 rooms the drain loop spreads sends over up to three minutes, so timestamps across rooms differ by minutes — this is expected and is not a defect (D-19, D-45).
  4. 4The tenant replies. Nothing in the product asks for a reply, but tenants reply anyway. The inbound message is stored nowhere except LINE; it never reaches a staff screen, and no member of staff is notified.
  5. 5Fixed auto-reply. Any inbound text that is not shaped like a link code gets one fixed reply pointing at the office, sent with the Reply API on the event's reply token — it writes no notifications row and does not consume the 500-message allowance (D-29, ADR ENG-4). Full wording and the shape rule are specified in LM-6.
  6. 6Input bar. The tenant's own keyboard. v1 attaches no rich menu, no quick reply and no postback button (D-31), so this bar is the only inbound channel and it is used for exactly one thing: the six-character link code.
  7. 7Corrected bill. Sent same-day under its own dedupe key BILL_CORRECTED, so it is never suppressed by the original BILL row (D-30). Where the correction followed a voided payment the template appends ยอดที่ชำระไว้เดิมได้คืนเป็นเงินสดแล้ว กรุณาชำระตามยอดใหม่ — the cash goes back across the counter first, because the system holds no refund or credit balance (D-22, D-25).

LM-2 · Reminder before due date — PRE_DUE · POST /internal/jobs/daily

Role: ผู้เช่า (tenant) Template: PRE_DUE (D-31 #3) Entry: the external scheduler calls POST /internal/jobs/daily at 09:00 Asia/Bangkok with X-Job-Token; the job selects Unpaid bills where due_date − today = 2 Primary action: none in-message — pay cash at the office before the due date Frequency: exactly once per bill, ever (D-33)

4⏰ แจ้งเตือนก่อนครบกำหนด ห้อง 205 มียอดค้างชำระ 1,790 บาท กรุณาชำระภายในวันที่ 5 กันยายน 2026 (อีก 2 วัน) ชำระเงินสดที่สำนักงานนิติบุคคล09:00 น.
5⏰ แจ้งเตือนก่อนครบกำหนด ห้อง 402 มียอดค้างชำระ 1,682 บาท กรุณาชำระภายในวันที่ 5 กันยายน 2026 (อีก 2 วัน) ชำระเงินสดที่สำนักงานนิติบุคคล09:00 น.
☺ข้อความ🎤＋
Left: the reminder in the demo timeline — the bill lands on 1 ก.ย., the reminder fires at 09:00 on 3 ก.ย., the tenant pays that afternoon. Right, the alternate state: one LINE account bound to two Active tenancies (a parent renting a second room) receives one message per room, never a merged statement, because uniqueness is on the tenant and every template names its room (D-29).
  1. 1Two days before, not two days of nagging. The condition is literally due_date − today = 2 evaluated once a day, so PRE_DUE is sent at most once in a bill's entire life. A bill approved late enough that the window has already passed simply never gets one — the due date is protected instead, by MAX(due day, approval + 3 days) (D-20, D-33).
  2. 2Amount and date repeated, breakdown dropped. A reminder answers one question — how much, by when — and the tenant already has the itemised message from LM-1 in the same thread. Nothing is added that the bill did not already say; there is no late fee, interest or penalty anywhere in this product (D-05, limitation 3).
  3. 309:00 Asia/Bangkok, always. One fixed hour, set by the external scheduler, never in-process (D-32). No evening send, no weekend variation, no per-tenant preference. If the scheduler pings twice, UNIQUE(job_name, run_date) makes the second call a no-op, and the notification dedupe key makes it harmless even from the Admin's manual รันงานแจ้งเตือนตอนนี้ button, which deliberately skips the job guard so a 14:00 demo still works.
  4. 4Room named in line 2, not only in the header. The header is the apartment, not the room. For a tenant with one room this is redundant; for the account on the right it is the only thing that distinguishes two otherwise identical messages, which is why D-31 forbids hard-coding it away.
  5. 5Two rooms, two messages, two rows. The 1,682 บาท is a second room's own bill, not a total. Both rows count separately against the 500-message monthly allowance, and each has its own ส่งแล้ว badge on its own bill. No screen ever aggregates a LINE account's rooms into one figure.

LM-3 · Overdue notice — OVERDUE · POST /internal/jobs/daily

Role: ผู้เช่า (tenant) Template: OVERDUE (D-31 #4) Entry: the same 09:00 job, on the days where days_overdue IN (1, 8, 15) Primary action: none in-message — contact the office Frequency: at most three per bill, ever; nothing after day 15 (D-33)
Whose bill this is

Room 205's demo bill is paid on 3 กันยายน 2026, so it never becomes overdue. The frames below are the counterfactual branch of that same bill — the tenant does not come to the counter — which keeps the §28 figures (ห้อง 205, 1,790 บาท, ครบกำหนด 5 กันยายน 2026) intact while showing the arrears path. PRD §15's short form ⚠️ คุณมียอดค้างชำระ 1,790 บาท is the summary of this message; D-31 fixes the sent text, which adds the room, the due date and the number of days late, because a tenant renting two rooms cannot act on the short form.

4⚠️ เลยกำหนดชำระแล้ว ห้อง 205 ยอดค้างชำระ 1,790 บาท ครบกำหนดวันที่ 5 กันยายน 2026 (เลยมาแล้ว 15 วัน) กรุณาติดต่อสำนักงานนิติบุคคล09:00 น.
521 กันยายน 2026
☺ข้อความ🎤＋
Left: day 1. Right, the whole life of an unpaid bill — days 1, 8 and 15, then silence. The empty day separator under the last message is the point of the frame: the tenant is not messaged again, and follow-up moves to the counter and the phone.
  1. 1Overdue is a display rule, not a stored status. The bill row still holds Unpaid; เกินกำหนด is derived from due_date < CURDATE() by one shared helper used by both the SQL and the JavaScript (D-21). The message does not change a status, add a charge or lock anything — it is a reminder and nothing else.
  2. 2Wording escalates, amount does not. ยอดค้างชำระ is the same 1,790 บาท on day 1, day 8 and day 15. There is no late fee, no interest and no penalty in this product, so a message implying a growing balance would be a lie the receipt could not honour (D-05, limitation 3).
  3. 3days_overdue IN (1, 8, 15). Not "every day", not "weekly until paid". Three touches is what an office needs for arrears follow-up; beyond that the tenant blocks the Official Account, and a blocked OA silently destroys bill delivery too, which we cannot even detect (D-33, D-30).
  4. 4Day 15 is the last message. On day 16 and after, the bill is still เกินกำหนด on every admin screen and still appears in the arrears report and on the Dashboard, but the tenant's phone goes quiet.
  5. 5Nothing on day 16. Also nothing at any hour other than 09:00, nothing to a bill whose most recent payment was voided (D-25), and nothing once the bill is Paid — the payment confirmation in LM-4 ends the thread for that bill permanently.

613 กันยายน 2026
☺ข้อความ🎤＋
Left: the demo path — paid on the afternoon of the day the reminder went out. Right, the alternate state: a bill paid two days after it went overdue. The day-8 and day-15 reminders that would otherwise have fired on 13 and 20 กันยายน never happen, because nothing is ever sent to a Paid bill (D-33).
  1. 1Sent after the commit, never inside it. One transaction writes the payment, flips the bill to Paid, allocates the receipt number and queues this message; the push happens afterwards (D-24, D-30). A LINE timeout can therefore never lose a payment, and a tenant who is already walking out of the office with a paper receipt is never contradicted by a missing message.
  2. 2Receipt number in the message. REC-202609-000852 is the same string printed on the A5 sheet, so a tenant who loses the paper can quote the number at the counter. Numbers are unique and monotonic but not gapless, and this message is the only place a tenant ever sees one (D-27, D-05 limitation 10).
  3. 3Full amount, never a part payment. The stored amount is fixed at the bill total and is not an input on the Payment screen; a tenant who is short is asked to come back (D-24). So this message can never read "ได้รับชำระบางส่วน", and no template exists for one.
  4. 4The thread ends here for this bill. No further automatic message concerns it. The next bubble in this chat will be next month's BILL, or an announcement.
  5. 5Same template, different receipt month position. The receipt number is built from the payment month, so a bill for กันยายน paid in ตุลาคม carries a REC-202610-… number. The message is the tenant's record of when the money was actually taken (D-27, D-44).
  6. 6Silence after payment is the feature. The one case where a second PAID message is correct is a voided payment followed by a real one: the void is agreed at the counter with cash returned first, the corrected bill is re-sent as BILL_CORRECTED, and the new payment issues a new receipt number while the old number stays allocated and stamped ยกเลิก (D-25, D-27).

ErrorPush failed after a committed paymentNothingส่งไม่สำเร็จ on the bill. The money is safe — this is precisely the case the commit-then-send rule exists for. The cashier can say so, and press ส่งแจ้งเตือนอีกครั้ง

☺ข้อความ🎤＋
Left: the PRD §15 announcement as the tenant of room 205 receives it. Right, the alternate state that the office must be warned about: the LINE account bound to two tenancies receives the announcement twice, because delivery is recorded one row per recipient tenant, not per LINE account.
  1. 1Title, blank line, body, signature. Exactly the four parts of the template — the title is not repeated in the body and the body is not truncated. The Announcements form caps หัวข้อ at 60 characters and เนื้อหา at 400, which keeps the whole message far inside LINE's 5,000-character limit even with the signature (D-35).
  2. 2Sent on demand, at whatever hour the office presses the button. Announcements are outside the 09:00 reminder schedule entirely; there is no scheduling, no send-later and no draft queue (D-33, D-35).
  3. 3Signature is the apartment, not the software. — นิติบุคคล {apartment_name}, one space after นิติบุคคล, taken from Settings. It is the only message of the seven that carries a signature, because it is the only one that is a statement from a person rather than a record of a transaction (D-01, D-31 #6).
  4. 4Audience is rooms, delivery is tenants. The two options are ทุกห้องที่มีผู้เช่า and เลือกชั้น — no per-room picking, no "unpaid only" audience, no exclusions. Broadcast is never used, because it would also reach ex-tenants who still follow the Official Account (D-34, D-35).
  5. 5Known duplicate, accepted in v1. One LINE account bound to two Active tenants gets the same announcement twice. Deduplicating by recipient_user_id would make the per-announcement result under-report delivery and break the one-row-per-recipient rule the whole notifications table rests on (D-30). The office is told to expect it; it is listed under Limitations rather than engineered around, and the fix — collapsing by userId while still writing both rows — is a post-v1 change.

D-31 #6

LM-6 · Account linking conversation — LINK_OK / LINK_FAIL · POST /line/webhook

Role: ผู้เช่า (tenant), using LINE only Templates: LINK_OK / LINK_FAIL / fixed auto-reply (D-31 #7, D-29) Entry: Admin presses สร้างรหัสผูก LINE on Tenants /tenants and hands over a printed slip carrying the OA QR code and a 6-character code Primary action (tenant): add the Official Account, then send the code as a chat message Delivery: Reply API on the event's reply token — no notifications row, no consumption of the 500-message allowance (ADR ENG-4)
Why this conversation exists at all

A tenant cannot read their own LINE userId anywhere in the LINE app, so staff literally cannot type it into the Tenants form — the field is read-only and is filled only by this flow (D-29). Until it is filled, every message in LM-1 to LM-5 is skipped_no_line and the tenant is served on paper. This is therefore the highest-value screen in the tenant surface, and the only one where the tenant sends anything.

☺ข้อความ🎤＋
Left: the happy path. Centre: a mistyped code (A instead of 4) rejected, the correct code accepted, and the same code replayed a minute later rejected because it is single-use — the field K7QM4T is still sitting in the input bar, which is exactly how a tenant produces the third bubble. Right: an account already bound to ห้อง 205 binding a second room, then asking a question and meeting the fixed auto-reply.
  1. 1Adding the OA is step one and it is not ours. The tenant scans the QR code on the printed slip; LINE fires a follow event, and the webhook upserts line_followers with the userId and display name so the office has a name to recognise when the code arrives. The webhook returns 200 before doing any of this work — LINE disables an endpoint that answers slowly, and a disabled webhook silently kills the only channel by which a userId ever reaches the system (D-29, D-43).
  2. 2Greeting message. Configured once in the LINE Official Account Manager, not sent by our code, and it is what stops a tenant staring at an empty chat wondering what to do. It states the one action available and the office's hours and phone number from Settings.
  3. 3The code. Six characters from ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789 — no O/0 and no I/1, because this string is read off paper and typed on a phone. Valid 7 days, single use. The webhook trims whitespace and uppercases the inbound text before matching, so k7qm4t and K7QM4T  both work.
  4. 4LINK_OK names the room. This is the tenant's only confirmation that the office bound the right room, and the reason the reply is not a generic "สำเร็จ". Binding is keyed on the tenant (UNIQUE(tenant_id)), so one LINE account may serve several Active tenancies and every later message says which room it is about (D-29).
  5. 5What happens on the office side. The code is expired, an audit row TENANT_LINE_LINK is written, and the Tenants row starts showing the LINE display name and the linked date with a ยกเลิกการผูก action for Admin. Ending a tenancy unbinds that tenancy only (D-08, D-38).
  6. 6LINK_FAIL is one string for four causes — unknown code, expired code, already-used code, and a code whose tenancy has ended. The tenant cannot act on the difference and is pointed at the office; the distinction is recorded in the audit log, not in the reply (D-29).
  7. 7Single use means single use. Replaying an accepted code fails. This is deliberate: an accepted code that stayed valid for seven days would let anyone who saw the printed slip bind their own LINE account to that room.
  8. 8Nothing clears the input bar but the tenant. The product cannot pre-fill, clear or disable it, which is why a replayed code is a realistic event rather than a hypothetical one.
  9. 9A second code, for a second room. Issued to a different tenant record — here the second room rented by the same family. A code from an already-bound account is not an error (D-29).
  10. 10The reply names the new room. After this, this LINE account receives two bills a month, two reminders, and — as LM-5 records — every announcement twice.
  11. 11The question a chat inevitably attracts. A tenant with an unexpected water charge will ask here first. Nobody is watching this inbox.
  12. 12The fixed auto-reply. Any inbound text that does not match the code shape ^[A-Z2-9]{6}$ after trim-and-uppercase gets this one string; text that matches the shape but not a live code gets LINK_FAIL. Saying plainly that no human reads the chat is kinder than silence, and it is the only honest thing to say about a v1 that has no operator inbox (D-29).

LM-6 states

StateTriggerTenant seesOffice sees / recovery
EmptyNo code has been issued, or the tenant never added the Official AccountNo chat, or a chat with only the greetingTenants row shows no binding; the tenant stays on paper. Recovery: press สร้างรหัสผูก LINE and hand over a new slip (D-29)
LoadingCold start on the free instance — the webhook ACKs immediately but processing takes a second or twoA short pause before the replyNothing. The 10-minute /healthz ping exists precisely so this pause stays short and the endpoint is never disabled (D-43)
ErrorWrong, expired or already-used code❌ ไม่พบรหัสนี้ หรือรหัสหมดอายุแล้ว กรุณาติดต่อสำนักงานนิติบุคคลNothing is bound; the tenant returns to the office. A bad X-Line-Signature is answered 401 and nothing happens at all — no reply, no row (ADR §7.5)
SuccessLive code matched✅ ผูกบัญชีสำเร็จ naming the roomTenants row shows the LINE display name and linked date; audit row TENANT_LINE_LINK; the next bill sends instead of skipping
Permission deniedAn outsider who saw the slip sends a code that has already been used, or the tenancy has endedThe same LINK_FAIL stringNo binding is moved or overwritten. The office's own action ยกเลิกการผูก is the only way to detach an existing binding (D-29)

LM-7 · What the tenant experiences — flow strip

Every tenant-visible event in one picture: what arrives, when, and the two places where the path leaves LINE entirely. Solid arrows are the demo path; dashed arrows are the branches that produce no message.

flowchart LR
  A["เพิ่มเพื่อน OA และส่งรหัส 6 ตัวอักษร"] --> B["✅ ผูกบัญชีสำเร็จ · ห้อง 205"]
  B --> C["🔔 บิลประจำเดือน · 1,790 บาท · ครบกำหนด 5 กันยายน 2026"]

  M --> I
Thirteen nodes, five of them messages. A bill produces at most four automatic messages in its entire life (one BILL, one PRE_DUE, up to three OVERDUE minus the ones cut short by payment) plus one PAID; everything else the tenant experiences happens on paper or across the counter (D-33).
#MomentMessageTriggerTiming
1LinkingLINK_OK / LINK_FAILTenant sends the codeImmediate reply
2Bill approvedBILLAdmin: อนุมัติและส่งบิลSeconds to three minutes after the click
2bBill correctedBILL_CORRECTEDAdmin re-approves after ตีกลับเป็นฉบับร่างSame as above
3Two days before duePRE_DUEDaily job09:00 on due_date − 2, once ever
4LateOVERDUEDaily job09:00 on days 1, 8 and 15 only
5Cash receivedPAIDAdmin: ยืนยันรับเงินสดImmediately after the transaction commits
6Anything the office wants to sayANNOUNCEMENTAdmin: ส่งประกาศOn demand, multicast
7Tenant writes anything elsefixed auto-replyInbound messageImmediate reply, no row, no allowance used

LM-8 · Deferred surfaces and assumptions recorded by this screen set

No rich menu and no Flex card in v1

The brief for this section asked for a rich menu "if the decisions include one" and for the bill to be a Flex card. D-31 excludes both: seven plain-text templates, no Flex Messages, no images, no rich menu. tokens.css carries .flex-card and .rich-menu for a post-v1 version and marks them reserved; a v1 mockup using either is a defect, so neither appears above and no rich-menu mockup was produced. What it would take to add one later: a rich menu is an image plus a tappable-area map uploaded to the LINE channel, so it needs a designed 2500×1686 asset the team does not have, and every cell must lead somewhere — and there is nowhere for a tenant to go, because there is no tenant-facing web surface (D-05, limitation 7). A Flex bill would additionally duplicate every amount in a second, richer format that must stay in step with the plain-text one; one template module, one string per message, is the decision (D-31).

  1. A-1 · Overdue uses the demo bill's counterfactual branch. Room 205's bill is paid on 3 กันยายน 2026, so it can never be overdue. LM-3 shows the same bill on the branch where the tenant does not come to the counter, which keeps the §28 figures exact rather than inventing a second room with invented amounts. The overdue dates (6, 13 and 20 กันยายน 2026) follow from due_date = 5 กันยายน 2026 and days_overdue IN (1, 8, 15).
  2. A-2 · PRD §15's overdue line is a summary, not the sent text. ⚠️ คุณมียอดค้างชำระ 1,790 บาท is reproduced in the copy table as documentation. The string actually sent is D-31 #4, which adds the room, the due date and the day count — a tenant renting two rooms cannot act on the short form, and D-31 is binding on exact wording.
  3. A-3 · Timestamps and battery levels in the frames are illustrative, except 09:00 น. on PRE_DUE and OVERDUE, which is fixed by D-32 and must not be redrawn at another hour. Bill and payment times follow the demo narrative (approved 1 ก.ย. 09:12, paid 3 ก.ย. 14:30) and match the receipt mockup in the foundations document.
  4. A-4 · Two strings are set here because no decision fixed them. The LINE greeting message (configured in the LINE Official Account Manager, not in code), and the code-shape rule that separates a code attempt from ordinary chat: trim, uppercase, then ^[A-Z2-9]{6}$ — a match that is not a live code gets LINK_FAIL, anything else gets the auto-reply. The auto-reply text is no longer one of them: D-29 mandates the reply and D-31 #7 words it, so this document transcribes the engineering spec §5.5.3 (7) string verbatim rather than setting its own. If engineering words the greeting or the shape rule differently, the template module wins and this table follows it.
  5. A-5 · Duplicate announcements for a shared LINE account are accepted, not fixed. LM-5's right-hand frame is real behaviour, not a mockup error. It follows from one notifications row per recipient tenant (D-30) combined with a per-tenant binding (D-29), and collapsing it would break the delivery counts that make ส่งบิลผ่าน LINE OA verifiable. It belongs in the Limitations list of any document that carries one.
  6. A-6 · The link-code slip is drawn, and it is drawn once. This assumption previously declined to mock it up. That was wrong: D-29 makes the printed slip the only channel by which a tenant ever receives their code, so leaving it undrawn left the entry point of the whole LINE flow unspecified. It is now a full A5 print frame in Rooms & Tenants §6 — GET /tenants/:id/link-code/slip — with its QR, its 24 pt code, its expiry, its Thai copy table and its expired state. It stays owned by that chapter, because the slip is a print of the tenant-detail panel and the two must never diverge; this section still contributes only the code alphabet and the two webhook replies a tenant sees after scanning.
  7. A-7 · LINE test mode is ON in every environment (D-34), so during development and rehearsal every message in this section is redirected to one whitelisted userId while the intended recipient is still recorded on the row. The frames above show what the real tenant would see with test mode off — the state that exists only during the graded demo run.
  8. A-8 · Read receipts, day separators, the input bar, the avatar and the Official badge are LINE client chrome, drawn for realism. The product neither controls nor reads any of them, and no requirement, report or test case may depend on them. Where an earlier message is drawn dimmed it is a reading aid in this document only — it marks the context bubble so the eye lands on the new one; LINE renders every message at full strength.

E. Conflicts and open points carried forward

#PointStatus
1One illustrative period snapshot for กันยายน 2026. The chapters previously drew two incompatible Septembers, both stamped 8 ก.ย.: a Dashboard set totalling 151,370.00 with 52 Paid / 9 Unpaid / 6 Overdue, and a Design Foundations §8.1 / Reports set totalling 121,566.00 with 55 Paid / 12 Overdue.Resolved. The 121,566.00 set is now the single house snapshot: 12,240.00 น้ำ (680 หน่วย) + 38,976.00 ไฟ (4,872 หน่วย) + 70,350.00 ส่วนกลาง, collected 98,420.00, outstanding 23,146.00 over 12 Overdue bills, 55 Paid, 67 approved. The Dashboard tiles, its three twelve-month chart series, its donut and its arrears table were all re-derived from it in one pass, and the Monthly Billing frame — 64 bills, 61 approved, 43 paid — was re-dated to 7 ก.ย. 2026, the day before, so the two frames read as one story rather than two Septembers. The Dashboard arrears table no longer lists any room that the D-23 banner reports as unread, and no room that carries a ฉบับร่าง bill in this period appears in any arrears list. The Dashboard frame was then moved back one further step, to the state a freshly seeded clone actually shows: room 205's bill still ฉบับร่าง, so 66 approved bills, ส่วนกลาง 69,300.00, billed 119,776.00, collected 96,630.00, ชำระแล้ว 54 — the same 23,146.00 outstanding over the same 12 rooms, because room 205 was paid in the later state and paid rooms do not sit in arrears. The two sets differ by exactly one bill of 1,790.00, and the transition between them is what the test plan asserts as TC-BILL-065 (69,300.00 → 70,350.00). Everything else in this document draws the later moment. It is still scenery under D-44, which reproduces only 80 / 67 / 13, 70,350.00 and the PRD §28 room-205 arithmetic; no test may assert on it. What a test may assert is the shape: every bill total is 1,050.00 + 18 × น้ำ + 8 × ไฟ and is therefore an even number of baht.
2tokens.css cascade order. The @container app (max-width:767px) override of .screen .kpi-grid sat in section 2, before the base .screen .kpi-grid rule in section 5, so at equal specificity the later base rule won and the intended two-up phone KPI grid never applied.Resolved — the fix is applied in both places. The .kpi-grid line has been moved out of the section-2 @container block and now sits in a phone-container block after the base rule, so the mobile override wins. The stylesheet is no longer a scratch file: it is delivered as docs/_shared/tokens.css, and the second <style> block of this document is that file inlined byte-for-byte — the two were diffed after the reorder and are identical. No markup changed; the phone frames in Meter Reading and the staff ภาพรวม now render the two-up grid Foundations §10 specifies.
3Report sub-routes and CSV filenames. The ADR names /reports and its reports/*.ejs views but not the four tab paths, so /reports/monthly, /reports/arrears, /reports/top-usage and /reports/room-history are derived from the ADR naming rule.Assumption. If engineering names them differently, the code wins and this table follows it.
4Form and detail routes for rooms and tenants. /rooms/new, /rooms/:id/edit, /tenants/new, /tenants/:id and /tenants/:id/link-code are form modes of the three EJS pages the ADR lists, not new pages.Assumption. Recorded in the Rooms & Tenants chapter.
5The LINE OA greeting and the link-code shape rule (trim → uppercase → ^[A-Z2-9]{6}$) have no owning decision. The fixed auto-reply text used to sit in this row and no longer does.Assumption, narrowed. The greeting and the shape rule are set in the LINE chapter as assumption A-4 and flagged for the decision log. The auto-reply body is D-31 #7, transcribed verbatim from engineering spec §5.5.3 (7), so the two documents now ship one string.
6A LINE account bound to two Active tenancies receives every announcement twice — one notifications row per recipient tenant (D-29 + D-30).Design finding, surfaced not hidden. Mocked up and annotated in LM-5; it is carried as a limitation in Operating Procedures in the product decisions document.
7Flex Message cards and a LINE rich menu are specified but not used. D-31 fixes v1 to plain-text templates; tokens.css marks .flex-card and .rich-menu reserved.Deferred by decision. A v1 screen using either is a defect. The cost of adding a rich menu later is a 2500 × 1686 asset plus a tenant destination that does not exist (D-05).
