The เปลี่ยนรหัสผ่าน screen (GET/POST /password) specified for the first time — desktop and phone frames, seven annotations, a states table and an exact Thai copy table — together with the Dashboard's non-blocking first-login banner คุณยังใช้รหัสผ่านเริ่มต้นอยู่ — กรุณาเปลี่ยนรหัสผ่าน. The route was specified in engineering §3.2.1/§3.3.1 and tested by TC-AUTH-019–021, and had no screen, no navigation entry, no copy and no requirement; it is now FR-49 and sits in M0. An account block is added to both navigations (§3 site map, §4, §8.1, §8.2 and every shell frame): นิติบุคคล · admin · เปลี่ยนรหัสผ่าน · ออกจากระบบ on the Admin sidebar and a one-line footer on the staff ภาพรวม — never a fourth staff tab, so D-36's three-tab surface is unchanged and TC-AUTH-027's href set holds.
2026-09-07Draft v1.1Senior Product DesignerReview pass applied. One house snapshot for กันยายน 2026 (Appendix E, point 1); the fourth Dashboard chart รายรับรายเดือน that D-41 requires; one twelve-month history for room 205, chained from the seeded baselines 1,140 / 4,510 (engineering spec §2.7) to the PRD §28 readings and used verbatim by Rooms & Tenants, Meter Reading, Payment and Reports tab 4; one navigation block per role, rendered identically on every page (Design Foundations §4); the new §8.9 seed-identity table as the single source for every room used in more than one chapter; one Settings value each for office hours and the receipt letterhead; reprint variants restored to the full receipt markup required by D-27 and D-09; and an accessibility pass — the phone tap-target floor enforced in tokens.css, real disabled attributes with visible reasons instead of title tooltips, programmatic labels on every search field, and annotation markers in Rooms & Tenants anchored to the elements they label instead of to guessed pixel offsets.

G. Editorial corrections applied at merge

Eight chapters were written in parallel against the same three binding sources. The following differences were reconciled while assembling this document and in the review pass that followed; in each case Design Foundations and ADR-001 were treated as the authority.

#DivergenceResolution
1Room 205’s tenant was สมชาย ใจดี in Rooms & Tenants and in Monthly Billing, and สมชาย ใจดี in Design Foundations §8.7, Payment & Receipt and Announcements. An earlier pass resolved this within this document, in favour of อารยา ศรีสุวรรณ — which put it at odds with the engineering spec and the test plan, both of which address tenants.id 37 สมชาย ใจดี.Re-resolved against the seed. Room 205’s tenant is สมชาย ใจดี throughout, phone 081-234-5678, move-in 1 ต.ค. 2025, LINE display name สมชาย linked 15 ส.ค. 2026 — the engineering spec §2.7 seed row verbatim. อารยา ศรีสุวรรณ moves to room 202, where the Tenants list already carried a second tenant record. The Staff persona stays ช่างสมชาย; the ช่าง prefix, not a different surname, is what separates the two.
2Room 205’s move-in date was 1 ต.ค. 2025 in Rooms & Tenants and 1 ต.ค. 2025 in Monthly Billing and Payment & Receipt; the LINE link date was 15 ส.ค. 2026 in one chapter and 15 ส.ค. 2026 in another. The pair chosen internally (1 ต.ค. 2025 / 15 ส.ค. 2026) matched neither sibling document.Re-resolved against the seed. 1 ต.ค. 2025 and 15 ส.ค. 2026, the move_in_date and line_linked_at of tenants.id 37 in engineering spec §2.7. The linking still follows the move-in; it simply follows it by ten months, which is what a tenancy that predates the LINE rollout looks like.
3A Bill Detail reference frame in Design Foundations §8.5 addressed itself /bills/205, using a room code where the route takes a bill id. The same defect survived untouched in /rooms/205, /rooms/108 and the /tenants/<room code>0 pattern, and the replacement bill id /bills/1842 was itself invented rather than taken from the seed.Corrected everywhere, to the seed ids. /bills/118, /rooms/41, /tenants/37 and /receipts/96 — engineering spec §3.1.10, the fixture table the payload examples and the test cases share. Rooms and tenants the spec fixes no id for carry a plain surrogate (/rooms/8, /tenants/44, /tenants/59), never one derived from a room code. ADR §6.6: room_code and receipt_no are the only identifiers ever shown to a human; every :id is a surrogate key.
4Five headings written inside mockup frames — four in the Design Foundations visual-language frame and the Thai 403 title in the Reports chapter — used <h2> and <h3>, which the document shell harvests into the table of contents.Re-tagged as styled <div> elements carrying the same product type tokens. The rendered screens are unchanged; the contents list no longer contains product UI strings.
5Each chapter shipped its own <style> block.Kept verbatim and unmerged. Every rule in all seven blocks is scoped under .screen, and the only selector declared twice — .screen{position:relative} — is byte-identical in both, so no cascade conflict is possible.
6Design Foundations §8.1 and the Reports chapter reproduced one illustrative period total for กันยายน 2026, the Dashboard chapter another.Reconciled, then split into two labelled moments. The 121,566.00 set is the single house snapshot for every frame drawn after the demo approval; the Dashboard tiles, charts, donut and arrears table were re-derived from it, and the Monthly Billing frame moved to 7 ก.ย. 2026. The Dashboard frame itself now draws the seeded 119,776.00 moment one approval earlier, so that the frame an examiner holds next to a fresh clone matches it. See conflict 1 in E, which now records the resolution rather than the divergence.
7Room 205's twelve-month history was drawn four different ways — Rooms & Tenants §2, Meter Reading §2, Payment "ชำระแล้วล่าสุด" and Reports tab 4 — with different units, different totals and two different receipt numbers for สิงหาคม 2026.Reconciled to the Rooms & Tenants §2 chain, the only one that runs from the room's own baselines to the PRD §28 readings 1,250→1,260 and 5,280→5,350. Re-derived a second time from the seeded baselines 💧 1,140 / ⚡ 4,510 on 30 ก.ย. 2025 (engineering spec §2.7), replacing this document's own 1,143 / 4,559. Water 10, 12, 9, 11, 8, 10, 10, 9, 12, 9, 10, 10; electricity 70, 63, 60, 66, 71, 79, 64, 73, 87, 67, 70, 70, oldest first; ส.ค. 2026 = 10 / 70 / 1,790.00 / REC-202608-000795, matching the seed's own 2026-08 rows. Reports tab 4's twelve rows, its totals row (120 หน่วย / 2,160.00 · 840 หน่วย / 6,720.00 · 12,600.00 · 21,480.00) and its averages (10.0 / 70.0 / 1,790.00) were regenerated from it.
8Room 205's tenant was สุภาพร ใจดี in six places in the Meter Reading chapter, which correction 1 above claimed had already been reconciled.Actually applied. All six occurrences now read สมชาย ใจดี, and the chapter's assumption 9 states that room 205 is not one of its illustrative rooms.
9Rooms were given contradictory identities across chapters: room 108 was both ว่าง (Rooms) and an occupied overdue room (Dashboard, Reports); room 312 had four tenants; rooms 209 and 214 were ฉบับร่าง in one chapter and เกินกำหนด in another; and the D-23 unread banner named a vacant room and a room that already carried a bill.Fixed with a new source of truth. Design Foundations §8.9 lists every room used in more than one chapter with its floor, tenant and occupancy; the frames were rewritten to it. Room 108 is ว่าง everywhere (its occupied identities moved to 113 and 115), room 312 carries the D-44 seeded tenancy (ประเสริฐ ชูเกียรติ until 31 ส.ค. 2026, then วิภาดา นาคเกษม), and the D-23 banner names 107, 313 and 504 — three occupied rooms that appear in no bill table.
